Conclusion: 结果建立了经典体能条件与全息探针所选择的内部几何之间的直接联系，并提示 Kasner 标度可作为自下而上全息中的一种互补诊断手段。

Abstract: A central question in bottom-up holography is whether a given bulk effective theory admits a consistent holographic dual. In this work, we explore whether the near-singularity Kasner scaling of planar AdS black hole interiors can serve as a useful diagnostic based on interior-sensitive holographic probes. By examining the metric combinations that control interior-sensitive observables, including Complexity=Volume (CV), the bulk contribution to Complexity=Action (CA), Hartman-Maldacena (HM) entropy, and the thermal $a$-function, we derive algebraic bounds on the Kasner exponents $(p_t,p_s)$ for the relevant semi-classical regimes: finite-radius late-time branches of CV and HM, finite CA complexity, and a finite near-singularity thermal $a$-function. The resulting inequalities delineate the corresponding regions of Kasner space in which the terminal scaling alone is sufficient to realize these behaviors. Furthermore, we demonstrate that classical energy conditions, specifically the null and dominant energy conditions, provide simple sufficient criteria ensuring that the Kasner exponents fall within these holographically allowed regions. These results establish a direct connection between classical bulk energy conditions and the interior geometry selected by holographic probes, and suggest that Kasner scaling can provide a complementary diagnostic in bottom-up holography.

</details>


### [84] [Carrollian Conformal Dynamics and Flat-Space Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17086)
*Simon Pekar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出渐近平坦时空共形边界具有 Carroll 几何，并发展了一套保持联络自由度的度量-仿射（metric-affine）共形动力学框架，用其重新导出 Einstein 引力的渐近演化方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近平坦时空的共形边界（conformal boundary）是零性的，因此携带 Carroll 几何而非 Lorentz 几何，这从根本上改变了对称性、变分原理与演化方程之间的关系。与 Lorentz 型 Levi-Civita 联络不同，与共形结构相容的无挠 Carroll 联络含有真正独立的额外数据，需要新的形式化处理。

Method: 作者建立共形动力学的度量-仿射表述（metric-affine formulation），通过超动量（hypermomenta）追踪联络自由度；在局部 Carroll 助推、旋转、Weyl 变换与微分同胚下导出协变演化方程。随后将该框架应用于四维渐近平坦时空的 Einstein 引力，利用 Penrose 共形紧化（conformal compactification）将零无穷（null infinity）识别为共形 Carroll 流形。

Result: 相容联络中未定的横向对称部分被等同于渐近 Bondi 剪切（Bondi shear）；在固定 Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs（BMS）规范并识别 Carroll 动量与超动量同 Bondi 数据后，一般 Carroll 演化方程重现了质量与角动量方面的标准演化方程。

Conclusion: 结果给出了引力演化方程的边界内禀推导（boundary-intrinsic derivation），并为平坦空间全息（flat-space holography）中的辐射自由度提供了几何实现。本文为“Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary”（2025年12月17-19日，雅典国立卡波季斯特里安大学）会议论文集供稿。

Abstract: The conformal boundary of asymptotically flat spacetime is null and therefore carries a Carrollian rather than Lorentzian geometry. This changes the relation between symmetry, variational principles and evolution equations in an essential way: unlike a Lorentzian Levi-Civita connection, a torsion-free Carrollian connection compatible with the conformal structure contains genuine independent data. We develop a metric-affine formulation of conformal dynamics that keeps track of these connection degrees of freedom through hypermomenta. Local Carroll boosts, rotations, Weyl transformations and diffeomorphisms then imply a set of covariant evolution equations. We apply this framework to Einstein gravity in four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. Penrose's conformal compactification identifies null infinity as a conformal Carrollian manifold; the undetermined transverse symmetric part of its compatible connection is the asymptotic Bondi shear. After fixing a Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs (BMS) frame and identifying the Carrollian momenta and hypermomenta with the Bondi data, the general Carrollian evolution equations reproduce the standard evolution equations for the mass and angular-momentum aspects. The result provides a boundary-intrinsic derivation of the gravitational evolution equations and a geometric realization of radiative degrees of freedom in flat-space holography. This is a proceedings contribution to the "Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary", held at the National and Kapodistrian University of Athens on December 17-19 2025.

</details>


### [85] [Supersymmetric extension of the Hull--Lambert--Sen chiral boson on the 2-Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17161)
*Amine El Amri,Pietro Antonio Grassi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hull 提出的双度规形式为手征玻色子（chiral boson）的量子化提供了强大且可处理的理论框架，本文旨在说明该框架可自然容纳超对称推广。

Method: 在双度规形式（two-metric formulation）基础上构造 Hull-Lambert-Sen 手征玻色子模型的超对称扩展，并在二维环面（two-torus）上显式写出该理论，进一步分析其与外部源（external sources）的耦合。

Result: 成功构造了该超对称扩展理论并给出其在二维环面上的显式形式，同时完成了对外部源耦合的分析。

Conclusion: Hull 的双度规形式在手征玻色子的量子化方面具有可扩展性，可自然地推广至超对称情形。

Abstract: We present a supersymmetric extension of the Hull-Lambert-Sen chiral boson model. The two-metric formulation developed by Hull provides a powerful and tractable framework for the quantization of chiral bosons, and we demonstrate that it admits a natural supersymmetric generalization. We construct the theory explicitly on the two-torus and analyze its coupling to external sources.

</details>


### [86] [Generalized complexity and dynamical response in holographic Vaidya spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17182)
*Mojtaba Shahbazi,Monireh Emami*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在光滑 Vaidya 几何中检验“复杂度=任意量”（Complexity=Anything）框架，采用 Weyl 平方泛函作为候选。数值分析显示 4、5 维 Reissner-Nordström（RN）与 5 维 Gauss-Bonnet（GB）解中候选量同时依赖 $r$ 与 $v$，即“双重复杂度”（doubled complexity）。借助 Fefferman-Graham 展开，作者揭示静态与动态解复杂度之差首个非零项出现在第五个 FG 系数，并由边界应力张量及其导数控制，进而关联到推迟应力张量关联子与谱密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态解中复杂度只依赖 $r$，而 Vaidya 等动态几何中复杂度行为尚不明确，需检验 Complexity=Anything 框架在含时情形下的适用性。同时，全息复杂度（holographic complexity）与边界动力学响应之间的定量联系尚未建立。

Method: 以 Weyl 平方泛函（Weyl squared functional）为候选量，对 4、5 维 RN 与 5 维 GB 光滑 Vaidya 几何做数值分析。随后采用 Fefferman-Graham（FG）展开，同时展开体度规、extremal embedding、诱导度规、法向量与外曲率，比较静态与动态解，并借助线性响应与广义 Kramers-Kronig 关系处理边界应力张量响应。

Result: 数值结果显示候选量在 $r$ 与 $v$ 上均有演化（doubled complexity），不同于仅依赖 $r$ 的静态解。FG 展开表明前四个 FG 系数在两几何间抵消，首个非零贡献出现在第五个系数，由边界应力张量及其导数控制；Vaidya quench 期间导数项编码边界态的含时响应，在线性响应区由推迟应力张量关联子（retarded stress-tensor correlator）支配，并可通过广义 Kramers-Kronig 关系用谱密度表示。

Conclusion: 广义全息复杂度（generalized holographic complexity）与边界应力张量的动力学响应存在联系，在线性响应区可由相应的应力张量谱密度（spectral density）表示。

Abstract: We investigate "Complexity=Anything" for smooth Vaidya geometries, using the Weyl squared functional as the most common candidate for our study. Our numerical analysis of candidates in 4- and 5-dimensional Reissner-Nordström (RN) and 5-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet (GB) shows evolution in both $r$ and $v$ ("doubled complexity"), unlike static solutions that depend only on $r$. We then study the difference between the complexity of static and dynamical solutions in the asymptotically AdS regime using a Fefferman-Graham expansion. By expanding the bulk metric, extremal embedding, induced metric, normal vector, and extrinsic curvature simultaneously, we show that the first four FG coefficients cancel between the two geometries. In contrast, the first nonvanishing contribution occurs at the fifth coefficient and is controlled by the boundary stress tensor and its derivatives. During the Vaidya quench, the derivative contribution encodes the time-dependent response of the boundary state. In linear response, this response is governed by the retarded stress-tensor correlator and, through generalized Kramers-Kronig relations, can be represented in terms of its spectral density. We thus identify a connection between generalized holographic complexity and the dynamical stress-tensor response, which in the linear-response regime can be represented in terms of the corresponding stress-tensor spectral density.

</details>


### [87] [Cohomological Aspects of Entanglement Entropy: From Information Theory to Noncommutative Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17195)
*Radoslav C. Rashkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个上同调框架（cohomological framework），以统一信息论、算子代数（operator algebras）与非交换几何（noncommutative geometry）对纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的描述；核心是将纠缠复形嵌入 Connes 循环双复形，并把纠缠上同调刻画为 von Neumann 代数到其子代数限制映射的核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵在信息论、算子代数与非交换几何中各有表述，缺乏统一框架。该工作旨在为量子场论（quantum field theory）与全息（holography）中的纠缠提供严格基础，尤其针对不存在局域密度矩阵的 Type III von Neumann 代数情形。

Method: 以熵的信息论刻画为 1-上闭链（1-cocycle）为起点，借助 Hochschild 与循环上同调（cyclic cohomology）推广至量子情形；通过条件期望（conditional expectation）将纠缠复形嵌入 Connes 循环双复形（Connes cyclic bicomplex），并引入 Tomita-Takesaki 模理论（modular theory）提供动力学结构。

Result: 纠缠上同调（entanglement cohomology）被识别为 von Neumann 代数到其子代数的限制映射（restriction map）的核；Connes-Radon-Nikodym 上闭链（Connes-Radon-Nikodym cocycle）成为编码相对纠缠的基本对象。

Conclusion: 该框架自然容纳 Type III von Neumann 代数，从而为量子场论与全息中的纠缠提供严格基础。

Abstract: We develop a cohomological framework for entanglement entropy that unifies perspectives from information theory, operator algebras, and noncommutative geometry. Starting from the information-theoretic characterization of entropy as a 1-cocycle, we show how this structure generalizes to the quantum setting through Hochschild and cyclic cohomology. A central result is the embedding of an entanglement complex into the Connes cyclic bicomplex via a conditional expectation, identifying entanglement cohomology as the kernel of the restriction map from a von Neumann algebra to its subalgebra. The Tomita-Takesaki modular theory provides the dynamical structure, with the Connes-Radon-Nikodym cocycle serving as the fundamental object encoding relative entanglement. This framework naturally accommodates Type III von Neumann algebras, where no local density matrix exists, offering a rigorous foundation for entanglement in quantum field theory and holography.

</details>


### [88] [Low-energy brane decoupling in AdS flux vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17342)
*Fien Apers,Noelia Sánchez González*

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究膜所源出的AdS通量真空（AdS flux vacua）是否与渐近体解耦，是判断此类真空是否可视为低维有效理论的关键问题，而此前缺乏对该解耦行为的定量刻画。

Method: 采用标量波吸收概率（scalar-wave absorption probability）作为工具，在单模截断（one-modulus truncation）下分析膜对渐近体标量波的吸收，并将结果与AdS真空维度及标量势（scalar potential）的渐近陡度（asymptotic steepness）关联；随后应用于多个尺度分离（scale-separated）AdS真空实例。

Result: 对任意陡度且维度大于2的情形，低能极限下吸收概率趋于零，即膜与体解耦。在DGKT中得 P_abs ∼ ω^{27/7}，有效横向维度 d_eff = 20/7；沿膜诱导模轨迹的标量势等同于将一个假想的(4+13/7)维引力理论在带有通量的13/7维球面上紧致化所得结果。分析亦推广至尺度分离的AdS_3实例及最简单模KKLT模型。

Conclusion: 标量波吸收概率的计算表明，维度大于2时膜普遍从渐近体解耦，支持将尺度分离AdS通量真空视作有效低维理论；DGKT等具体模型给出的分数维度指数揭示了其与高维球面紧致化的对应关系。

Abstract: We study the decoupling of branes sourcing AdS flux vacua from the asymptotic bulk using scalar-wave absorption probabilities. In a one-modulus truncation, the result depends only on the dimension of the AdS vacuum and on the asymptotic steepness of the scalar potential. We find that for any steepness and for any dimension greater than two the absorption probability vanishes in the low-energy limit, showing that the branes decouple from the bulk. We illustrate the general analysis in several scale-separated AdS vacua. For DGKT we find $P_{\rm abs}\simω^{27/7}$ and an effective transverse dimension $d_{\rm eff}=20/7$. Interestingly, the scalar potential along the brane-induced moduli trajectory agrees with that obtained by compactifying a hypothetical $(4+13/7)$-dimensional gravity theory on a $13/7$-dimensional sphere threaded by flux. We also apply the analysis to scale-separated $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ examples and to the simplest one-modulus KKLT model.

</details>


### [89] [Topological Modular Forms Constrain Threshold States in Extremal $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17359)
*Yutaka Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，$\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ 极端谱（extremal spectra）存在一个不由三个偶自旋结构配分函数的亏格一模性（genus-one modular covariance）所隐含的拓扑约束；在假定 Ramond Witten 指标满足拓扑模形式（topological modular forms）整除性的前提下，证明当中心荷 $c=12n$ 且 $n$ 为无穷多个奇数时，极端全纯 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论（superconformal field theory）需要在 BTZ 阈值处有奇数个 Neveu–Schwarz 初级场，从而排除无阈值初级场的严格假设（strict ansatz）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者意图揭示一个额外的拓扑约束，该约束无法由已知的三个偶自旋结构配分函数的亏格一模性推出，从而加深对极端 $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ 谱结构的理解。

Method: 方法上，作者假定 Ramond Witten 指标具有预期的拓扑模形式整除性，并据此分析中心荷 $c=12n$ 下极端全纯 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论的谱约束。

Result: 结果显示，对于无穷多个奇数 $n$，在中心荷 $c=12n$ 时，极端全纯 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论要求 BTZ 阈值处存在奇数个 Neveu–Schwarz 初级场。

Conclusion: 结论是，对于这一无穷族，无阈值初级场的严格假设（strict ansatz with no threshold primaries）被排除。

Abstract: We identify a topological constraint on extremal $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ spectra that is not implied by genus-one modular covariance of the three even spin structure partition functions. Assuming the expected topological modular forms divisibility of the Ramond Witten index, we show that for infinitely many odd $n$ at central charge $c=12n$, an extremal holomorphic $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal field theory requires an odd number of Neveu--Schwarz primaries at the BTZ threshold. Thus the strict ansatz with no threshold primaries is excluded for this infinite family.

</details>


### [90] [Channel-Resolved High-Overtone Quasinormal Modes Decode Kasner Scaling inside Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17426)
*Zi-Qing Xiao,Jun Nian,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 高泛音准正规模（high-overtone quasinormal-mode）谱可编码黑洞内部近奇异区（near-singularity）的 Kasner 标度行为；谱中不同结构分别独立决定时间与空间 Kasner 阶数，从而重建度规指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在不施加 Kasner 约束、不借助全息（holography）的前提下，从高泛音谱中直接提取黑洞内部近奇异区的几何信息。

Method: 利用高泛音准正规模谱中不同结构分别确定时间与空间 Kasner 阶数（Kasner grades），重建对应度规指数；在五维渐近平坦 Schwarzschild 与平面 Schwarzschild–AdS$_5$ 的固定 Kasner 点上演示，并推广至连续变化的 Gibbons–Maeda 黑洞族。

Result: 重建所得指数事后（a posteriori）满足 Kasner 关系，构成非平凡的几何自洽性检验；高泛音谱可分解为全局谱数据与局部分数幂代数修正（fractional-power algebraic corrections），其分数幂由近奇异 Kasner 几何固定。

Conclusion: 这些分数修正为视界后（behind the horizon）局部几何提供直接的谱学探针。

Abstract: The high-overtone quasinormal-mode spectrum encodes the near-singularity Kasner scaling of black-hole interiors. We show that distinct structures in this spectrum independently determine the temporal and spatial Kasner grades, allowing the corresponding metric exponents to be reconstructed without imposing Kasner constraints or invoking holography. We demonstrate this independent reconstruction at the fixed Kasner point of five-dimensional asymptotically flat Schwarzschild and planar Schwarzschild--AdS$_5$, and then extend it to a continuously varying Gibbons--Maeda black-hole family. The reconstructed exponents satisfy the Kasner relation a posteriori, providing a nontrivial geometric consistency check. More generally, the high-overtone spectrum separates into global spectral data and local algebraic corrections whose fractional powers are fixed by the near-singularity Kasner geometry. These fractional corrections therefore provide a direct spectral probe of the local geometry behind the horizon.

</details>


### [91] [On the relaxation dynamics of non-equilibrium quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17447)
*Matthias Carosi,Björn Garbrecht,Silvia Pla,Nils Wagner,Edward Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文以 Zubarev 非平衡统计算符方法为框架，在单一位非守恒荷的最小设定下，系统梳理了近局域平衡下近似守恒荷的弛豫规律，并给出等价简化推导与扩散-弛豫方程，最终以电弱 B+L washout 和微扰标量模型验证其与线性化 Boltzmann 方程的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是刻画相互作用量子系统在近局域平衡（local equilibrium）下近似守恒荷（approximately conserved charge）的弛豫行为，并厘清导致局域弛豫律成立的物理假设。

Method: 采用 Zubarev 非平衡统计算符（non-equilibrium statistical operator）方法进行教学式综述，限定于单一非守恒荷的最小设定；同时给出基于中间时间尺度演化的更简单局域平衡构造作为替代推导，并将荷密度推广到空间变化情形。

Result: 在弱荷破坏（weak charge violation）、微观关联短时标与荷弛豫长时标分离、以及由此产生的微观记忆丧失等条件下，主导弛豫率由荷破坏算符的平衡关联函数（equilibrium correlation function）决定；该方法还建立了弛豫率与同一荷的平衡扩散之间的联系，空间变化情形导致扩散-弛豫方程（diffusion-relaxation equation）；在电弱 B+L washout 与微扰标量模型中结果与线性化 Boltzmann 方程（linearized Boltzmann equation）一致。

Conclusion: Zubarev 方法与简化局域平衡构造给出相同弛豫规律，明确了两种途径共有的物理要素，并在平衡关联函数描述与动理学（kinetic）描述之间建立了直接联系。

Abstract: We investigate the relaxation of an approximately conserved charge in interacting quantum systems close to local equilibrium. To this end, we provide a pedagogical review of Zubarev's non-equilibrium statistical operator approach in the minimal setting of a single non-conserved charge and apply it to the problem at hand. We explicitly highlight the physical assumptions that lead to a local relaxation law: weak charge violation, a separation between the short timescale of microscopic correlations and the much longer timescale of charge relaxation, and the resulting loss of microscopic memory. Under these conditions, the leading relaxation rate is determined by an equilibrium correlation function of the charge-violating operator. We show that the same result follows from a simpler local-equilibrium construction based on the system's evolution over an intermediate timescale, providing a direct alternative for practical calculations and making the common physical ingredients of the two approaches explicit. Beyond the decay law itself, we relate the relaxation rate to the equilibrium diffusion of the same charge. We then allow the charge density to vary in space, which leads to a diffusion-relaxation equation. Finally, we illustrate the formalism through electroweak $\mathrm{B+L}$ washout and a perturbative scalar model, where agreement with the linearized Boltzmann equation establishes a direct connection between equilibrium-correlator and kinetic descriptions.

</details>


### [92] [Exact Black Branes in General Dimensions in Higher-Curvature Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17528)
*Tianhao Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在无势（potential-free）膨胀子耦合的 Lovelock–Horndeski 理论中构造了两族中性平面黑膜（neutral planar black branes），并指出在固定标量零模（scalar zero mode）下，Wald 熵保持常数且为正，其机制源于视界处指数型标量因子对冲了面积增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质紧化（heterotic compactification）将高维曲率与标量动力学联系起来，为该相互作用结构的引入提供动机。研究旨在刻画此类耦合理论中黑膜解的性质及其对熵与标度设定的影响。

Method: 在无势膨胀子耦合的 Lovelock–Horndeski 框架中求解黑膜解，并借助精确系数分析（exact coefficient analysis）确定四维分支及其通过闭合耦合关系向任意高维的延拓。

Result: 在固定标量零模时，视界半径与温度变化而完整静态 Wald 熵保持常数且为正，视界处指数标量因子精确补偿面积增长。线性族（linear family）共形平坦且在固定耦合下不固定 AdS 标度；分数族（fractional family）具有 Weyl 曲率并固定该标度。共同动力学关系排除了二者由度规–膨胀子母理论的实单标量平坦环面约化（flat-torus reduction）实现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了两族可延拓至任意高维的黑膜解，并揭示熵恒定现象的几何解释，同时通过动力学关系限定了其与平坦环面约化模型的关系。

Abstract: We obtain two families of neutral planar black branes in a potential-free dilaton-coupled Lovelock--Horndeski theory. Heterotic compactification relates higher-dimensional curvature to scalar dynamics and motivates the interaction structure. At fixed scalar zero mode, the horizon radius and temperature vary while the complete stationary Wald entropy remains constant and positive. The exponential scalar factor at the horizon exactly compensates the area growth, giving this behavior a direct geometric explanation. An exact coefficient analysis determines the two four-dimensional branches and their continuation to every higher dimension through closed coupling relations. The linear family is conformally flat and leaves the AdS scale free at fixed couplings; the fractional family is Weyl curved and fixes that scale. The common kinetic relation also excludes their realization through a real one-scalar flat-torus reduction of a metric--dilaton parent.

</details>

### [93] [Nonlocal Magic across the Many-Body Localization Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16935)
*Shan-Zhong Li,Zhi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文以最小相对非局域魔法（min-relative nonlocal magic, NLM）为探针，刻画随机场 XXZ 链中从遍历性（ergodicity）到多体局域化（many-body localization, MBL）的过渡，揭示纠缠熵无法显现的 Schmidt 谱结构重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）虽为遍历性破缺的常用诊断量，但可能掩盖纠缠的内部组织方式。本文引入非局域魔法（nonlocal magic）——即两子系统独立局域幺正变换下可达的最小非稳定子性（nonstabilizerness）——以补充刻画 MBL 转变。

Method: 在随机场 XXZ 链中计算最小相对非局域魔法（min-relative NLM），通过 Schmidt 谱与二元平坦稳定子谱（dyadic-flat stabilizer spectra）的距离来度量纠缠的组织方式；并在深 MBL 区提出两能级 cut-hybridization 模型，另结合乘积态淬火（product-state quench）动力学进行研究。

Result: 由弱至中等无序，NLM 自 O(1) 的 Haar 类值演化为尺寸增强的穹顶形，而纠缠仍保持体积律（volume-law），显示熵不可见的谱重组。深 MBL 区中，两能级 cut-hybridization 模型刻画了近二元 Schmidt 谱，并解释 NLM 的均值与中位数分别近似按 W^{-1} 与 W^{-2} 衰减。淬火后，NLM 在遍历区先过冲后弛豫，在强无序区则缓慢且近似对数增长。

Conclusion: NLM 能分辨纠缠熵所无法显现的 Schmidt 谱结构，可作为遍历性破缺（ergodicity breaking）的互补探针。

Abstract: Nonlocal magic quantifies the minimum nonstabilizerness attainable under independent local unitary transformations on the two subsystems. Here, we use min-relative nonlocal magic (NLM) to characterize the crossover from ergodicity to many-body localization (MBL) in the random-field XXZ chain. Unlike entanglement entropy, NLM probes how entanglement is organized through the distance of the Schmidt spectrum from dyadic-flat stabilizer spectra. From weak to intermediate disorder, NLM evolves from an $O(1)$ Haar-like value into a size-enhanced dome, while entanglement remains volume-law, revealing a spectral reorganization not visible in the entropy. Deep in the MBL regime, a two-level cut-hybridization model captures the nearly binary Schmidt spectrum and explains why the mean and median NLM decay approximately as $W^{-1}$ and $W^{-2}$, respectively. Following a product-state quench, NLM overshoots and relaxes in the ergodic regime, whereas at strong disorder it grows slowly and approximately logarithmically. These results show that NLM resolves Schmidt-spectrum structure not visible in the entanglement entropy and provides a complementary probe of ergodicity breaking.

</details>


### [94] [Latent kinetic Ising models of neural spike trains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17213)
*Davide Ghio,David Saad*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: SpiKIsing 是一种潜变量模型，将离散时间的非对称动力学 Ising 模型（kinetic Ising model）与连续时间、依赖历史的点过程（point process）结合，从神经元脉冲序列中联合推断网络耦合与发放参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从神经元脉冲序列推断有向有效相互作用（directed effective interactions）是统计物理（statistical physics）与计算神经科学的核心逆问题。动力学 Ising 模型虽可处理该任务，但通常需将脉冲序列分箱为二值活动变量，从而丢弃箱内时间信息，并混淆集体网络动力学与单神经元历史效应。

Method: 提出 SpiKIsing：以离散时间非对称动力学 Ising 模型描述集体网络活动，以连续时间、依赖历史的点过程在潜状态条件下生成脉冲，显式建模不应期（refractoriness）与脉冲后恢复。推导变分平均场期望最大化（variational mean-field expectation-maximization）方案，将点过程似然作为有效观测场引入，实现潜活动、网络耦合与发射参数的联合推断；并扩展至带结构化先验的最大后验推断（maximum-a-posteriori inference），含稀疏性先验及偏好 Dale 一致外向交互的层次扩展。

Result: 在匹配的合成数据（synthetic data）上验证了参数恢复。在由循环电导型漏泄积分放电（recurrent conductance-based leaky integrate-and-fire）网络生成的脉冲序列上测试，SpiKIsing 能恢复稀疏连接结构，并正确分类全部兴奋性与抑制性神经元，尽管数据生成动力学与模型之间存在显著失配。

Conclusion: SpiKIsing 通过分离集体网络动力学与单神经元历史效应两个描述层次，为从脉冲序列推断有向有效相互作用提供了可处理框架，并在与生成机制存在明显失配的条件下仍保持较好的结构与细胞类型恢复能力。

Abstract: Inferring directed effective interactions from neuronal spike trains is a central inverse problem in statistical physics and computational neuroscience. Kinetic Ising models provide a tractable framework for this task, but their application to neural data typically requires binning spike trains into binary activity variables, discarding within-bin timing and conflating collective network dynamics with single-neuron history effects.
  We introduce SpiKIsing, a latent-variable model that separates these two levels of description. A discrete-time asymmetric kinetic Ising model describes collective network activity, while continuous-time, history-dependent point processes generate the observed spikes conditional on the latent states, accounting explicitly for refractoriness and post-spike recovery.
  We derive a variational mean-field expectation-maximization scheme in which the point-process likelihood enters as an effective observation field, enabling joint inference of latent activity, network couplings, and emission parameters. The framework extends naturally to maximum-a-posteriori inference with structured priors, including sparsity and a hierarchical extension favouring Dale-consistent outgoing interactions.
  We validate parameter recovery on matched synthetic data and test the method on spike trains generated by a recurrent conductance-based leaky integrate-and-fire network. In this setting, SpiKIsing recovers sparse connection structure and correctly classifies all excitatory and inhibitory neurons despite the substantial mismatch between the data-generating dynamics and the SpiKIsing model.

</details>


### [95] [Bias-Induced Crossover in Absolute Capacity of Dense Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17477)
*Yuto Sakurai,Takeaki Shimokawa,Kazunori Iwata,Kazushi Mimura*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究分析有偏（biased）中心化二值模式在 Krotov-Hopfield 单点判据下的稠密联想记忆（dense associative memory）绝对容量，发现固定偏置 q<1/2 时容量随多项式阶数 n 呈现与无偏情形的不同渐近形式，并给出偏置诱导的交叠（crossover）预测及基于活动依赖控制势的容量恢复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于稠密联想记忆绝对容量的分析主要针对无偏（unbiased）模式，而有偏模式在实际中普遍存在，其容量性质尚未被充分刻画。该研究旨在考察偏置对中心化二值模式容量的影响，并厘清偏置与多项式相互作用阶数之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用 Krotov-Hopfield 单点判据 P_error=1/N（N 为神经元数）定义容量，模式分量以概率 q 取 1-q、否则取 -q（0<q≤1/2）。对 n 阶多项式相互作用进行信噪比（signal-to-noise）分析以推导绝对容量；对结果做渐近匹配（asymptotic matching）以定位交叠区域；并将计算机模拟（computer simulations）与有限尺寸条件高斯（conditioned-Gaussian）预测进行比较。

Result: 在 q=1/2 时绝对容量为 N^{n-1}/ln N 阶；固定 q<1/2 时，偶数 n≥4 容量为 O(N^{n/2})，奇数 n≥5 为 O(N^{(n+1)/2})；而 n=3 时无论无偏还是固定偏置容量均为 O(N^2/ln N)。n≥4 时不同渐近形式意味着 q 接近 1/2 处大 N 极限非一致，渐近匹配预测交叠发生在 1-2q=O(ln N/N^{⌊n/2⌋-1}) 区域，其起源是偏置相关的串扰均值（bias-dependent crosstalk mean）降低了携带高频值 -q 位点的稳定性。

Conclusion: 偏置显著改变高阶多项式相互作用下的稠密联想记忆容量及其大 N 标度行为。在条件高斯近似内，引入可抵消条件串扰均值的活动依赖控制势（activity-dependent control potential）可对固定 0<q<1/2 恢复 N^{n-1}/ln N 容量。

Abstract: The absolute capacity of dense associative memory has mainly been analyzed for unbiased patterns. Here we examine the effect of bias in centered binary patterns under the Krotov-Hopfield single-site criterion $P_{\mathrm{error}}=1/N$, where $P_{\mathrm{error}}$ is the probability that a single-site flip lowers the energy of a stored pattern and $N$ is the number of neurons. Each pattern component takes $1-q$ with probability $q$ and $-q$ otherwise, where $0<q\le1/2$. For polynomial interactions of order $n$, a signal-to-noise analysis gives an absolute capacity of order $N^{n-1}/\ln N$ at $q=1/2$. For fixed $q<1/2$, however, the capacity is $O(N^{n/2})$ for even $n\ge4$ and $O(N^{(n+1)/2})$ for odd $n\ge5$. For $n=3$, both the unbiased and fixed-bias capacities remain $O(N^2/\ln N)$. For $n\ge4$, these different asymptotic forms imply a nonuniform large-$N$ limit near $q=1/2$. Asymptotic matching predicts a bias-induced crossover in the region $1-2q=O(\ln N/N^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor-1})$. The crossover originates from a bias-dependent crosstalk mean that reduces the stability of sites carrying the more frequent value $-q$. Computer simulations are compared with the finite-size conditioned-Gaussian predictions. An activity-dependent control potential that cancels the conditional crosstalk mean restores the $N^{n-1}/\ln N$ capacity for fixed $0<q<1/2$ within the conditioned-Gaussian approximation.

</details>


### [96] [Coupling spherical p-spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17522)
*Riccardo Cipolloni,Leticia F. Cugliandolo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究构建了耦合球面 p-spin 模型（spherical p-spin models）的平均场框架，推导多子系统双时关联、响应与球面约束方程组，并用边缘一步复本对称破缺（marginal 1RSB）与两步破缺分析，刻画耦合玻璃体系的共同老化标度与有效温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面 p-spin 模型是研究玻璃动力学的平均场工具，而将多个此类系统耦合可受控地考察其联合演化、交叉关联及是否共享共同老化尺度。作者希望理解耦合强度与淬火无序（quenched disorder）关联如何影响子系统间关联与有效温度。

Method: 针对具有位点无关的系统间耦合（site-independent inter-system couplings）与一般相关淬火无序的球面 p-spin 系统，推导闭合的多子系统双时方程以描述关联、响应和球面约束；采用边缘 1RSB 构造给出静态基准，并聚焦两个铁磁耦合的 p=3 系统，将动力学数据与边缘 1RSB 预测比较；弱耦合情形下另做边缘两步复本破缺（two-step breaking）分析。

Result: 独立淬火无序使系统间等时关联低于无无序值，相关无序则增强该关联。耦合足够强时，系统内部与系统之间的有限时间涨落-耗散估计（fluctuation-dissipation estimates）与共同有效温度（effective temperature）相容，即使某一或两个子系统单独时本会以顺磁体（paramagnet）平衡化；该共同值高于各组成系统单独的有效温度，并随无序关联和耦合强度增大而升高。边缘 1RSB 值 T/m 能捕捉这些趋势，但略低于有限时间动力学估计。弱耦合下动力学结果反而表明每个系统出现两个老化区制（aging regimes），伴随各自的部分平衡化。

Conclusion: 边缘两步复本破缺的预测与数值测量较为吻合，支持对弱耦合下双老化区制的描述。然而数值精度不足以判定所观测的有限时间交叉（finite-time crossover）最终是否会成为尖锐转变（sharp transition）。

Abstract: Spherical p-spin models provide a mean-field framework for glassy dynamics. Coupling such systems opens a controlled way to probe how they evolve jointly, develop cross correlations, and possibly share common aging scales. We study spherical p-spin systems with site-independent inter-system couplings and generally correlated quenched disorders. We derive closed multi-subsystem two-time equations for correlations, responses, and spherical constraints. A marginal one-step replica-symmetry-breaking construction provides static benchmarks for overlap plateaux, aging crossover scales, and effective temperatures. We then specialize to two ferromagnetically coupled p=3 systems and compare dynamical data with marginal 1RSB predictions. Independent quenched disorders reduce the equal-time correlation between the systems below its disorder-free value, while correlated disorders enhance it. For sufficiently strong coupling, finite-time fluctuation-dissipation estimates within and between the systems are compatible with a common effective temperature even when one or both subsystems would equilibrate as a paramagnet in isolation. This common value lies above the effective temperatures that the constituent systems would have separately and increases with disorder correlation and coupling strength. The marginal 1RSB value T/m captures these trends but is slightly below the finite-time dynamical estimate. For weak coupling, the dynamical results instead suggest that two aging regimes develop for each system, with partial equilibration within each. We perform a marginal replica analysis with two-step breaking, whose predictions compare favorably with the numerical measurements. Our numerical accuracy is insufficient to determine whether the observed finite-time crossover will eventually become a sharp transition.

</details>

### [97] [Investigating past high-energy fluxes with paleo-detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17157)
*Claudio Galelli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍古探测器（paleo-detectors）方法，利用天然矿物记录宇宙线（CR）诱导的核反冲损伤径迹，以重建数百万年尺度上的宇宙线通量历史。作者在前期蒸发岩研究基础上，进一步提出法国奥弗涅（Auvergne）橄榄岩捕虏体（olivine xenoliths）方案，并借助 INFN 资助的 PRImuS 实验验证高吞吐光学显微镜与等离子体刻蚀技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线通量的长期演化及近邻超新星等瞬变高能事件缺乏直接的历史记录手段。天然矿物中累积的损伤径迹可构成地质档案，为极长时间尺度（very-long-range）的时域天体物理（time-domain astrophysics）提供观测途径。

Method: 以天然矿物作为粒子探测器，读取宇宙线诱导核反冲（nuclear recoils）产生的持久损伤径迹。研究扩展至多样化的陆地记录，重点分析法国奥弗涅的橄榄岩捕虏体，利用其喷发年代序列（eruption chronosequences）区分过去 50 kyr 的宇宙线通量情景；PRImuS 实验则验证高吞吐光学显微镜（high-throughput optical microscopy）与等离子体刻蚀（plasma etching）分析矿物靶标的效果，并检验理论径迹长度谱（track-length spectra）与实验效应估计。

Result: 前期对墨西拿盐度危机（Messinian Salinity Crisis）蒸发岩（evaporites）的研究表明，具备特定地质历史的矿物可探测原生宇宙线通量变化；新工作提出奥弗涅橄榄岩捕虏体可在过去 50 kyr 内区分不同宇宙线通量情景。PRImuS 实验旨在验证相关分析技术的有效性并细化实验效应估计。

Conclusion: 古探测器有望成为极长时间尺度时域天体物理的有力工具。通过验证理论与实验分析流程，该研究为重建宇宙线通量历史提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Paleo-detectors provide a unique avenue to reconstruct the multi-million-year history of cosmic-ray (CR) flux, preserving signatures of transient high-energy events such as nearby supernovae. This technique aims to use natural minerals as particle detectors, looking at the persistent damage tracks created by CR-induced nuclear recoils, accumulated over the minerals' geological lifespan, offering a geological archive of past particle fluxes. Building on our study of Messinian Salinity Crisis evaporites, which demonstrated that minerals with specific geological histories may enable the detection of primary CR flux variations, we have now expanded to diverse terrestrial records. This contribution presents our recent publication proposing olivine xenoliths from Auvergne, France, where eruption chronosequences could allow for the differentiation of CR flux scenarios over the last 50 kyr. This phenomenological work is supported by the INFN-funded PRImuS experiment, which aims to prove the efficacy of high-throughput optical microscopy and plasma etching to analyze these mineral targets. By validating theoretical track-length spectra and refining the estimates of experimental effects, PRImuS's goal is to establish paleo-detectors as a powerful tool for very-long-range time-domain astrophysics.

</details>


### [98] [On the dynamical accessibility of twin stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16126)
*Mahdi Naseri,Vasileios Paschalidis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 强一阶强子-夸克相变（hadron-to-quark first-order phase transition）可产生稳定混合星及孪生星（twin stars），但其动力学形成仍是开放问题。本文结合引力束缚能分析与广义相对论流体动力学模拟（general relativistic hydrodynamical simulations）考察多条转变通道，发现唯有冷却时标接近或短于恒星动力学时标（dynamical timescale）时孪生星才可形成；现实冷却机制时标更长，故在守恒总静止质量的通道中中子星可能动力学占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孪生星作为与中子星同质量的稳定混合强子-夸克星，其平衡态存在性已有讨论，但能否在动力学过程中实际形成尚属未解问题。本文旨在判定引力束缚能上的能量优势是否足以决定动力学终态。

Method: 在固定重子静止质量（baryonic rest mass）下比较竞争平衡构型的引力束缚能（gravitational binding energy），并对若干可能的转变通道开展广义相对论流体动力学模拟，重点考察压缩与激波（shocks）产生的热压（thermal pressure）对系统演化归宿的影响。

Result: 孪生星虽比同静止质量的中子星更受引力束缚，但该能量偏好本身不决定动力学结果。演化中的压缩与激波产生热压，可阻止系统沉降到冷孪生星分支；模拟显示足够快速的冷却能移除热支撑并促成孪生星形成，而冷却缓慢或无冷却则一般遗留中子星。进入孪生星分支需冷却时标与恒星动力学时标相当或更短。

Conclusion: 由于现实冷却机制作用时标远长于动力学时标，在守恒总静止质量的通道中，即便存在同一静止质量下束缚更紧的孪生星构型，中子星仍可能在动力学上被优先选择。该结论至少在夸克退禁闭密度（quark deconfinement density）在温度升至10–20 MeV时无显著变化的物态方程（equations of state）范围内成立，属于原理性论证。

Abstract: A sufficiently strong hadron-to-quark first-order phase transition can give rise to a third family of stable compact stars that are commonly referred to as hybrid hadron-quark stars. Stable hybrid stars that have the same gravitational mass as neutron stars are referred to as twin stars. Although equilibrium twin stars may exist, whether they can be dynamically formed remains an open question. We investigate this problem by examining the gravitational binding energy of competing equilibrium configurations at fixed baryonic rest mass and by performing general relativistic hydrodynamical simulations of several possible transition channels. While twin stars are more gravitationally bound than neutron stars with the same rest mass, this energetic preference alone does not determine the dynamical outcome. Compression and shocks during the evolution generate thermal pressure that can prevent the system from settling on the cold twin star branch. Our simulations show that sufficiently rapid cooling can remove this thermal support and enable twin star formation, whereas slower or no cooling generally favors a neutron star remnant. Accessing the twin star branch through the formation channels considered here requires cooling on a timescale comparable to or shorter than the stellar dynamical timescale. Since realistic cooling mechanisms operate on much longer timescales, our results suggest that in channels that conserve the total rest-mass neutron stars may be dynamically favored even when a more gravitationally bound twin star configuration exists with the same rest mass. Our results demonstrate a point of principle, at least for equations of state where the quark deconfinement density does not change appreciably for temperatures up to $10-20\,\textrm{MeV}.$

</details>


### [99] [Formation of Merging Black Hole Binaries Inside Massive AGN Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16290)
*Sizheng Ma,Douglas N. C. Lin,David A. Velasco-Romero*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于三维流体力学模拟（three-dimensional hydrodynamic simulations），研究活动星系核（AGN）盘内恒星捕获恒星级黑洞（stellar-mass black hole）的过程，证实该通道可高效组装双黑洞系统并供给引力波（gravitational-wave）源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核盘中的恒星可长至数百太阳质量，同一盘内预期存在恒星级黑洞群体，恒星可能捕获途经黑洞，将其内部转化为致密双黑洞系统乃至引力波事件的生成场所，故需检验该通道的运行难易程度。

Method: 采用三维流体力学模拟研究黑洞—恒星相遇（black hole--star encounters）；作为原理验证（proof of principle），取恒星与黑洞质量比为 34:1，追踪捕获过程、动力学摩擦（dynamical friction）导致的下沉及后续双星形成。

Result: 捕获未破坏恒星结构：恒星被加热 10%–30%，质量损失仅 ≲1%；被捕获黑洞迅速失去轨道角动量并在 ≲10^4 s 的恒星动力学时间内沉至恒星中心；若恒星中心原已存在黑洞，二者形成束缚双星，其引力波并合时标低于 10^4 yr；依相遇几何与速度不同，轨道可为近圆或保留显著偏心率（eccentricity）。

Conclusion: 计算确认活动星系核盘中的黑洞—恒星相遇确实是组装恒星级双黑洞并为引力波探测器供给源的可行通道。

Abstract: Stars embedded in the disks of active galactic nuclei (AGN) can grow to hundreds of solar masses, and the same disks are expected to host a population of stellar-mass black holes. A star may therefore capture passing black holes, turning its interior into a potential factory for forming compact binary black hole systems and, ultimately, for the gravitational-wave events seen by our detectors. We test how readily this channel operates using three-dimensional hydrodynamic simulations of black hole--star encounters. As a proof of principle, we adopt a star-to-black-hole mass ratio of $34\!:\!1$, and find that the capture does not disrupt the structure of the star: it heats the star by $10$--$30\%$, and the star loses only $\lesssim1\%$ of its mass throughout. The captured black hole loses its orbital angular momentum rapidly to dynamical friction, sinking to the stellar center within a stellar dynamical time $\lesssim10^{4}\,$s. If the star already harbors a black hole at its center, the two form a bound binary whose gravitational-wave coalescence time falls below $10^{4}\,$yr. Depending on the geometry and speed of the encounter, the resulting orbit may be nearly circular or retain substantial eccentricity. Our calculations confirm that black hole--star encounters in AGN disks are indeed a viable channel for assembling stellar-mass black hole binaries and supplying sources for gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>

### [100] [Quantum Critical Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16147)
*Subham Dutta Chowdhury,Leo Radzihovsky,Luca V. Delacrétaz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在横向声子速度为零的“软”量子固体（floppy quantum solids）中，立方弹性非线性（cubic elastic nonlinearity）在 d<4 时变为相关，驱动系统趋向 d=3 的强耦合 Lifshitz 量子临界点（Lifshitz quantum critical point）。该相变通常为一级相变，但其临界数据仍可在 Ginzburg 区内获得；该非线性在 d=2 精确消失，主导相互作用转为边缘的四次项，产生弱对数奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注横向声子速度（transverse phonon velocity）趋零的量子固体的命运，研究此类“软”固体中立方非线性所导致的临界行为，并探讨其在量子材料中的可能实现。

Method: 采用 ε = 4 − d 展开（ε-expansion）分析 d=3 处的强耦合 Lifshitz 量子临界点，考察立方弹性非线性的相关性，并单独处理该非线性在 d=2 精确消失后由四次相互作用主导的情形。

Result: 立方非线性在 d<4 时相关并驱动系统趋向强耦合定点；相关相变通常为一级，使系统无法完全达到强耦合定点，但该量子临界点的临界数据可在相变附近的 Ginzburg 区（Ginzburg region）内获取。在 d=2，立方非线性精确消失，主导的四次相互作用为边缘的（marginal），导致弱对数奇异性（weak logarithmic singularities）。

Conclusion: 论文提出并刻画了“晶体 Lifshitz 临界性（crystal Lifshitz criticality）”，并讨论其在量子材料中的可能实现方式。

Abstract: We study the fate of quantum solids with a vanishing velocity of transverse phonons. Such ``floppy'' solids exhibit a cubic elastic nonlinearity that is relevant in spatial dimensions $d<4$ and drives the system to a strongly-coupled Lifshitz quantum critical point in $d=3$, that we analyze in an $ε= 4-d$ expansion. The associated phase transition is generically first-order, preempting the system's full access to the strongly-coupled fixed point. Nevertheless, the critical data of this quantum critical point is accessible within a Ginzburg region in the vicinity of the transition. This cubic nonlinearity vanishes exactly in $d=2$, where the leading interactions are instead quartic and are marginal, leading to weak logarithmic singularities. We discuss possible realizations of this ``crystal Lifshitz criticality'' in quantum materials.

</details>


### [101] [Analytical solutions to the non-equilibrium Green's functions in large-$q$ SYK models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16438)
*Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作给出了在非对易的 $q/2$-体 Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）哈密顿量之间做淬火（quench）时，所有时间分块（time block）的解析解，填补了此前非平衡分块仅有数值结果的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK 模型在 $1/q$ 领头阶精确可解，其中淬火后瞬时热化的时间分块已有闭式解，但其余本质上非平衡（out of equilibrium）的分块此前只能通过数值方法获得，缺乏解析理解。

Method: 针对两个非对易（non-commuting）的 $q/2$-体 SYK 哈密顿量之间的淬火，推导得到每个时间分块的格林函数（Green's function）闭式解析表达式，并借助柯西-施瓦茨不等式（Cauchy-Schwarz inequality）进行论证。

Result: 获得了所有时间分块的格林函数闭式解；提取出淬火前、后能量间的简单关系 $\varepsilon_1 \propto \varepsilon_0$，由此得到精确的温度更新规则（temperature update rule）。

Conclusion: 通过柯西-施瓦茨不等式证明闭系统中加热不可避免，从而表明第二定律（second law）以几何方式涌现；该解被用于姊妹论文中讨论有限 $q$ 下“瞬时热化（instantaneous thermalization）”的含义。

Abstract: It is known that the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model is exactly solvable at leading order in $1/q$; specifically, the instantaneously thermal time block after the quench is known in closed form. Thus far the remaining (inherently out of equilibrium) blocks have only been numerically accessible. Here we provide their analytical solutions for a quench between non-commuting $q/2$-body SYK Hamiltonians. We obtain the Green's functions in closed form for every time block. We extract a simple relation between the pre- and post-quench energies $ε_1 \propto ε_0$ leading to an exact temperature update rule. Via a Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we show that heating is inevitable for the closed system; hence, the second law emerges geometrically. The solution is used in a companion paper to address what ``instantaneous thermalization'' means at finite $q$.

</details>


### [102] [Hartree-Fock Density-Matrix Renormalization Group for Very Long Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16587)
*Steven R. White*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将 Hartree--Fock 表述为对单个 Slater 行列式的密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）扫描，通过分别压缩占据态与长程相互作用，把每步扫描化为小型局域 Fock 问题，从而在普通台式机上实现超大规模含长程库仑相互作用的 HF 计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 Hartree--Fock 计算中，长程库仑相互作用的 1/r 尾部与交换项使计算成本随体系尺寸迅速增长，难以在普通硬件上处理。作者希望借助 DMRG 对态与环境的压缩能力，使含长程 Hartree 与交换作用的大体系 HF 计算可在桌面级计算机上完成。

Method: 在假定密度--密度（density--density）电子--电子相互作用的前提下，将 Hartree--Fock 表述为单个 Slater 行列式（Slater determinant）的 DMRG 扫描；分别压缩占据态（occupied state）与长程相互作用（long-range interaction），使每个扫描步（sweep step）归约为一个小型局域 Fock 问题，并在整个过程中保留交换（exchange）作用。

Result: 均匀反铁磁氢链（uniform antiferromagnetic hydrogen chains）为该方案提供了近乎理想的测试体系，并能保留库仑相互作用及其 1/r 尾部。作者在 64-GB Mac mini 上，用不到三分之一内存、约两小时完成含 100,000 个电子、1.8 百万空间基函数的链的初始化与收敛。每次扫描的工作量近似与链长成正比，且收敛所需扫描数从 1000 个原子到 100,000 个原子变化很小。

Conclusion: DMRG 对态与环境的压缩使含长程 Hartree 与交换作用的大规模 HF 计算在普通台式计算机上变得可行，表明该表述在超大体系的第一性原理计算中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We formulate Hartree--Fock as a density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) sweep of a single Slater determinant, assuming a density--density electron--electron interaction. We compress the occupied state and long-range interaction separately, reducing each sweep step to a small local Fock problem. Exchange is retained throughout. Uniform antiferromagnetic hydrogen chains provide an almost ideal setting for this approach, while retaining the Coulomb interaction and its $1/r$ tail. We initialize and converge a chain of 100,000 electrons in 1.8 million spatial basis functions in about two hours on a 64-GB Mac mini, using less than a third of its memory. The work per sweep is nearly proportional to chain length, and the number of sweeps needed for convergence changes little from 1000 to 100,000 atoms. DMRG's compression of states and environments thus makes large HF calculations with long-range Hartree and exchange practical on an ordinary desktop computer.

</details>


### [103] [La substitution studies on the heavy-fermion superconductor CeRh$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17080)
*Sushma Lakshmi Ravi Sankar,Manuel Brando,Jochen Wosnitza,Seunghyun Khim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 Ce$_{1-x}$La$_x$Rh$_2$As$_2$（$x \le 0.1$）单晶中，La 替代同时引入等效负压（约 −0.3 GPa）与无序；$T^{*}_{\mathrm{max}}$ 与 $T_0$、$T_\mathrm{c}$ 均被快速抑制，其中 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 的急剧下降更符合替代无序引起的强配对破坏，暗示相 I 的巡游起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CeRh$_2$As$_2$ 因存在不寻常的双相超导电性而受到广泛关注：超导（SC）相在 Ce-4$f$ 矩有序态（相 I）中出现于 $T_\mathrm{c}$ = 0.35 K，而相 I 在 $T_0$ = 0.55 K 以下形成，但其微观本质尚未被完全确立。本文旨在通过 La 替代调控晶格与无序，以厘清这些低温相的微观性质。

Method: 对 Ce$_{1-x}$La$_x$Rh$_2$As$_2$（$x$ 至 0.1）进行单晶研究，考察 La 替代对低温相的影响；晶格参数随 $x$ 单调增大，对应 $x$ = 0.1 时约 −0.3 GPa 的等效负压。通过电阻率测量追踪 $T^{*}_{\mathrm{max}}$ 以及 $T_0$ 与 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 的演化。

Result: Ce$^{3+}$ 局域价态与非费米液体（non-Fermi-liquid）行为得以保持，但 $T^{*}_{\mathrm{max}} \approx$ 45 K 随 $x$ 增大向低温移动，表明晶格膨胀抑制了 Kondo 能量尺度；同时 $T_0$ 与 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 在 $x$ > 0.05 时迅速被抑制至 0.1 K 以下。$T_\mathrm{c}$ 在中等负压下如此快速下降出乎预期，反而与替代诱导的无序效应（在非常规超导体中导致强配对破坏）相一致。

Conclusion: 相 I 在压力与无序下均表现出脆弱性，这一特征可能暗示其具有巡游（itinerant）起源。

Abstract: CeRh$_2$As$_2$ has been receiving considerable attention due to its unusual two-phase superconductivity. The superconducting (SC) phase appears at $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ = 0.35 K in an ordered state (phase I) of the Ce-4$f$ moments, which develops below $T_{\mathrm{0}}$ = 0.55 K. The microscopic nature of phase I has not been fully established yet. We report a single-crystal study of the effect of La substitution on these low-temperature phases in Ce$_{1-\textit{x}}$La$_\textit{x}$Rh$_2$As$_2$ up to $x$ = 0.1. The lattice parameters increase monotonically with $x$, corresponding to an effective negative pressure of approximately -0.3 GPa for $x$ = 0.1. While the Ce$^{3+}$ local valence state and the non-Fermi-liquid behavior are preserved, the resistivity coherence maximum $T^{*}_{\mathrm{max}}$ $\approx$ 45 K in the pristine sample shifts to lower temperatures with increasing $x$, indicating a suppression of the Kondo energy scale upon lattice expansion. On the other hand, both $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ and $T_{\mathrm{0}}$ are rapidly suppressed to below 0.1 K for $x$ $>$ 0.05. Notably, such a rapid decrease of $T_{\mathrm{c}}$ under moderate negative pressure is unexpected, but is rather consistent with substitution-induced disorder effect which leads to strong pair breaking in unconventional superconductors. The observed fragility of phase I under both pressure and disorder may imply its itinerant origin.

</details>

### [104] [Gauged AKSZ sigma models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16043)
*Noriaki Ikeda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类新的 sigma 模型（sigma model），称为带规范（gauged）AKSZ sigma 模型，作为 AKSZ sigma 模型的推广，并借助 QP-流形（QP-manifold）予以构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AKSZ sigma 模型已构成拓扑场论与高阶几何的重要框架，但将其与规范对称性（gauging）相结合的方式尚待系统刻画，故本文旨在给出该类推广模型及其一致性条件。

Method: 在 QP-流形（QP-manifold）框架下构造带规范 AKSZ sigma 模型；以目标分级流形（target graded manifold）上的几何条件刻画 AKSZ 结构与规范化的相容性；并以高维高阶 Courant sigma 模型（higher Courant sigma model）的规范化为显式示例进行分析。

Result: 给出了带规范 AKSZ sigma 模型的构造及相容性几何条件；在高阶 Courant sigma 模型中显式实现规范化；当引入通量（flux）与边界（boundary）时，动量映射（momentum map）与哈密顿 G-空间（Hamiltonian G-space）的推广作为模型一致性条件出现。

Conclusion: 带规范 AKSZ sigma 模型构成 AKSZ 框架的一类自然推广，其一致性要求对应于动量映射与哈密顿 G-空间等几何结构的推广，为高阶规范场论的研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We propose a new class of sigma models that extend AKSZ sigma models, which we refer to as gauged AKSZ sigma models. These models are constructed using QP-manifolds. The compatibility between the AKSZ structure and gauging is characterized by geometric conditions on the target graded manifold. As an example, we explicitly analyze the gauging of higher Courant sigma models in higher dimensions. When fluxes and boundaries are introduced into gauged higher Courant sigma models, generalizations of momentum maps and Hamiltonian $G$-spaces arise as consistency conditions for the models.

</details>


### [105] [Reflectionless edge states in Dirac models of topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16949)
*Alexis Drouot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究沿弯曲界面（bent interface）拼接的拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）有效模型，其哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）为 Dirac 算子 $\mathcal{D} = D_1\sigma_1 + D_2\sigma_2 + \kappa(x)\sigma_3$。对于以 0 为中心的谱窗（spectral window）内大多数能量，构造出模数为 1 透射系数（transmission coefficient）的无反射边缘态（reflectionless edge state），即畸变平面波（distorted plane wave）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在从数学上解释物理文献中的实验观测，以及作者自身的数值模拟结果。其出发点是拓扑绝缘体沿带拐角的界面拼接时所出现的无损耗边缘输运现象。

Method: 在 $L^2(\mathbb{R}^2,\mathbb{C}^2)$ 上考察 Dirac 型哈密顿量，其中 $\kappa(x)$ 表示具有拐角（corner）的畴壁（domain wall）：初始为直线，穿越 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 时发生弯曲。针对中心在 0 的谱窗内的大多数能量，构造算子的无反射边缘态，即透射系数模数为 1 的畸变平面波。

Result: 对谱窗内大多数能量，成功构造出无反射边缘态，其透射系数模数为 1。这意味着算子 $\mathcal{D}$ 存在局域在 $\kappa^{-1}(\{0\})$ 附近的模式，它们穿过拐角时既不向体态（bulk）泄漏也不发生反射，即无损耗传播。

Conclusion: 该结果从理论上解释了物理文献中的实验观测，并与作者自身的数值模拟一致，表明弯曲界面上边缘态可无损耗穿越拐角。

Abstract: We study an effective model of topological insulators joined along a bent interface. The Hamiltonian is a Dirac operator $\mathcal{D} = D_1σ_1 + D_2σ_2 + κ(x) σ_3$ on $L^2(\mathbb{R}^2,\mathbb{C}^2)$, where $κ(x)$ represents a domain wall with a corner: while initially straight, it undergoes a bend when passing from one side of $\mathbb{R}^2$ to the other. For most energies in a spectral window centered at $0$, we construct a reflectionless edge state of $\mathcal{D}$: a distorted plane wave with transmission coefficient of modulus $1$. This implies that $\mathcal{D}$ admits modes confined near $κ^{-1}(\{0\})$, that travel through the bend with no loss neither to the bulk nor to reflection. Our result explains experimental observations from the physics literature, as well as our own numerical simulations.

</details>


### [106] [Operator-theoretic and analytic properties of a driven single-mode Kerr cavity in Bargmann space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16969)
*Maciej Janowicz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Bargmann–Fock空间（Bargmann–Fock space）中研究相干驱动单模Kerr腔（Kerr cavity）的定态谱（stationary spectrum）。对正Kerr耦合，哈密顿量自伴、下有界且具紧预解式；通过连分数与Liouville变换将本征值方程归结为退化双合流Heun问题（degenerate double-confluent Heun problem），并在强驱动下证明主导转折点能量系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干驱动Kerr腔是非线性量子光学的核心模型，但其定态谱的严格解析刻画尚不完整。论文旨在借助Bargmann–Fock表示与特殊函数工具，建立精确的谱条件并对强驱动渐近系数给出严格或条件性结果。

Method: 在Bargmann–Fock空间表述问题；对非零驱动由最小解连分数（minimal-solution continued fraction）导出精确标量谱条件；用Liouville变换将本征值方程化为退化双合流Heun问题并分类奇点；采用Olver型Volterra构造（Olver-type Volterra construction）给出无穷远处归一化扇形解及显式误差界；辅以Bogoliubov与复WKB计算作一致性检验，并形式化弱驱动展开及上边缘三对角级数求和。

Result: 证得正Kerr耦合下哈密顿量自伴、下有界、具紧预解式，非零驱动时全部本征值单重；获得精确标量谱条件与Heun型奇点分类，及带显式误差界的扇形解；强驱动区主导转折点能量系数被证明，次阶系数依赖于所陈述的全局连接猜想（global-connection conjecture）；弱驱动展开由Bargmann递推验证，其上边缘三对角级数以闭式求和。

Conclusion: 论文构建了该问题谱分析的严格框架，明确了可证结果与依赖猜想的部分。文中探索性WKB迭代图示仅作可视化辅助，不构成对全局连接猜想的证明。

Abstract: We study the stationary spectrum of a coherently driven single-mode Kerr cavity in Bargmann--Fock space. For positive Kerr coupling the Hamiltonian is self-adjoint, bounded below, and has compact resolvent. For nonzero drive, a minimal-solution continued fraction yields an exact scalar spectral condition, with all eigenvalues simple. A Liouville transformation identifies the eigenvalue equation with a degenerate double-confluent Heun problem and classifies its singularities. An Olver-type Volterra construction gives normalized sectorial solutions at infinity with explicit error bounds. In the strong-drive regime, the leading turning-point energy coefficient is proved, whereas the next coefficient remains conditional on a stated global-connection conjecture; independent Bogoliubov and complex-WKB calculations provide consistency checks. A formal weak-drive expansion is verified from the Bargmann recurrence, and its upper-edge tridiagonal series is summed in closed form. Exploratory WKB iteration portraits are included only as visual aids and do not establish the conjectural global connection.

</details>


### [107] [Diassociative conformal algebras and their deformation cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17441)
*Anupam Sahoo,Apurba Das*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入二结合共形代数（diassociative conformal algebra），统一了结合共形代数（associative conformal algebra）与二结合代数（diassociative algebra），并建立其范畴等价、上同调（cohomology）与形变（deformation）理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合共形代数与二结合代数原为两类独立结构，缺乏统一框架；本文旨在给出可同时涵盖二者的代数体系，并为后续形变与扩张研究提供上同调工具。

Method: 定义二结合共形代数；证明其范畴等价于形式分布二结合代数（formal distribution diassociative algebra）的等价类范畴；借助 Maurer-Cartan 刻画（Maurer-Cartan characterization）定义上同调并构造 Gerstenhaber 代数（Gerstenhaber algebra）结构；另在结合共形代数上定义平均算子（averaging operator）。

Result: 所定义的上同调携带 Gerstenhaber 代数结构，并可用于研究该结构的形变与扩张；同时揭示结合共形代数上的平均算子与二结合共形代数之间的密切联系。

Conclusion: 二结合共形代数提供了统一结合共形代数与二结合代数的框架，其范畴等价与 Maurer-Cartan 型上同调为形变、扩张及平均算子研究奠定基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce diassociative conformal algebras that unify both associative conformal algebras and diassociative algebras. We show that the category of diassociative conformal algebras is equivalent to the category of equivalence classes of formal distribution diassociative algebras. By employing its Maurer-Cartan characterization, we then define the cohomology of a diassociative conformal algebra and show that it carries a Gerstenhaber algebra structure. Our cohomology can be used to study deformations and extensions of the structure. Finally, we define averaging operators on associative conformal algebras and find their close relations with diassociative conformal algebras.

</details>


### [108] [Higher abelian gauge theory in BV formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17518)
*Shuhan Jiang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在二次椭圆高次阿贝尔规范理论（quadratic elliptic higher abelian gauge theory）的零模空间上构造 BV 结构，并证明相应 BV 积分在温和假设下与零模拉格朗日量的选取无关，同时给出若干 p-形式阿贝尔规范理论的显式零模配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅从内容推断其关注高次阿贝尔规范理论中零模空间的处理及 BV 形式体系在该情形下的适用性。

Method: 在零模空间上构造 BV 结构（BV structure），并考察与之关联的 BV 积分；通过设定温和假设（mild hypotheses）讨论其对零模拉格朗日量（zero-mode Lagrangian）选取的依赖性。

Result: 证明所构造的 BV 积分独立于零模拉格朗日量的选取；并对若干 p-形式阿贝尔规范理论（p-form abelian gauge theories）给出显式的零模配分函数（zero-mode partition functions）。

Conclusion: 该构造为高次阿贝尔规范理论的零模分析提供了不依赖拉格朗日量选取的 BV 框架，并可用于显式计算特定 p-形式理论的零模配分函数。

Abstract: A BV structure is constructed on the space of zero modes of a quadratic elliptic higher abelian gauge theory. The associated BV integral is shown to be independent of the choice of zero-mode Lagrangian under mild hypotheses. The construction yields explicit zero-mode partition functions for several $p$-form abelian gauge theories.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Newton geometry of Bogdanov-Takens degeneracies in integrable dilatonic models: invariant divisors and boundary multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16677)
*E. Chan-López,A. Martín-Ruiz,J. M. Paulin Fuentes*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究Grumiller二维膨胀子引力模型Kantowski–Sachs内部约化动力系统，揭示其秩一幂零平衡点（nilpotent equilibrium）的Bogdanov–Takens（BT）退化互补性由单一除子组织结构强制产生，并给出精确局部恒等式确定重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约化系统在Q≠0时出现两个互补BT退化的幂零平衡点，其重数μ=3与μ=2的并存现象需要统一解释，且versal双参数BT展开是否可能存在尚待澄清。

Method: 利用Darboux可积性构造X=(1/2)u^4 X_I，其中I为质量函数、u^4为逆积分因子；通过除子上的不变性与det DX|_{u=0}=Φ_x G的分析，结合精确局部恒等式μ_0=m_1ℓ_y+m_2ℓ_x确定重数，并借助Newton图混合协体积（mixed covolume）Interpretation。

Result: 证明ab=0在整个类中恒成立，故versal双参数BT展开不可能；两个点处Bernstein–Kushnirenko界失效，而局部恒等式无需非退化假设即给出μ=3,2；对更一般系统ψ(u)情形，真空恒有μ=3、a=0、b=−m，其Dumortier–Llibre–Artés判别式为(m−2)^2。

Conclusion: 两个幂零平衡点的互补BT退化由除子组织结构统一决定，重数可用混合协体积解释；非方便图（non-convenient diagram）对应的普适定理仍待解决，且m>1时真空必有一个双曲扇区与一个椭圆扇区，与ψ无关。

Abstract: The Kantowski--Sachs interior of Grumiller's two-dimensional dilaton gravity model reduces to $\dot H=\tfrac12(Λ-3H^2-u^2)+Qu$, $\dot u=-Hu$, where $H$ is the expansion rate of the orbit two-spheres, $u$ their inverse areal radius, and $Q$ is minus twice the Rindler acceleration. For $Q\neq0$ it has two distinct rank-one nilpotent equilibria with complementary Bogdanov--Takens (BT) degeneracies: $(a,b)=(0,\neq0)$ with multiplicity $μ=3$, and $(a,b)=(\neq0,0)$ with $μ=2$. We show that this complementarity is forced by a single divisor-organized structure. The system is Darboux integrable, $X=\tfrac12u^4X_I$, where $I$ is the mass function and $u^4$ is an inverse integrating factor whose zero divisor is the invariant axis $\{u=0\}$. Off the divisor, every nilpotent equilibrium of $RX_I$ with $R(p)\neq0$ has $b=0$, from $b=D_{q_0}\operatorname{tr}DX$. On the divisor, invariance of the coordinate axis forces $a=0$ because $\det DX|_{u=0}=Φ_xG$ and both factors vanish at the corner. Hence $ab=0$ throughout the class, so a versal two-parameter BT unfolding is impossible. The Bernstein--Kushnirenko bound fails at both points; instead, the exact local identity $μ_0=m_1\ell_y+m_2\ell_x$ gives $μ=3,2$ without a nondegeneracy hypothesis. Both multiplicities have a mixed-covolume interpretation, with $\operatorname{Covol}=\sum_{k,i}\min(p_kq_i',p_i'q_k)$ on convenient diagrams; the corresponding general theorem for the non-convenient diagrams arising here remains open. For $\dot H=\tfrac{1-m}{2}H^2+ψ(u)$, $\dot u=-Hu$, with $ψ(0)=0$, $ψ'(0)\neq0$, $m\neq1$, the vacuum always has $μ=3$, $a=0$, $b=-m$. Its Dumortier--Llibre--Artés discriminant is $b_1^2+8a_3=(m-2)^2$, so for every $m>1$ it has one hyperbolic and one elliptic sector, independently of $ψ$.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Toward Pólya's Conjecture: Improving the Individual Li-Yau Bound via Energy Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17219)
*Yifan Wang,Hehu Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文为 Dirichlet Laplacian（狄利克雷拉普拉斯算子）的单个特征值建立了两类互补的下界机制：一是基于能量正交性（energy orthogonality）与径向容量浴缸原理（radial-capacity bathtub principle）的显式下界，二是通过保留求和估计中被丢弃的谱亏损（spectral deficit）并经第一 Riesz 平均恒等式（first Riesz-mean identity）积分得到的单调标量方程根。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果（如 Li-Yau 求和不等式）主要约束特征值之和，其对单个特征值的推论往往偏弱；本文旨在寻求对单个特征值更精确且保持 Weyl 标度（Weyl scaling）的下界。

Method: 机制一：由能量正交性给出有限谱投影 Fourier 密度（Fourier density）的频率相关上界，结合标准 L² Bessel 估计与径向容量浴缸原理导出显式下界，常数 c_n 由标量方程刻画。机制二：在特征值之和被其最大项控制时保留部分谱亏损，借助计数函数下界与精确的第一 Riesz 平均恒等式积分，得到严格单调的标量方程并求其唯一正根。

Result: 对 R^n（n≥2）中任意有限正测度开集，得到 λ_k ≥ c_n(2π)²ω_n^{-2/n}|Ω|^{-2/n}k^{2/n}，其中 n/(n+2)<c_n<1，且 c_2=0.5383068077…；该估计保持 Weyl 标度，并严格优于 Li-Yau 求和不等式的单个特征值推论（但不改进该求和不等式的尖锐首项系数）。机制二给出的根不弱于仅含体积（volume-only）的界，并给出相对该基线与任意独立下界取得严格改进的充要判据；借助 Jiang-Lin 的定量估计，可在有界 Lipschitz 区域上给出显式实现。

Conclusion: 两类机制互补：前者给出显式常数与 Weyl 标度下界，后者通过谱亏损的回收给出可判定的改进准则。最后的比较与数值算例将本框架内的改进与在附加几何或谱假设下才能获得的更强估计区分开来。

Abstract: We establish two complementary lower-bound mechanisms for individual eigenvalues of the Dirichlet Laplacian. First, energy orthogonality yields a frequency-dependent cap on the Fourier density of a finite spectral projection. Combining this cap with the standard $L^2$ Bessel estimate and a radial-capacity bathtub principle gives, on every open set of finite positive measure in $\mathbb R^n$ with $n\geq2$, \[ λ_k\geq c_n(2π)^2ω_n^{-2/n}|Ω|^{-2/n}k^{2/n}, \qquad \frac{n}{n+2}<c_n<1. \] The constant $c_n$ is characterized by a scalar equation, with $c_2=0.5383068077\ldots$. This estimate preserves Weyl scaling and strictly improves the individual consequence of the Li-Yau sum inequality, although it does not improve the sharp leading coefficient in that sum inequality. Second, we retain part of the spectral deficit discarded when an eigenvalue sum is bounded by its largest term. A lower bound for the counting function, integrated through the exact first Riesz-mean identity, leads to a strictly monotone scalar equation. Its unique positive root is no weaker than the volume-only bound, and we give necessary and sufficient criteria for strict improvement over both that baseline and any independent lower bound. Quantitative estimates of Jiang-Lin provide an explicit implementation on bounded Lipschitz domains. The final comparisons and numerical example distinguish improvements within this framework from stronger estimates available under additional geometric or spectral assumptions.

</details>
