### [111] [Weak ferromagnetism in the square-lattice Heisenberg $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_2^\prime$ model with easy-axis single-ion anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16593)
*Bin-Zhou Mi,Qiang Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究了具有易轴单离子各向异性（easy-axis single-ion anisotropy）$D$ 的方格子海森堡（Heisenberg）$J_1$-$J_2$-$J_2^\prime$ 模型的磁性，指出 $J_2\neq J_2^\prime$ 导致有限温度下的弱铁磁性（weak ferromagnetism），且 $D$ 为二维长程磁有序提供必要背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于近期利用光晶格（optical lattices）中超冷原子（ultracold atoms）对海森堡模型与二维交替磁性（altermagnetism）的实验实现，需从理论上考察相关模型的磁性质。

Method: 采用数值计算方法，计算方格子 $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_2^\prime$ 模型在易轴单离子各向异性 $D$ 下的子晶格磁化（sublattice magnetization）、净磁化（net magnetization）与临界温度（critical temperature）；其中 $J_1>0$ 为最近邻反铁磁交换参数，$J_2$ 与 $J_2^\prime$ 为格点上交替的两个不同次近邻超交换（superexchange）参数。

Result: 有限温度下净磁化并非恒为零，其绝对值随温度先增后减，表现为温度依赖的弱铁磁性；$J_2\neq J_2^\prime$ 是有限温度弱铁磁性的主要因素，而 $D$ 提供二维长程磁有序的必要背景。固定 $J_2+J_2^\prime$ 时，$|J_2-J_2^\prime|$ 越大，最大净磁化与临界温度越高，弱铁磁性的温度区间也越宽。

Conclusion: $J_2$ 与 $J_2^\prime$ 的差异源于磁性原子间由两类不同非磁性原子介导的迥异超交换路径；该机制不同于 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用等常规机制，或可在真实材料中被探测。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experimental realizations of the Heisenberg model and two-dimensional altermagnetism using ultracold atoms in optical lattices, we systematically investigate the magnetic properties of the square-lattice Heisenberg $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_2^\prime$ model in the presence of easy-axis single-ion anisotropy $D$. Here, $J_1 (>0)$ is the nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange parameter, while $J_2$ and $J_2^\prime$ are two distinct next-nearest-neighbor superexchange parameters that alternate on the lattice. We perform numerical calculations of the sublattice magnetization, net magnetization, and critical temperature. Interestingly, the net magnetization is not identically zero at finite temperatures; its absolute value first increases and then decreases with increasing temperature, revealing a temperature-dependent weak ferromagnetism. We find that $J_2\not=J_2^\prime$ is the primary factor responsible for weak ferromagnetism at finite temperatures, while $D$ provides the necessary background for two-dimensional long-range magnetic ordering. Moreover, for a fixed $J_2+J_2^\prime$, we observe that a larger $|J_2-J_2^\prime|$ leads to a greater maximum net magnetization and critical temperature, as well as a broader temperature range for weak ferromagnetism. The difference between $J_2$ and $J_2^\prime$ originates from the distinct superexchange pathways between magnetic atoms, with these pathways mediated by two different types of nonmagnetic atoms. This mechanism, which differs from conventional ones such as the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, may be detectable in real materials.

</details>


### [112] [Stable three-dimensional lattice solitons in spin-orbit-coupled Bose-Einstein condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17092)
*Liangwei Zeng,Boris A. Malomed,Yaroslav V. Kartashov,Xing Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该摘要研究三维（3D）光学晶格（OL）中自旋-轨道耦合（SOC）二元玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（BEC）内的孤子，发现SOC与OL的协同作用可稳定全涡旋孤子与半涡旋孤子，其稳定区域随SOC强度与晶格深度增加而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在均匀的SOC型BEC中，具有特定相位结构的全涡旋孤子无法存在，因此需要探索能够承载并稳定此类三维涡旋结构的外部条件。

Method: 采用对置于三维光学晶格中的二元自相互作用BEC的理论分析，考察自旋-轨道耦合与光学晶格的协同作用，构建全涡旋孤子与格点中心半涡旋的自洽解，并分别在自吸引与自排斥体系中分析孤子态及带隙孤子。

Result: SOC与OL的相互作用形成稳定的全涡旋孤子（由晶格相邻势阱中的四个密度峰组成并叠加全局涡旋相位）与半涡旋（涡度仅存在于一个分量中）。在自吸引与自排斥三元体系中均存在此类态；其稳定区域以化学势与粒子数为坐标，随SOC强度和OL深度增加而扩大，且未受三维超临界坍缩的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建三维具有涡旋特性的凝聚体复合结构提供了可行路径，并指出这类结构可借助现有实验技术实现。

Abstract: We address three-dimensional (3D) solitons maintained by spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in the binary self-interacting Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) held in the 3D optical lattice (OL). The analysis reveals that the SOC-OL interplay results in the formation of stable full-vortex (FV) soli-tons, built as sets of four density peaks residing in neighboring wells of the lattice potential, with the superimposed global vortical phase, and site-centered semi-vortices (SV), in which the vorticity is present in only one component. The full-vortex solitons, with their specific phase textures, do not exist in a uniform BEC with SOC. Full-vortex and semi-vortex states in the binary self-attractive BEC are stable despite the possibility of the supercritical collapse in the 3D system. Such states also exist, as gap solitons, in the self-repulsive 3D system. In terms of the chemical potential and number of particles, the stability regions of the 3D full-vortex solitons and semi-vortices expand with the increase of the SOC strength and OL depth. The results open the route to the creation of 3D complexes of vorticity-carrying condensates that can be realized with existing experimental techniques.

</details>


### [113] [Course on two-dimensional quantum gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17097)
*Hélène Perrin*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本课程讲义围绕二维量子气体（two-dimensional quantum gases）展开，分为三次讲座：从理想气体玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensation）与弱相互作用理论出发，进而讨论二维体系的相位涨落、准长程有序、BKT 超流机制与标度对称性，最后以快速旋转准二维玻色气体及涡旋晶格热熔化（thermal melting）为专题研讨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该材料为 ICTP-SAIFR 冷原子学校课程讲义，旨在系统介绍二维量子气体的基础理论与特有物理现象，为学习者建立从三维理想玻色气体到二维强涨落体系的知识框架。

Method: 采用三次讲座的渐进式教学结构：第一讲以平均场层面的理论工具为主，包括 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程（Gross-Pitaevskii equation）及其流体力学表述；第二讲聚焦二维体系的核心特征，如相互作用的重要性、增强的相位涨落（phase fluctuations）、准长程有序（quasi long-range order）、Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 机制下的超流及标度对称性（scaling symmetry）；第三讲以研讨会形式处理快速旋转准二维玻色气体。

Result: 摘要未给出具体研究数据或实验结果，仅说明课程内容的组织方式与涵盖主题，并指出配套幻灯片可在学校网站获取。

Conclusion: 课程内容构成一条由基础到专题的完整讲解路径，最终落脚于快速旋转准二维玻色气体中涡旋晶格（vortex lattice）的热熔化问题。

Abstract: This course on two-dimensional quantum gases, prepared for the S{ã}o Paulo ICTP-SAIFR IVth School on Light and Cold Atoms, is organized into three lectures. The first introductory lecture addresses Bose-Einstein condensation in an ideal gas, the effect of interactions in the weakly interacting limit, Gross-Pitaevskii equation and its hydrodynamic formulation, as well as superfluidity. The second lecture is devoted to the main features of two-dimensional quantum gases: importance of the interactions, enhanced phase fluctuations and the emergence of a quasi long-range order, superfluidity through the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless mechanism, scaling symmetry. The third lecture has taken the form of a seminar on fast rotating quasi two-dimensional Bose gases and thermal melting of the vortex lattice. The slides accompanying these notes can be found on the school website: https://www.ictp-saifr.org/slca2025/.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Generalized Detectors at Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16114)
*Mark Gonzalez,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将探测器关联函数（detector correlation functions）的研究从平均零能算符 $\mathcal{E}(\vec{n})$ 推广至更一般的探测器算符 $\mathcal{E}^n_R(\vec{n})$，并建立其红外-紫外匹配的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能量关联函数（energy correlators）研究主要集中于平均零能算符 $\mathcal{E}(\vec{n})$ 的关联函数，但对带电强子等强子子集上能量幂次的广义探测器算符 $\mathcal{E}^n_R(\vec{n})$ 缺乏理论描述。这类算符通常不具备红外与共线安全性（infrared and collinear safe），需要在红外强子探测器与紫外部分子探测器之间进行非微扰匹配（nonperturbative matching）。

Method: 引入普适的非微扰匹配系数，称为“探测器函数（detector functions）”，用于实现红外-紫外匹配；对于所研究的 $\mathcal{E}^n_R$ 算符，这些系数由单强子与多强子碎裂函数（fragmentation functions）的能量加权矩表示。进一步给出其重整化群（renormalization group）结构，推导投影关联函数的 QCD 因子化定理（QCD factorization theorems），并在次领头阶（next-to-leading order）计算喷注函数（jet functions）。在固定耦合纯杨-米尔斯（pure Yang-Mills）极限下推导强子探测器的光锥算符乘积展开（light-ray OPE），并将其与 QCD 因子化框架相联系。

Result: 建立了计算广义探测器多点关联函数的框架，明确了探测器函数的重整化群结构与 QCD 因子化定理，并完成次领头阶喷注函数计算。识别出由软辐射（soft radiation）产生的普适非微扰幂修正（power corrections），其在共线极限（collinear limit）下被增强，可显著改变微扰角标度行为。部分子簇射（parton shower）研究结果与所预测的微扰及非微扰标度行为定性一致。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓展了理论上可控的探测器算符空间，并具有潜在的唯象学应用价值。

Abstract: Recent progress in collider physics has reformulated phenomenological questions in terms of detector correlation functions and advanced their theoretical understanding. These advances have primarily focused on correlators of the average null energy operator, $\mathcal{E}(\vec{n})$, known as energy correlators. However, colliders have access to a much broader class of detector operators, $\mathcal{E}^n_R(\vec{n})$, which measure powers of the energy on a subset of hadrons $R$, such as charged hadrons. These generalized detectors are generically not infrared and collinear safe, and their description requires nonperturbative matching between the hadronic detectors measured in the infrared and the partonic detectors used in ultraviolet calculations. We develop a framework for computing their multi-point correlation functions. We introduce universal nonperturbative matching coefficients, termed "detector functions", that implement this infrared-ultraviolet matching. For the $\mathcal{E}_R^n$ operators studied here, these coefficients are represented by energy-weighted moments of single- and multi-hadron fragmentation functions. We present their renormalization group structure, derive QCD factorization theorems for the projected correlators, and compute jet functions through next-to-leading order. In the fixed-coupling pure Yang-Mills limit, we derive the light-ray OPE of hadronic detectors and connect it to the QCD factorization framework. We also identify universal nonperturbative power corrections generated by soft radiation, which are enhanced in the collinear limit and can substantially modify the perturbative angular scaling. A parton shower study finds qualitative agreement with the predicted perturbative and nonperturbative scaling behaviors. Our work significantly broadens the space of detector operators under theoretical control, with potential phenomenological applications.

</details>


### [115] [Composite dark matter at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16156)
*Alan S. Cornell,Louie Corpe,Aldo Deandrea,Benjamin Fuks,Taylor Murphy,Clarisse Prat*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究部分复合（partial compositeness）启发的标量暗物质（scalar dark matter）模型：暗物质经一组色荷矢量样费米子（coloured vector-like fermions）与第三代夸克耦合，并综合利用遗迹丰度、直接/间接探测与大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）数据检验其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单中介粒子（single-mediator）描述过于简化，无法体现部分复合框架中多个中介态（mediator states）并存所带来的更丰富现象学。因此需要构建并系统检验含多个色荷中介粒子的复合启发模型，并与多类观测数据对照。

Method: 在部分复合动机下，构建标量暗物质与第三代夸克通过非最小色荷矢量样费米子集合耦合的模型，并对该框架施加遗迹丰度（relic abundance）、直接探测（direct detection）、间接探测（indirect detection）及 LHC 数据的约束。在碰撞机层面，比较专用新物理搜寻（dedicated new-physics searches）与标准模型过程展开测量（unfolded measurements）的敏感度，并评估高阶修正（higher-order corrections）对不同产生机制的影响。

Result: 专用搜寻通常给出最强的碰撞机排除界限，而展开测量提供互补敏感度。直接探测随相互作用强度增大而愈发有力，并可成为主导约束。这种更广泛的互补性源于：碰撞机数据直接检验色荷中介粒子的产生与衰变，而宇宙学与天体粒子（astroparticle）观测则约束支配暗物质丰度、散射与湮灭的相互作用。

Conclusion: 现有约束显著限制了该模型，但仍存在与观测暗物质丰度相容的可行参数区域（viable regions）。

Abstract: We investigate a composite-inspired model in which a scalar dark matter candidate couples to third-generation quarks through a non-minimal set of coloured vector-like fermions, as motivated by partial compositeness. The simultaneous presence of several mediator states leads to a richer phenomenology than conventional single-mediator descriptions, and we confront the framework with the observed relic abundance, direct-detection and indirect-detection constraints, as well as data from the Large Hadron Collider. At colliders, we compare the sensitivity of dedicated new-physics searches with that of unfolded measurements of Standard Model processes, and we assess the impact of higher-order corrections on the different production mechanisms. We find that dedicated searches generally provide the strongest collider exclusions, while unfolded measurements offer complementary sensitivity. Direct detection becomes increasingly powerful as the interaction strength grows and can become the dominant constraint. This broader complementarity arises because collider data directly test the production and decay of the coloured mediators, whereas cosmological and astroparticle observables constrain the interactions governing dark-matter abundance, scattering and annihilation. Although present constraints significantly restrict the model, viable regions compatible with the observed dark-matter abundance remain.

</details>


### [116] [Top-quark pair production sensitivity to heavy new physics at the FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16342)
*Jacopo De Piccoli,Gauthier Durieux,Sergio Sánchez Cruz,Michele Selvaggi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究评估 FCC-ee 上顶夸克对产生过程对重新物理的敏感性，采用基于矩阵元的判别量，在标准模型有效场论框架下结合探测器重建与喷注味标记，给出近最优的多维约束方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是探究 FCC-ee 顶夸克对产生过程对重新物理的探测能力，并以标准模型有效场论（Standard Model effective field theory, SMEFT）系统参数化新物理效应。

Method: 基于矩阵元（matrix element）构造判别量，纳入 IDEA 探测器的快速模拟所得探测器重建效应及喷注味标记（jet flavour tagging）；考虑修改顶夸克电弱耦合或产生四费米子相互作用（four-fermion interactions）的六维算符，在领头阶（leading order）与含两个共振顶夸克的图进行计算，并利用完整六体运动学信息。

Result: 对 12 个判别量（每个算符一个）每个维度取两个分箱，可提供简单稳健且近最优的多维约束；单算符拟合（single-operator fit）中新物理标度上界可达 30 TeV，全局多算符拟合（global multi-operator fit）中可达 5 TeV；与高亮度 LHC（High-Luminosity LHC）顶夸克算符预期的联合分析显示互补敏感性，有助于破除近似简并。

Conclusion: FCC-ee 顶夸克对产生结合矩阵元判别量与快速探测器模拟，可对重新物理给出近最优多维约束；与 HL-LHC 结果联合可提升对近似简并的区分能力。

Abstract: We study the sensitivity of top-quark pair production at the FCC-ee to heavy new physics, parametrised in the Standard Model effective field theory, using discriminants based on matrix elements. The analysis includes detector reconstruction effects, obtained with a fast simulation of the IDEA detector concept, and jet flavour tagging. We consider dimension-six operators that modify the electroweak couplings of the top quark or generate four-fermion interactions, at leading order and in diagrams with two resonant top quarks. The full six-body kinematic information is employed to maximise the discrimination power against the Standard Model. Two bins per dimension in the space of 12 discriminants, one per operator considered, provide a simple and robust methodology to set nearly optimal multidimensional constraints. Bounds on the new physics scale can reach values up to 30 TeV in a single-operator fit and up to 5 TeV in a global multi-operator fit. A combination with High-Luminosity LHC prospects on top-quark operators shows complementary sensitivities which help lift approximate degeneracies.

</details>


### [117] [Topological Integer-Winding Dark Matter: Stability from $π_2(\mathrm{SU}(3)/\mathrm{SO}(3)) = \mathbb{Z}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16383)
*Ahmed Ali*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过 SU(3)_dark -> SO(3)_dark x Z_2 对称性破缺产生由 pi_2 = Z 分类的绝对稳定点状拓扑缺陷（topological defects），其最轻中性成员 chi^0 仅经动力学混合门户（kinetic-mixing portal）与引力耦合，可给出可证伪的质量比与多信使观测预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建暗物质模型，使暗部门（dark sector）中的拓扑缺陷获得绝对稳定性，并以最少耦合（动力学混合与引力）解释暗物质丰度及可检验信号。

Method: 采用 SU(3)_dark -> SO(3)_dark x Z_2 对称性破缺模式，由 pi_2(SU(3)/[SO(3) x Z_2]) = Z 对点状拓扑缺陷分类；引入含暗荷（darkicity）T_dark 的扩展 Gell-Mann-Okubo 质量算符，并使用八个耦合 Boltzmann 方程（Boltzmann equations）加入共湮灭（co-annihilation）计算遗迹丰度。

Result: 预测两个标度无关质量比 r_21 = 1.042 +/- 0.003 与 r_31 = 0.809 +/- 0.004，可在 FCC-ee 检验；在 M_0 = 150 GeV 时给出 Omega_DM h^2 = 0.120；自相互作用截面（self-interaction cross-sections）满足从矮星系到子弹星团（Bullet Cluster）的所有天体物理限制；预言 f_* = 1.65 mHz 的随机引力波背景（LISA，SNR = 8.5）与 r_DAO = 2.3 +/- 0.4 Mpc 的暗声学振荡（dark acoustic oscillations，SKA）。

Conclusion: 该模型在动力学混合耦合 g_f = 10^-3 且 M_0 = 150 GeV 下同时满足暗物质丰度与天体物理约束，并提供 FCC-ee、LISA 与 SKA 可检验的多信使预言。

Abstract: We propose a dark matter model in which the symmetry-breaking pattern SU(3)_dark -> SO(3)_dark x Z_2 produces absolutely stable point-like topological defects classified by pi_2(SU(3)/[SO(3) x Z_2]) = Z. The lightest neutral member chi^0 of the resulting dark octet couples to Standard Model fields only through a kinetic-mixing portal (g_f = 10^-3) and gravity. An extended Gell-Mann-Okubo mass operator incorporating the quantum number darkicity T_dark predicts two scale-independent mass ratios, r_21 = M(chi^++)/M(chi^0) = 1.042 +/- 0.003 and r_31 = M(Sigma^0_dark)/M(chi^0) = 0.809 +/- 0.004, falsifiable at FCC-ee. Eight coupled Boltzmann equations with co-annihilation yield Omega_DM h^2 = 0.120 at M_0 = 150 GeV. Self-interaction cross-sections satisfy all astrophysical bounds from dwarf galaxies to the Bullet Cluster. The model predicts a stochastic gravitational-wave background at f_* = 1.65 mHz (LISA, SNR = 8.5) and dark acoustic oscillations at r_DAO = 2.3 +/- 0.4 Mpc (SKA).

</details>


### [118] [Searching for new physics with contact-free transitions in muonic atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16728)
*Noam Burger,Ben Ohayon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文开展可行性研究（feasibility study），论证量子传感（quantum sensing）技术的新进展可用于对μ子原子（muonic atoms）中无接触跃迁（contact-free transitions）的理论预言与实验测量进行严格比对，从而探测μ子—质子自旋无关相互作用（spin-independent muon-proton interactions）的新参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: μ子原子中无接触跃迁的理论与实验对比尚未达到足够严格的水平，导致自旋无关μ子—质子相互作用的参数空间存在未探索区域，且基本常数（fundamental constants）提取与新物理（physics beyond the Standard Model）搜寻相互纠缠。

Method: 采用量子传感技术的最新进展，将其应用于μ子原子无接触跃迁的测量，以实现高精度的理论—实验比对。

Result: 可行性研究结果表明，该类比对能够探测此前未探索的μ子—质子自旋无关相互作用参数空间，并可分离基本常数提取与新物理搜寻。

Conclusion: 量子传感技术可为实现严格的μ子原子无接触跃迁检验提供可行路径，兼具探测新相互作用与厘清基本常数提取的双重价值。

Abstract: We present a feasibility study demonstrating how recent advances in quantum-sensing technologies can facilitate stringent comparisons between theoretical predictions and experimental measurements of \emph{contact-free} transitions in muonic atoms. Such comparisons can probe previously unexplored regions of the parameter space governing spin-independent muon-proton interactions and disentangle the extraction of fundamental constants from searches for physics beyond the Standard Model.

</details>


### [119] [Glueballs: hadrons without quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16790)
*Cyrille Chevalier,Vincent Mathieu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了胶球（glueball）——由胶子（gluon）构成的色单态束缚态（colour-singlet bound state）——的理论与实验研究现状。内容涵盖纯规范谱（pure-gauge spectrum）、多种理论方法、淬火近似下的标度设定困难、全QCD中的混合效应、衰变机制以及实验候选者（如 f0(1370)/f0(1500)/f0(1710)、η(1405)/η(1475)、X(2370) 等）的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶球是量子色动力学（Quantum Chromodynamics, QCD）非阿贝尔结构（non-Abelian structure）的必然结果，在纯杨-米尔斯极限（pure Yang-Mills limit）下甚至是理论中唯一的物理激发。因此，系统梳理关于胶球的理论与实验认知具有基础性意义。

Method: 综述性方法：先确定两胶子与三胶子态可携带的自旋、宇称与电荷共轭量子数，再依次介绍格点QCD（lattice QCD）、组分胶子与库仑规范模型（constituent-gluon and Coulomb-gauge models）、泛函Dyson-Schwinger与Bethe-Salpeter方程、全息模型（holographic models）及QCD求和规则（QCD sum rules）等主要理论途径，随后转入全QCD层面讨论。

Result: 各类理论方法得出了各自的结论；特别关注了限制淬火胶球质量（quenched glueball mass）换算为物理单位的标度设定（scale-setting）不确定性。在全QCD部分，讨论了去淬火效应（unquenching effects）、胶球-夸克反夸克混合（glueball-$q\bar{q}$ mixing）、大$N_c$计数（large-$N_c$ counting）、质量矩阵与有效拉格朗日方法、选择定则与衰变机制，并逐扇区评估实验候选态。

Conclusion: 标量扇区（scalar sector）存在竞争性诠释；赝标量扇区（pseudoscalar sector）涉及 $η(1405)/η(1475)$ 与 $X(2370)$；张量区（tensor region）较为拥挤；$C=-1$ 扇区基本未被探索且与 Odderon 相关。文末展望了可验证或识别更多候选态的研究计划。

Abstract: Glueballs are colour-singlet bound states built from gluons alone. They are an unavoidable consequence of the non-Abelian structure of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD), and, in the pure Yang--Mills limit, they are the only physical excitations of the theory. The present article reviews what is known about them. After establishing which spin, parity and charge-conjugation quantum numbers two- and three-gluon states can carry, the pure-gauge spectrum is surveyed. Each of the main theoretical approaches and their respective conclusions are briefly presented. They include lattice QCD, constituent-gluon and Coulomb-gauge models, functional Dyson--Schwinger and Bethe--Salpeter equations, holographic models, and QCD sum rules. Particular attention is paid to the scale-setting ambiguity that limits how precisely a quenched glueball mass can be converted into physical units. The discussion then turns to full QCD. After discussing the meaning of a glueball assignment in this context, unquenching effects and questions related to glueball--$q\bar{q}$ mixing are addressed. The large-$N_c$ counting that underpins the mixing picture, as well as mass-matrix and effective-Lagrangian treatments of mixing are presented. The selection rules and dynamical mechanisms that shape glueball decays are also discussed. The gluon-rich production mechanisms used experimentally are finally reviewed. The candidates are assessed sector by sector: $f_0(1370)/f_0(1500)/f_0(1710)$ and the competing interpretations of the scalar sector, $η(1405)/η(1475)$ and $X(2370)$ in the pseudoscalar sector, the crowded tensor region, and the essentially unexplored $C=-1$ sector and its connection to the Odderon. Outlooks on the programmes that could help in validating some of these candidates or identifying others are reviewed as a conclusion.

</details>


### [120] [Cosmological Constrained Axion-Portal Inelastic Dark Matter for the LZ Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17412)
*Haipeng An,Fei Gao,Jia Liu,Minghao Liu,Changlong Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）高反冲候选事件，研究双态非弹性暗物质（inelastic dark matter）模型：χ₁、χ₂ 通过轴子样粒子（axion-like particle, ALP）非对角耦合，并追踪两态宇宙学演化，发现暗扇区转换过程决定今日 χ₂ 丰度，进而动态选择直接探测中的主导非弹性散射方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN 报告的高反冲候选事件提示需要非标准运动学与动量依赖相互作用（momentum-dependent interactions）的暗物质情景。既有处理多假设今日仅存在单一暗物质态，本文旨在纳入两态的完整宇宙学演化。

Method: 构建双态非弹性暗物质模型，χ₁ 与 χ₂ 非对角耦合至同时耦合胶子与光子的 ALP。追踪两态宇宙学演化，通过暗扇区转换过程 χ₂χ₂↔χ₁χ₁ 确定激发态 χ₂ 的残余丰度，并区分标量（scalar）与赝标量（pseudoscalar）跃迁耦合的洛伦兹结构。

Result: χ₂ 寿命足够长可存活至今，其丰度强烈依赖 DM–ALP 相互作用的洛伦兹结构：标量跃迁耦合高效耗竭 χ₂，倾向吸热（endothermic）χ₁N→χ₂N 散射；赝标量跃迁耦合保持 f₂≃1/2，反冲谱由放热（exothermic）χ₂N→χ₁N 下散射主导。四种情景的基准谱均可峰值于观测反冲能约 250 keV 附近。

Conclusion: 直接探测中非弹性散射的主导方向可由暗扇区的早期宇宙演化动态选择；更多数据下年度调制（annual modulation）测量有望区分这些情景，且 ALP 门户可在对撞机上直接探测。

Abstract: The recent high-recoil candidate event reported by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) motivates dark-matter scenarios with nonstandard kinematics and momentum-dependent interactions. We study a two-state inelastic dark-matter model in which $χ_1$ and $χ_2$ couple off-diagonally to an axion-like particle (ALP) that also couples to gluons and photons. Unlike treatments that assume only one dark-matter state is present today, we track the cosmological evolution of both states. The excited state $χ_2$ is sufficiently long-lived to survive to the present, with its relic fraction determined by the dark-sector conversion process $χ_2χ_2\leftrightarrowχ_1χ_1$. We find that this fraction depends strongly on the Lorentz structure of the DM--ALP interaction: scalar transition couplings efficiently deplete $χ_2$, favoring endothermic $χ_1 N\toχ_2 N$ scattering, whereas pseudoscalar transition couplings preserve $f_2\simeq1/2$, yielding recoil spectra dominated by exothermic $χ_2 N\toχ_1 N$ down-scattering. Benchmark spectra in all four scenarios can peak near the observed recoil energy of $250\,\mathrm{keV}$. Our results demonstrate that the dominant direction of inelastic scattering in direct detection can be dynamically selected by the early-Universe evolution of the dark sector. With additional data, annual modulation measurements could distinguish these scenarios. We further show that the ALP portal can be directly probed at colliders.

</details>


### [121] [Vanilla Scotogenic Model at the future Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17530)
*Niloy Mondal,Dipankar Pradhan,Abhik Sarkar,Biplob Bhattacherjee,Subhaditya Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要讨论原味斯科托genic模型（vanilla scotogenic model, VSM）在轻子生成（leptogenesis）、暗物质（dark matter, DM）与中微子质量统一解释下的可探测性，重点分析长寿命带电标量（long-lived particle, LLP）在缪子对撞机上的位移顶点信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VSM 提供中微子质量、暗物质与宇宙重子不对称（baryon asymmetry of the Universe）的统一解释，但典型轻子生成能标约 10 TeV 以上，难以被现有或近期对撞机探测。作者希望借助增强的第二代汤川耦合（second-generation Yukawa couplings）将能标降至约 1 TeV，从而在未来的缪子对撞机（muon colliders）上进行检验。

Method: 在同时满足暗物质、中微子质量、轻子生成与味道相关约束（flavour dependent constraints）的前提下，对模型中带电标量的瞬时（prompt）与位移顶点（displaced vertex）两类信号进行分析，并考虑时间分辨率（timing resolutions）与位移顶点重建效率的影响。

Result: 带电标量很可能是长寿命粒子（LLP）；瞬时单光子（mono-photon）信号仍然难以观测，而 LLP 搜索则为探测该框架提供了有前景的途径，但其灵敏度受时间分辨率与位移顶点重建效率制约。

Conclusion: 在约束条件下，瞬时信号探测困难，LLP 位移顶点搜索是检验该低能标轻子生成框架的更有希望方向。

Abstract: The vanilla scotogenic model (VSM) featuring three right-handed neutrinos and an inert scalar doublet, offers a unified explanation of neutrino masses, dark matter (DM), and the baryon asymmetry of the Universe through leptogenesis. The typical mass scale of such a scenario lies in the vicinity of $\sim$ 10 TeV and above, whereas enhanced second-generation Yukawa couplings lower the leptogenesis scale to $\sim 1$ \TeV, allowing the model to be probed at the future muon colliders. Adhering to DM, neutrino mass, Leptogenesis, and flavour dependent constraints, the charged scalar is likely to be a long-lived particle (LLP). We analyse both the prompt and displaced vertex signatures of the model to find that, prompt mono-photon signals remain challenging, but LLP searches offer a promising avenue to probe such a framework, subject to the timing resolutions, and displaced-vertex reconstruction efficiency.

</details>


### [122] [Reconciling axion quality with post-inflation cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16110)
*Luca Di Luzio,Samuele Di Valeriano,Marco Nardecchia,Enrico Nardi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对后暴胀 Peccei-Quinn（PQ）破缺情形下 QCD 轴子模型常见的轴子质量/宇宙学张力，提出 PQ 对称性由 SU(N)_L × SU(N)_R 规范对称性自发破缺中偶然涌现的框架，使 PQ 破缺最早出现在 N 维算符，从而受保护。构造避免了畴壁问题、分数电荷残留与 Landau 极点，并给出轴子构成主要暗物质的可行参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后暴胀 PQ 破缺情景中，QCD 轴子模型常面临轴子质量（axion quality）与宇宙学约束之间的张力。该文旨在提出一个能同时满足 PQ 质量保护与宇宙学要求的框架。

Method: PQ 对称性从 SU(N)_L × SU(N)_R 规范对称性自发破缺至 SU(N)_{L+R} 的过程中偶然涌现；PQ 破缺最早出现在算符维度 N，因而其质量受到保护。同时要求构造避免畴壁问题、无稳定分数电荷残留、并在 PQ 破缺算符能标以下无 Landau 极点。

Result: 产生 PQ 反常所需的奇异夸克（exotic quarks）强子化为不稳定介子与宇宙学稳定的中性重子；但大 N 下 SU(N) 规范理论中重子形成被强烈抑制的论证表明，其对暗物质能量密度的贡献仍处次要地位。结合宇宙学约束、PQ 质量与微扰性要求，得到轴子构成大部分暗物质且质量可预测的可行参数空间。

Conclusion: 该框架可缓解 PQ 质量与宇宙学的张力，在可行参数空间内轴子构成主要暗物质，且其质量保持可预测。

Abstract: We address the axion quality/cosmology tension that often plagues QCD axion models in post-inflationary Peccei-Quinn (PQ) breaking scenarios. In the proposed framework, the PQ symmetry emerges accidentally from an ${\rm SU}(\mathcal{N})_L\times{\rm SU}(\mathcal{N})_R$ gauge symmetry spontaneously broken to ${\rm SU}(\mathcal{N})_{L+R}$, and its quality is protected because PQ breaking first appears at operator dimension $\mathcal{N}$. Moreover, the construction avoids the domain-wall problem, ensures that no stable fractionally charged relic survives ${\rm SU} (\mathcal{N})_{L+R}$ confinement, and remains free of Landau poles below the scale of PQ-breaking operators. The exotic quarks required to generate the PQ anomaly hadronize into unstable mesons and cosmologically stable neutral baryons. However, plausible arguments indicating a strong suppression of baryon formation in ${\rm SU}(\mathcal{N})$ gauge theories at large $\mathcal{N}$, suggest that their contribution to the dark matter energy density remains subdominant. Combining cosmological constraints with PQ quality and perturbativity requirements yields a viable parameter space in which the axion makes up most of the dark matter and its mass remains predictable.

</details>


### [123] [Eclectic flavour symmetries without flavons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16118)
*Ferruccio Feruglio,Antonio Marrone*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个无需flavon的框架，将eclectic群实现为味对称性，聚焦$\\Omega(1)$群。该群可视为整Jacobi群（integral Jacobi group）的有限像，作用在两个模（moduli）$(\\tau,z)$上。作者构建了超对称Jacobi不变理论、费米子质量模型与CP对称性，并推广至$T^2/\\mathbb Z_4$、$T^2/\\mathbb Z_2$情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统eclectic味模型依赖flavon场来破缺对称性，带来额外自由度与动力学假设。本文旨在以模（modular）几何图像替代flavon机制，将eclectic群自然实现为味对称性。

Method: 将$\\Omega(1)$实现为整Jacobi群（$H(\\mathbb Z)$与$SL(2,\\mathbb Z)$的半直积）的有限像，作用于模$(\\tau,z)$；其中$H(\\mathbb Z)$保持$\\tau$不变，其有限像$\\Delta(27)$充当传统味对称群。进而构造超对称（supersymmetric）Jacobi不变理论（含整体超对称与超引力）及一致的CP对称性。

Result: 给出构造超对称Jacobi不变理论所需要素；建立一个现实可行的费米子质量模型，并在拉格朗日量层面施加CP对称性，使其由模自发破缺；说明传统eclectic-flavour描述如何在适当极限下恢复；将该几何解释推广到$T^2/\\mathbb Z_4$与$T^2/\\mathbb Z_2$构件，并阐明$T^2/\\mathbb Z_6$结构上不同的原因。

Conclusion: Jacobi群框架为eclectic味对称性提供几何解释，可在无需flavon的情况下实现$\\Omega(1)$等对称结构，并自然容纳CP破缺与模动力学，同时涵盖多种环面（torus）构件情形。

Abstract: We develop a new framework in which eclectic groups are realised as flavour symmetries without flavons. Focusing on the group $Ω(1)$, we show that it can be realised as a finite image of the integral Jacobi group, i.e. the semidirect product of $H({\mathbb Z})$ with $SL(2,{\mathbb Z})$, acting on two moduli $(τ,z)$. The Heisenberg subgroup $H({\mathbb Z})$ leaves the modulus $τ$ unchanged, and its finite image $Δ(27)$ plays the role of the traditional flavour symmetry. We provide the ingredients needed to construct supersymmetric Jacobi-invariant theories, both in globally supersymmetric theories and in supergravity. We develop a realistic model of fermion masses within this framework and formulate a consistent CP symmetry, which can be imposed at the Lagrangian level and broken spontaneously by the moduli. We discuss how the conventional eclectic-flavour description is recovered in an appropriate limit. We also extend this geometric interpretation to the $T^2/\mathbb Z_4$ and $T^2/\mathbb Z_2$ eclectic building blocks, and clarify why the $T^2/\mathbb Z_6$ case is structurally different.

</details>


### [124] [A Casimir bottleneck in primordial large-N baryon formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16123)
*Luca Di Luzio,Samuele Di Valeriano,Enrico Nardi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在限制性 $SU(\mathcal{N})$ 规范理论（gauge theory）中早期宇宙的重子（baryon）形成问题，指出在大 $\mathcal{N}$ 极限下，色禁闭（confinement）期间的瓶颈效应主导了遗迹丰度（relic density），而非湮灭冻结（freeze-out）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $SU(\mathcal{N})$ 暗物质（dark matter）模型中，重子遗迹丰度的估计通常依赖于标准的热冻结机制，但大 $\mathcal{N}$ 情形下的禁闭过程可能改变这一图像，因此有必要重新审视重子形成动力学。

Method: 采用夸克位于基础表示（fundamental representation）的限制性 $SU(\mathcal{N})$ 规范理论，并借助禁闭势的卡西米尔标度（Casimir scaling）分析大 $\mathcal{N}$ 下的重子形成与破坏过程。

Result: 在大 $\mathcal{N}$ 下，小夸克团簇（quark cluster）阶段的重子破坏过程远超形成过程，形成瓶颈（bottleneck）；在 $\mathcal{B}$-$\bar{\mathcal{B}}$ 对称等离子体（plasma）中，宇宙学稳定重子的遗迹丰度由禁闭时期设定，而非由湮灭冻结决定。

Conclusion: 大 $\mathcal{N}$ 下禁闭动力学对遗迹丰度的主导作用，会影响 $SU(\mathcal{N})$ 暗物质模型的遗迹丰度估计，需修正以往基于冻结机制的预测。

Abstract: We study baryon $(\mathcal{B})$ formation in the early Universe in a confining $SU(\mathcal{N})$ gauge theory with quarks transforming in the fundamental representation. Casimir scaling of the confining potential implies that, at large $\mathcal{N}$, $\mathcal{B}$ formation is hindered by a bottleneck: for small quark clusters, representing the initial stages of $\mathcal{B}$ assembly, destruction processes greatly outweigh formation processes. In a $\mathcal{B}$-$\bar{\mathcal{B}}$ symmetric plasma, the relic density of cosmologically stable $\mathcal{B}$'s is set, for large $\mathcal{N}$, during confinement rather than by annihilation freeze-out. This affects relic-density estimates for $SU(\mathcal{N})$ dark matter models.

</details>


### [125] [Matching relations for gluon TMDs up to one-loop accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16945)
*Alessio Carmelo Alvaro,Nanako Kato,Barbara Pasquini,Cristian Pisano,Simone Rodini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作给出了胶子横向动量依赖部分子分布函数（gluon transverse momentum dependent parton distribution functions, gluon TMDs）匹配关系（matching relations）至单圈（one-loop）精度的新结果，涵盖树图阶高达twist 3的关系以及一圈阶的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶子TMD与共线部分子分布函数之间的匹配关系是大b与小b区域间演化的关键环节，但此前对高twist及质量修正的处理尚不完整，因此需要系统建立更高精度与更高twist的匹配关系。

Method: 树图阶（tree-level）采用旋量形式（spinor formalism），将匹配关系推导至twist 3精度，覆盖T-even与T-odd分布并纳入强子质量修正（hadron-mass corrections）；一圈阶（one-loop）则将部分子内部分子方法（parton-in-parton approach）扩展至小b展开（small-b expansion）中的高twist算符。

Result: 首次导出了worm-gear T分布的Wandzura-Wilczek关系（Wandzura-Wilczek relation），并建立了一套在一圈与twist-2精度下纳入质量修正的系统方法。

Conclusion: 该研究拓展了胶子TMD匹配关系的精度与适用范围，为高twist胶子TMD及其质量修正的处理提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We present new results on the matching relations for gluon transverse momentum dependent parton distribution functions up to one-loop accuracy. At tree-level, employing the spinor formalism, we obtain these relations up to twist 3 accuracy for both T-even and T-odd distributions, including the hadron-mass corrections. At one-loop order, we extend the parton-in-parton approach to include higher-twist operators in the small-$b$ expansion. As a result, we derive for the first time the Wandzura-Wilczek relation for the worm-gear T distributions and establish a systematic method to include mass corrections at one-loop and twist-2 accuracies.

</details>


### [126] [A possible interpretation of the LUX-ZEPLIN recoil event in the 2HD+a scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17196)
*Giorgio Arcadi,Mattia di Mauro,Abdelhak Djouadi,Farinaldo Queiroz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的约 248 keV 高能核反冲候选事件，尝试解释为 2HD+a 模型中弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）暗物质（DM）的信号。研究发现参数空间中存在与 LZ 反冲事件相容的区域：赝标量玻色子 a 质量约 1–10 GeV，费米子暗物质质量约数百 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 实验近期报道了一个约 248 keV 的高能核反冲候选事件，可能对应长期寻找的 WIMP 暗物质信号。尽管该信号仍然较弱、尚需更多数据与检验确认，作者尝试在具体粒子物理模型框架内对其进行解释。

Method: 采用 2HD+a 模型，即在双希格斯二重态模型（2HDM）中额外引入一个轻赝标量希格斯玻色子 a 和一个同位旋单态费米子暗物质粒子 χ。工作对模型参数空间进行了广泛研究，纳入暗物质现象学与对撞机搜寻的互补约束以及精密测量约束。

Result: 模型能够重现正确的宇宙学暗物质密度，并规避此前暗物质直接与间接探测的限值，同时通过希格斯、电弱玻色子及重味部门的对撞机搜寻与高精度测量约束。参数空间中发现与 LZ 反冲事件相容的区域，其特征为较重的 2HDM 谱、非常轻的赝标量 a 玻色子（m_a ≈ 1–10 GeV）以及质量约数百 GeV 的费米子暗物质。

Conclusion: 2HD+a 模型中的费米子暗物质可在与现有各类约束一致的前提下解释 LZ 核反冲候选事件，对应轻赝标量 a 与数百 GeV 暗物质费米子的参数区间；该解释仍依赖于 LZ 信号的进一步确认。

Abstract: It is tantalizing to consider the recent observation of the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment of a high energy nuclear recoil candidate at $E_{\rm Recoil} \simeq 248$ keV as the long-awaited signal of a weakly interacting massive Dark Matter (DM) particle. Although this signal is still weak and needs to be confirmed by further data and inspection, we attempt to interpret it in the context of the 2HD+a model in which a two-Higgs doublet model (2HDM) is supplemented by a light pseudoscalar Higgs boson $a$ and an isosinglet fermionic DM particle $χ$. The model has the virtue of reproducing the correct cosmological density and evading the previous limits from direct and indirect detection of DM particles, while passing all constraints from colliders searches and high-precision measurements in the Higgs, electroweak boson and heavy-flavor sectors. We indeed find regions of the parameter space of the model, with a rather heavy 2HDM spectrum but a very light pseudoscalar $a$ boson, $m_a \approx 1-10$ GeV, as well as a DM fermion with a mass of a few hundred GeV, which are compatible with the LZ recoil event. The related extensive investigation of the parameter space of the 2HD+a model with fermionic DM, including the wide range of complementary constraints from both DM phenomenology and collider searches as well as precision measurements are presented.

</details>


### [127] [The Interplay Between Electromagnetic Fields and Baryon Stopping in a Hydrodynamic Model for Charged Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17228)
*Tuna Demircik,Dmitri E. Kharzeev,Krishna Rajagopal,Raimond Snellings*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出半解析流体力学框架，将旁观者诱导电磁场与基于Glauber的重子阻滞结合，解释STAR观测到的定向流劈裂（directed flow splitting）随中心度的符号变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷依赖定向流（charge-dependent directed flow）是探测相对论重离子碰撞早期电磁场与重子阻滞（baryon stopping）的敏感探针，而STAR最新测量显示其劈裂随中心度发生符号变化，仅靠电磁效应不足以描述该观测量，需引入重子阻滞的贡献。

Method: 构建半解析流体力学框架，将旁观者诱导电磁场与基于Glauber的重子阻滞描述相结合，背景流体动力学流采用Gubser的解析解，并假设电导率为常数。

Result: 对√s_NN=200 GeV的Au+Au碰撞，重子阻滞对定向流劈裂给出正贡献且随碰撞更边缘而减小，电磁场给出负贡献且随更边缘而增大；二者竞争自然复现Au+Au中随中心度的符号变化，并能描述50–80%中心度区间的快度依赖及U+U碰撞中的趋势。

Conclusion: 模型透明地揭示了输运重子数与旁观者诱导电磁场如何共同塑造电荷依赖定向流；但解析背景与常数电导率等简化假设限制了定量比较能力。

Abstract: Charge-dependent directed flow provides a sensitive probe of early electromagnetic fields and baryon stopping in relativistic heavy-ion collisions. Recent STAR measurements show a centrality-dependent sign change in the directed flow splitting (the difference between the directed flow of protons and antiprotons), indicating that electromagnetic effects alone are not sufficient to describe this observable and that the baryon stopping $-$ in particular the component of the stopped proton distribution that is odd in rapidity and odd under reflection in the impact parameter direction $-$ must also be included. We develop a semi-analytic hydrodynamic framework that combines spectator-induced electromagnetic fields with a Glauber-based description of baryon stopping, built upon an analytic solution for the background hydrodynamic flow due to Gubser together with the simplifying assumption of a constant electrical conductivity. For Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=200$ GeV, we find that baryon stopping gives a positive contribution to the directed flow splitting that decreases for more peripheral collisions, while electromagnetic fields give a negative contribution that is larger for more peripheral collisions. The competition between these two effects naturally reproduces the observed sign change in Au+Au collisions as a function of centrality, describes the observed rapidity dependence in the $50$--$80\%$ centrality interval, and reproduces trends seen in U+U collisions. Although the simplifying assumptions that we have made regarding the analytic background and constant conductivity limit our ability to make quantitative comparisons, our model provides a transparent explanation of how transported baryon number and spectator-induced electromagnetic fields jointly shape charge-dependent directed flow.

</details>

### [128] [Topological superconductors and Majorana fermions based on $X$-wave magnets with $X=p,d,f,g,i $](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16084)
*Motohiko Ezawa*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究超导体与 X 波磁体（X = p, d, f, g, i）的耦合，区分交错磁体（altermagnet，X = d, g, i）与奇宇称磁体（odd-parity magnet，X = p, f），并给出丰富的超导相图与拓扑分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于自旋单态 s 波超导的形成条件对磁有序宇称具有选择性，需厘清不同 X 波磁性耦合下超导态的差异及其拓扑性质。

Method: 以与 X 波磁体耦合的超导体为模型，考虑磁有序强度、化学势（chemical potential）以及在位与最近邻相互作用比值，推导超导相图并计算陈数（Chern number）与绕数（winding number）。

Result: 交错磁序稳定手性 (p_x + i p_y) 波超导，属 D 类拓扑超导体（class D topological superconductor），具马约拉纳手性边缘态（Majorana chiral edge states）；i 波交错磁体中出现两个手性边缘模，陈数为 2。p（f）波奇宇称磁序稳定 p（f）波拓扑超导体，属 DIII 类（class DIII），具马约拉纳平带（Majorana flat bands）。

Conclusion: 马约拉纳平带在 s 波超导混合存在时保持鲁棒，该混合对奇宇称磁体不可避免，尽管其幅度很小。

Abstract: We study superconductors coupled with $X$-wave magnets with $X=p,d,f,g,i$. They are essentially different between altermagnets with $X=d,g,i$ and odd-parity magnets $X=p,f$ due to the compatibility of the spin-singlet formation of the $s$-wave superconductor. The altermagnets and odd-parity magnets show rich superconducting phase diagrams depending on the strength of the magnetic order, the chemical potential and the ratio of the on-site and nearest-neighbor interactions. Especially, we find that the altermagnetic order stabilizes the chiral ($p_{x}+ip_{y}$)-wave superconductor, which is a class D topological superconductor hosting Majorana chiral edge states. It is characterized by the Chern number. Especially, there emerge two chiral edge modes in the $i$-wave altermagnet, which are characterized by the Chern number 2. On the other hand, the $p$ ($f$)-wave odd-parity magnetic order stabilizes the $p$ ($f$)-wave topological superconductor, which is a class DIII topological superconductor hosting Majorana flat bands. It is characterized by the winding number. Majorana flat bands are robust in the presence of the mixing with the $s$-wave superconductivity, which is inevitable for odd-parity magnets although the magnitude of the mixing is tiny.

</details>


### [129] [Optical and magnetic signatures of drive-enhanced coherence in phase-disordered superconducting bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16137)
*Duilio De Santis,Sambuddha Chattopadhyay,Marios H. Michael,Andrea Cavalleri,Gil Refael,Patrick A. Lee,Eugene A. Demler*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究双层超导体在周期调制超流刚度下的响应，比较层对称与层反对称两种驱动。两种驱动均增强层平均相位关联并产生相似的类超导光学电导；但仅对称驱动增强相对相位锁定与逆流磁响应，故增强的1/ω电导本身并不意味着增强磁屏蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在强亚皮秒光激发后观察到材料在其平衡转变温度以上出现类超导特征，引发了对相位无序超导体在周期调制超流刚度下行为的理论兴趣；此前分析多限于单层光学电导。

Method: 将单层研究扩展至双层系统（如 YBCO 铜氧化物），考察具有不同对称性的驱动：层对称驱动（两层同时变刚与变软）与层反对称驱动（一层变刚、另一层变软），并分析其对层平均相位关联、相对相位锁定及平行于层面磁场下逆流响应的影响。

Result: 两种驱动均增强层平均相位关联，并产生相近的类超导光学电导；然而只有对称驱动增强相对相位锁定及相应的逆流磁响应。因此，增强的1/ω光学电导本身并不蕴含增强的磁屏蔽。

Conclusion: 针对已报道类超导面内光学与瞬态抗磁性的驱动 YBCO，该结果提供了对称性判据：在相位同步机制内，实验上活跃的低频刚度调制必须包含显著的层对称分量。

Abstract: Recent experiments demonstrating superconducting-like characteristics in materials above their equilibrium transition temperatures following strong sub-picosecond optical excitation have stimulated theoretical interest in phase-disordered superconductors subject to a periodically modulated superfluid stiffness. Previous analyses of the single-layer optical conductivity have shown that such a drive can enhance the low-frequency $1/ω$ response, even when the period-averaged stiffness remains unchanged. We extend these studies to bilayer systems, such as the YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{6+x}$ (YBCO) cuprate, by considering drives with different symmetries. Specifically, we investigate a layer-symmetric drive, in which the two planes stiffen and soften simultaneously, and a layer-antisymmetric drive, in which one plane stiffens while the other softens. Both drives enhance correlations of the layer-averaged phase and generate comparable superconducting-like optical conductivities. Only the symmetric drive, however, strengthens relative-phase locking and the associated counterflow response to a magnetic field parallel to the planes. Thus, an enhanced $1/ω$ optical conductivity does not by itself imply enhanced magnetic screening. In view of applications to driven YBCO, where superconducting-like in-plane optics and transient diamagnetism have both been reported, our result provides a symmetry diagnostic: within a phase-synchronization mechanism, the experimentally active low-frequency stiffness modulation must contain a substantial layer-symmetric component.

</details>


### [130] [Mode-selective phonon effects on magnetism and superconductivity in trilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16143)
*C. Alexander Baum,Jun Zhan,Congcong Le,Ronny Thomale,Xianxin Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用从头算（ab initio）与泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group, fRG）计算，揭示三层镍酸盐（trilayer nickelates）中面外氧呼吸模（out-of-plane oxygen breathing modes）的电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）通过层间相对相位调控自旋密度波（spin-density-wave, SDW）与 $s_\pm$ 波超导，并解释磁态同位素效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层镍酸盐高温超导的关联态微观起源仍存争议，而三层镍酸盐磁性态的同位素效应（isotope effect）实验凸显晶格振动的重要作用，促使作者探究 EPC 对磁性与超导的影响。

Method: 采用从头算（ab initio）与泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group）计算，识别具有显著 EPC 的面外氧呼吸模，并构建按对称性分辨、依赖层数的耦合（symmetry-resolved, layer-dependent couplings）。

Result: 三层间相对相位产生不同延迟相互作用（retarded interactions），导致模式选择性地增强或抑制自旋密度波序与 $s_\pm$ 波超导；该选择性源于跨越多个能标的屏蔽介导自旋-声子相互作用（screening-mediated spin-phonon interplay）。所选模式给出磁态的负同位素系数，与实验定性一致。

Conclusion: 结果确立了层间声子如何改变电子驱动的有序态，并为理解三层镍酸盐中的同位素效应提供微观理论框架。

Abstract: The discovery of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in multilayer nickelates has stimulated intense interest, yet the microscopic origins of their correlated states remain debated. Recently the observed isotope effect of the magnetic state in trilayer nickelates highlights the important role of lattice vibrations in shaping these systems. Here, we investigate the impact of electron-phonon coupling (EPC) on magnetism and superconductivity in trilayer nickelates using ab initio and functional renormalization group calculations. We identify out-of-plane oxygen breathing modes with pronounced EPC and construct symmetry-resolved, layer-dependent couplings. Their relative phases across the three layers generate distinct retarded interactions, producing mode-selective enhancement or suppression of spin-density-wave order and $s_\pm$-wave superconductivity. This selectivity arises from screening-mediated spin-phonon interplay across a hierarchy of energy scales: EPC modifies charge screening at higher energies, reshapes magnetic fluctuations at lower energies, and ultimately influences pairing. Selected modes yield negative isotope coefficients for the magnetic state, qualitatively consistent with experiments. Our results establish how interlayer phonons modify electronically driven order and provide a microscopic framework for understanding isotope effects in trilayer nickelates.

</details>


### [131] [Current - voltage characteristics in magnetic field for layered superconductor with intrinsic pinning of Josephson vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16277)
*T. B. Charikova,V. N. Neverov,M. R. Popov,S. D. Popov,N. G. Shelushinina,A. A. Ivanov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在 Nd2-xCexCuO4 外延薄膜中于正交电磁场下测量霍尔构型横向电压 Uy，分析了约瑟夫森涡旋（Josephson vortex）跨 CuO2 层的运动及本征钉扎效应：其临界脱钉电流呈指数依赖，区别于阿布里科索夫涡旋（Abrikosov vortex）的幂律依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究层状高温超导体中电荷与涡旋子系统在正交电磁场下的输运行为，特别是约瑟夫森涡旋沿 c 轴跨层运动的钉扎与脱钉机制。

Method: 采用霍尔构型（Hall configuration）测量横向电压 Uy，在正交磁场与电场条件下，对 Nd2-xCexCuO4 外延薄膜中的电荷与涡旋运动进行实验观测，并分析本征钉扎（intrinsic pinning）对约瑟夫森涡旋跨 CuO2 层运动的影响。

Result: 约瑟夫森涡旋的临界脱钉电流 jc(B) 呈指数依赖，而阿布里科索夫涡旋遵循幂律依赖；随外磁场增大，在 B=Bc 发生磁场脱钉后出现涡旋蠕变（vortex creep）区，并在交叉场 B=Bf 演变为磁通流（flux-flow）区。

Conclusion: 约瑟夫森涡旋与阿布里科索夫涡旋在钉扎行为上具有本质差异，且随磁场增强可依次呈现脱钉、涡旋蠕变与磁通流三个特征区域。

Abstract: The results of measurements of the transverse voltage in the Hall configuration, Uy, under conditions of motion of the charge and vortex subsystems in orhtogonal magnetic and electric fields on epitaxial films of the layered high-temperature superconductor Nd2-xCexCuO4 are presented. the motion of Josephson vortices across CuO2 layers (along the c-axis) is analyzed considering the effects of intrinsic pinning. An exponential dependence of critical depinning current jc(B) for Josephson vortices is established in contrast to the power law dependences for Abrikosov vortices. With increasing external magnetic field after the depinning by a magnetic field at B=Bc, the onset of the vortex creep regime, which evolves into the flux-flow regime at crossover field B=Bf, is observed.

</details>


### [132] [NaFeP: first pairing prediction for an unmade 111 iron phosphide, and the pnictogen-height rule inside one compound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17028)
*Reinaldo Inácio*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究将前作（arXiv:2609.10614）建立的物理判别器（physics judge）端到端应用于唯一通过筛选的化合物 NaFeP，预测其为 s± 超导体，且内层空穴面接近节点（near-nodal）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作已构建可从结构判断超导候选体能隙对称性的判别器，并在线族（wire families）中重现已知规范结果，但尚未对通过初筛的单一化合物做端到端验证。本文旨在检验该判别器在真实候选材料上的适用性与内部一致性。

Method: 从 BaFe2As2 电子窗口内的 58 个铁砷族（iron-pnictide）组分中，采用零成本文献与稳定性筛选（literature-and-stability screen），得到唯一无体相合成、无能带/磁性/配对计算记录的 NaFeP（111 型、P4/nmm、Fe2+ d6、无砷）。判别器经显式 Stoner 门控（Stoner gate）与框架无关的对称性读取器（frame-independent symmetry reader）改进后，对 NaFeP 进行基组与晶胞扫描。

Result: 判别器给出顺磁（paramagnetic）DFT 基态与 s± 能隙：在嵌套矢量（nesting vector）(1/2,1/2,1/2) 处，Gamma 点空穴圆柱与布里渊区角电子面之间呈清晰符号反转。磷高度（1.16 Å）低于区分节点与非节点铁砷族约 1.33 Å 的经验阈值，结构规则判定为节点型（nodal）；金属基组密封判定为非节点（nodeless），而通过同样门控的 d+p 基组将内层空穴圆柱置于节点阈值（min|g|/max|g| = 0.07–0.10）。磷高度扫描在单一化合物内复现了高度规则的要素：第三个空穴口袋、嵌套矢量切换至 (1/2,1/2,0)、单调逼近 Stoner 不稳定性，以及 1.33 Å 之外 s± 与 d 通道的近似简并。

Conclusion: 作者坚持 d+p 基组读出的预测：NaFeP 为 s± 超导体，具近节点（near-nodal）内层空穴面，位于规则的 LiFeP 一侧；基组间分歧被如实报告而非以取舍方式解决。不主张 Tc 值，并预先声明判别性测量为节点/非节点判定。

Abstract: Companion to arXiv:2609.10614, which built a physics judge that classifies the gap symmetry of a superconductor candidate from its structure and showed that generation in the wire families re-finds the known canon. Here the judge is applied end to end to the single compound that survived its own screen. From 58 iron-pnictide compositions in the electronic window of BaFe$_2$As$_2$ absent from the Materials Project, a zero-cost literature-and-stability screen leaves one with no bulk synthesis and no band-structure, magnetic or pairing calculation: NaFeP (111-type, P4/nmm, Fe$^{2+}$ d$^6$, arsenic-free). The judge, refined with an explicit Stoner gate and a frame-independent symmetry reader, returns a paramagnetic DFT ground state and an s$\pm$ gap: a clean sign reversal between the hole cylinders at Gamma and the electron sheets at the zone corner, at nesting vector (1/2,1/2,1/2), in every basis and cell tried. Whether the gap is nodeless is where the instrument is tested. The phosphorus height (1.16 Å) lies below the empirical threshold of about 1.33 Åseparating nodal from nodeless pnictides, so the structural rule calls NaFeP nodal. The sealed verdict on a metal-only basis said nodeless; a d+p basis that passes the same gates puts the inner hole cylinder at the node threshold (min|g|/max|g| = 0.07-0.10), and a scan of the phosphorus height reproduces, inside one compound, the ingredients of the height rule: a third hole pocket, a switch of the nesting vector to (1/2,1/2,0), a monotonic approach to the Stoner instability, and near-degeneracy of s$\pm$ and d channels beyond 1.33 Å. The prediction we stand behind is the d+p reading: an s$\pm$ superconductor with a near-nodal inner hole sheet, on the LiFeP side of the rule, with the disagreement between bases reported, not resolved by choice. No Tc is asserted; the discriminating measurement, nodal or nodeless, is stated in advance.

</details>


### [133] [Interplay of dimerization and quasiperiodicity in the superconducting proximity effect of a one-dimensional hybrid ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17069)
*Sourav Karmakar,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 采用自洽 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 形式，研究由自旋单态超导体与三种晶格模型描述的正常区构成的杂化环中近邻诱导配对振幅的空间变化，揭示准周期性与二聚化对诱导超导的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于准晶与拓扑体系中超导近邻效应（superconducting proximity effect）的研究，为探索准周期性（quasiperiodicity）与拓扑如何影响近邻诱导超导开辟了新方向，本文旨在刻画该诱导配对振幅的空间分布规律。

Method: 构建自旋单态超导体与正常区组成的杂化环（hybrid ring），正常区分别由对角 Aubry-André-Harper（AAH）模型、Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链及二者结合的 SSH-AAH 模型描述，并用自洽 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 形式（self-consistent Bogoliubov-de Gennes formalism）求解。

Result: AAH 情形下，准周期势增强使诱导序参量空间涨落加剧、幅值逐渐被抑制，越过局域化转变（localization transition）后配对因电子态局域化而显著减弱；SSH 情形下，弱二聚化引入深入正常区的振荡调制，强二聚化则限制振荡并大幅降低超导关联的穿透；SSH-AAH 联合模型中，SSH 二聚化决定振荡行为与穿透深度，AAH 势增强空间不均匀性并进一步降低幅值。

Conclusion: 二聚化与准周期势两种效应为在准周期杂化体系中调控近邻诱导超导提供了多样化手段。

Abstract: Recent studies of the superconducting proximity effect in quasicrystalline and topological systems have opened up a new research direction for exploring how quasiperiodicity and topology influence proximity induced superconductivity. In this work, we investigate spatial variation of the proximity induced pairing amplitude in a hybrid ring composed of a spin singlet superconductor and a normal region described by three different lattice models using the self-consistent Bogoliubov-de Gennes formalism. We first consider the normal region described by the diagonal Aubry-André-Harper (AAH) model. Increasing the quasiperiodic potential enhances spatial fluctuations in the induced order parameter, while progressively suppressing its magnitude in the normal region. Beyond the localization transition, proximity-induced pairing is strongly diminished due to the localized nature of the underlying electronic states. The normal region is then modeled by the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain to investigate the effect of hopping dimerization. Weak dimerization introduces oscillatory modulations in the induced pairing that extend deep into the normal region, whereas strong dimerization confines these oscillations and significantly reduces the penetration of superconducting correlations. Finally, we study the combined SSH-AAH model to explore the interplay between the dimerized hopping and quasiperiodicity. The results show that the SSH dimerization determines the oscillatory behavior and penetration of the induced pairing, while the AAH potential enhances spatial inhomogeneity and further reduces its magnitude. Together, these two effects provide a versatile means of controlling proximity-induced superconductivity in quasiperiodic hybrid systems.

</details>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Probabilistic, Structure-Intrinsic Clustering with an Hierarchical Embedding (PSICHE) in Time and Space Applied In Particle Physics Jet Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16276)
*Margaret Lazarovits,Christopher Rogan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 论文提出一种新的喷注（jet）聚类算法 PSICHE，在自洽的概率框架下实现动态可变簇尺寸、无监督多尺度涌现特征学习、时空联合聚类以及领域特定实验不确定性的纳入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注重建（jet reconstruction）在粒子物理中仍是开放问题，涉及喷注尺寸、多重数、子结构以及堆积（pileup）与噪声下的实验表现；类似挑战也普遍存在于无监督学习中的聚类与关联任务。现有喷注聚类方法存在若干不足，需要新算法加以应对。

Method: 提出 PSICHE（Probabilistic, Structure-Intrinsic Clustering with an Hierarchical Embedding），即带层次嵌入的概率化、结构内蕴聚类算法。其特点包括：（i）动态学习且可变的喷注（簇）尺寸；（ii）对喷注子结构与多重数等涌现特征进行无监督、多尺度学习；（iii）在空间与时间上进行聚类；（iv）纳入领域特定的实验不确定性；上述性质均在计算可行的自洽概率框架内实现。

Result: 论文展示了该算法在上述特性方面的验证，覆盖 LHC 场景下多种物理现象、多种聚类输入、不同堆积条件以及不同探测器性能参数的喷注聚类示例。

Conclusion: PSICHE 以自洽、概率化且计算可行的框架，针对现有喷注聚类方法的若干挑战与不足提出改进，并在 LHC 相关多场景示例中展示了其特性。

Abstract: Jet reconstruction is an active and open area of particle physics research, with questions related to jet size, multiplicity, substructure, and experimental performance in the presence of pileup and noise. Analogous challenges are ubiquitous throughout other applications of unsupervised learning in the context of clustering and association. This paper introduces a new algorithm for clustering in the context of jet reconstruction: Probabilistic, Structure-Intrinsic Clustering with an Hierarchical Embedding (PSICHE). PSICHE addresses several challenges and shortcomings of existing jet clustering methods with novel features, including but not limited to: (i) dynamically-learned and variable jet (cluster) sizes, (ii) unsupervised, multi-scale learning of emergent features, like jet substructure and multiplicity, (iii) clustering in space and time, and (iv) incorporation of domain-specific experimental uncertainties. All of these properties are achieved in a self-consistent, probabilistic framework that is computationally tractable. Furthermore, demonstrations of these features are presented for a range of jet clustering examples in LHC scenarios for various physics phenomena, clustering inputs, pileup conditions, and detector performance parameters.

</details>

J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用 BESIII 探测器在 BEPCII 对撞机上采集的 4.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 对撞数据，首次搜寻 Cabibbo 压低的半轻子衰变 Λ_c⁺→pπ⁻e⁺ν_e，测得分支比并给出 4.2σ 的信号显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作针对 Cabibbo 压低（Cabbibo-suppressed）的半轻子衰变 Λ_c⁺→pπ⁻e⁺ν_e 开展首次搜寻（first search），以补充该衰变道的实验信息。

Method: 基于 BESIII 探测器在 BEPCII 对撞机上采集的 4.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 对撞数据，质心能量（center-of-mass energy）范围 4.600 GeV 至 4.699 GeV，对该衰变道进行搜寻与分支比测量。

Result: Λ_c⁺→pπ⁻e⁺ν_e 的分支比（branching fraction）测量值为 (2.96±0.95_stat±0.23_syst)×10⁻⁴，信号显著性（signal significance）为 4.2σ。

Conclusion: 在所用数据范围内完成了该 Cabibbo 压低半轻子衰变道的首次搜寻，并给出了分支比测量值及其统计与系统不确定度。

Abstract: Based on $4.5\, \mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider at center-of-mass energies between $4.600\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and $4.699\,\mathrm{GeV}$, the first search for the Cabbibo-suppressed semileptonic decay $Λ_c^+\to pπ^-e^+ν_e$ is performed. The branching fraction of $Λ_c^+\to pπ^-e^+ν_e$ is measured to be $(2.96\pm0.95_{\rm stat}\pm0.23_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$ with a signal significance of $4.2σ$.

</details>


### [136] [Transverse momentum and azimuthal angle distributions of hadrons in DIS at COMPASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17222)
*Vendula Benešová*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: COMPASS 实验利用 2016–2017 年非极化液氢靶与纵向极化缪子束数据，分析半单包容深度非弹性散射，提取强子多重数与方位角不对称性，用于研究核子结构与横向动量依赖部分子分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMPASS experiment 在核子结构（nucleon structure）研究中具有重要地位，而 2016–2017 年采集的高统计量数据为获取横向动量依赖部分子分布（transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution）与碎裂函数（fragmentation functions）提供了契机。

Method: 采用非极化液氢靶（unpolarised liquid hydrogen target）与纵向极化 160 GeV/c 缪子束，通过半单包容深度非弹性散射（semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering）分析 2016 年数据集，确定以 x、Q² 和 P_T 微分的强子多重数及方位角调制振幅。

Result: 提取了非极化不对称性 A^{cos φ_h}_UU（与 Cahn effect 相关）和 A^{cos 2φ_h}_UU（对 Boer–Mulders function 敏感），以及束自旋不对称性（beam-spin asymmetry）A^{sin φ_h}_LU；这些量在与多重数匹配的四维分箱中给出，并对部分变量做了积分。

Conclusion: 该分析基于 2016 年数据给出了强子多重数与方位角不对称性的系统测量结果，为理解核子结构及横向动量依赖的部分子分布与碎裂函数提供了实验依据。

Abstract: The COMPASS experiment plays an important role in studies of nucleon structure. Measurements performed in 2016-2017 with an unpolarised liquid hydrogen target and a longitudinally polarised 160 GeV/$c$ muon beam provide a high-statistics data sample for the analysis of semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, giving access to transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution and fragmentation functions. The 2016 data set was analysed to determine the multiplicities of hadrons produced in DIS differential in $x$, $Q^2$ and $P_\text{T}$, as well as the amplitudes of azimuthal modulations in the hadron azimuthal-angle distributions. In particular, the unpolarised asymmetries $A^{\cos φ_\text{h}}_\text{UU}$ (related to the Cahn effect), $A^{\cos 2φ_\text{h}}_\text{UU}$ (sensitive to the Boer--Mulders function), and the beam-spin asymmetry $A^{\sin φ_\text{h}}_\text{LU}$ were extracted in 4-dimensional bins matching those of the multiplicities, and also integrated over some of the variables.

</details>
