# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [A complete classification of the existence of finite-dimensional quantum solutions to inconsistent linear constraint systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16490)
*Markus Frembs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究线性约束系统（linear constraint systems, LCS）在奇素数模下的量子—经典间隙问题，首先论证张量积量子解中经典障碍按因子分解，从而排除仅靠张量拼接产生的真全局障碍；进而将 LCS 与秩一投影算子（rank-one projector）安排及单位分解（resolution of the identity）关联，把量子可解性变为自动成立、把间隙归约为经典不可满足性，并给出完整存在性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制情形（如 Mermin–Peres 正方形）中，不一致的 LCS 可借提升变量为局部 Pauli 算子求解，但奇素数模下一系列 no-go 结果排除了 Pauli、Clifford 及单项式酉算子（monomial unitary）构造。作者意图厘清二进制与奇素数模差异的根源，并探究是否存在超越二进制情形的量子—经典间隙，以正面解决其存在性问题。

Method: 方法分两层：其一，分析张量积量子解，指出 Z_n 上 LCS 经典解的障碍可分解为由各因子承载的障碍类，故全局障碍不能仅由局部无阻碍系统的张量积产生，并说明该论证依赖奇模（偶模下重排相位（reordering phase）不一定消失）。其二，将 LCS 关联到有限维空间中的有限秩一投影算子安排及单位分解，并借组值框架函数（group-valued frame functions）建立联系，识别出 Harding、Jager 与 Smith 工作中的一类例子；对 d=n 为素数的情形构造显式例子。

Result: 证明了对所有维度 d 与所有 n∈N，Z_n 上是否存在具有各维量子解而经典不可满足的 LCS 的问题；给出该类系统的存在性刻画：此类系统存在当且仅当 d≥3 且 gcd(n,d)>1。在有限域（finite fields）情形，存在具有量子—经典间隙的 LCS 当且仅当 d≥3 且 p∣d。

Conclusion: 论文正面解决了二进制情形之外 LCS 的存在性问题，给出 Z_n 与有限域上量子—经典间隙的充要条件，显示了量子可解性在投影算子安排框架下自动成立、间隙完全取决于模关联问题的经典不可满足性。该结论将二进制特例推广到一般模与维度，并明确了奇偶模差异的代数根源。

Abstract: Linear constraint systems (LCS) provide a compact algebraic language for nonlocal games and state-independent contextuality. In the binary case the Mermin--Peres square shows that inconsistent LCS can be solved by promoting variables to local Pauli operators, whereas a series of no-go results excludes analogous Pauli-, Clifford- and monomial unitary-based constructions at odd prime modulus. We first isolate the reason for this difference: for tensor-product quantum solutions, the obstruction to a classical solution of a LCS over $\mathbb{Z}_n$ decomposes into obstruction classes carried by the individual factors. As a consequence, a genuinely global obstruction cannot arise solely by tensoring locally unobstructed systems. The argument is specific to odd modulus; at even modulus the reordering phase need not vanish, as in Pauli-based examples.
  Given this distinction, we instead associate LCS to finite arrangements of rank-one projectors and resolutions of the identity in finite-dimensional vector spaces. Quantum solvability for such LCS is automatic and the search for a quantum-classical gap rests entirely on proving classical unsatisfiability of the underlying modular incidence problem of the arrangement. By relating such LCS with group-valued frame functions, we identify a family of examples in the work of [Harding, Jager, and Smith, Int. J. Theor. Phys. 44, 539 (2005)]. To establish the (non)existence of classically unsatisfiable LCS over $\mathbb{Z}_n$ with quantum solutions in all dimensions $d$ and for all $n\in\mathbb{N}$, we further construct explicit examples for the cases $d=n$ prime. Our main result thus positively resolves the existence problem of such LCS beyond the binary case: such systems exist if and only if $d\geq 3$ and $\gcd(n,d)>1$. Moreover, we establish the existence of LCS with a quantum-classical gap over finite fields if and only if $d\geq 3$ and $p\mid d$.

</details>


### [138] [Exact Ionization Amplitudes for a Delta-Function Well in an Arbitrary-Strength DC Electric Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16507)
*Ilki Kim,Gerald J. Iafrate*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究一维吸引δ势阱在任意强度均匀直流电场中的有限时间场致电离（field-induced ionization），目标是在不构造完整含时传播子或波函数的前提下确定束缚态存活振幅\(a_b(t)\)。作者在规范等价的Kramers–Henneberger表象（Kramers–Henneberger representation）中将问题化为闭合Volterra方程，并通过精确的端点相位因子化与相对时间卷积层级，得到任意直流场强与有限时间下的精确非微扰解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统场致电离分析通常依赖弱场展开、重散射截断或渐近时间近似，或需要构造完整含时传播子/波函数，难以兼顾任意场强与有限时间的精确描述。该文旨在直接获取任意观测时刻的束缚态存活振幅\(a_b(t)\)，避免上述近似与整体求解的复杂性。

Method: 在Kramers–Henneberger表象中，电场表现为接触点运动，动力学约化为闭合Volterra方程；利用精确端点相位因子化（endpoint-phase factorization）将完整时间顺序重散射历史组织为相对时间卷积层级与精确预解式（resolvent）。引入累积束缚态振幅并在相对时间与互补中心时间域重构两时间结构，使最终接触贡献化为显式边界积分；所有空间积分解析完成。

Result: 场驱动无接触项以Faddeeva函数闭式给出，直接与重复重散射项表示为可显式求值的时间积分。结果适用于任意直流场强与有限时间，不含弱场展开、重散射截断或渐近时间近似；并通过精确无场极限与原始物理Volterra方程的独立数值解进行验证。

Conclusion: 该形式为一基本电离模型提供了精确非微扰解，并揭示了受驱量子动力学（driven quantum dynamics）中此前隐藏的解析结构。

Abstract: We investigate finite-time field-induced ionization from a one-dimensional attractive delta-function well in a uniform dc electric field of arbitrary strength. Our aim is to determine the physical bound-state survival amplitude \(a_b(t)\) at any observation time without constructing the complete time-dependent propagator or wavefunction. In the gauge-equivalent Kramers--Henneberger representation, the field appears as motion of the contact point, and the resulting dynamics reduces to a closed Volterra equation. Exact endpoint-phase factorization organizes the full chronological rescattering history into a relative-time convolution hierarchy and an exact resolvent. By introducing the accumulated bound-state amplitude and reorganizing its two-time domain in relative and complementary center times, the final contact contribution becomes an explicit boundary integral. All spatial integrations are carried out analytically. The field-driven contact-free term is obtained in closed form using the Faddeeva function, while the direct and repeated-rescattering terms are expressed as explicitly evaluable time integrals. The result applies at arbitrary dc-field strength and finite time, with no weak-field expansion, rescattering truncation, or asymptotic-time approximation. It is verified through the exact field-free limit and an independent numerical solution of the original physical Volterra equation. The formulation gives an exact nonperturbative solution of a fundamental ionization model and reveals otherwise hidden analytical structure in driven quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [139] [TSS Graphs for Hadamard Matrices: Real vs Complex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16813)
*Wesley Lewis,Darsh Pareek,Ravi Janjam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究观察到实或复阿达玛矩阵（Hadamard matrices）对任意单一输入态均产生相同概率的稠密输出，并发现对输入态作叠加组合时可生成非均匀概率，从而在无需类似 Grover 算子手动参数化的情况下实现振幅放大（amplitude amplification）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索阿达玛矩阵在量子算法（quantum algorithms）中的适用性，尤其是复阿达玛矩阵的特殊阶数（unusual orders）与相位（phase）在受控相位变换门中的潜在价值，并寻求免手动参数化的振幅放大途径。

Method: 采用幺正矩阵方法（unitary matrix approach）研究阿达玛矩阵；通过构造输入态的叠加组合（superposed combinations）分析概率分布；并引入多种图论性质（graph theoretic properties）刻画叠加拓扑结构（Topological Structure of Superpositions, TSS）图及其趋势。

Result: 实或复阿达玛矩阵对单一输入态输出相同概率的稠密结果；复阿达玛矩阵具有不寻常阶数并引入相位，在受控相位变换门中有用；输入态叠加组合可产生非均匀概率，利于振幅放大；TSS 图呈现出可探索的趋势，且对相同输入态集合近乎同构（nearly isomorphic）。

Conclusion: 阿达玛矩阵可为免手动参数化的振幅放大及量子算法设计提供依据；TSS 图的近似同构性质对量子算法（quantum algorithms）的开发具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We have observed that Hadamard matrices whether real or complex lead to a dense output of identical probabilities for any given single input state. Complex Hadamard matrices have unusual orders and introduce phase which prove useful when controlled phase transformation gates are used. By taking a superposed combinations of input states, we discovered that the Hadamards generate non-uniform probabilities which is practically significant towards amplitude amplification without the need for manual parameterization like Grover's operator~\cite{grover1996}. A variety of graph theoretic properties are applied to TSS graphs and their trends were explored. Additionally, graphs turn out to be nearly isomorphic for the same set of input states which has potential applications for developing Quantum Algorithms.
  Keywords: Quantum Algorithms, Hadamard Matrices, Unitary Matrix Approach, Topological Structure of Superpositions (TSS), Graph Theory

</details>


### [140] [Strictly Localized Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17013)
*Sigurd Sørlie Rustad,Jan Gulla,Johannes Skaar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子场论（quantum field theory）中的严格局域混合态（strictly localized mixed states），即在其时空区域外与真空不可区分的态。作者分析此类态的生成机制、纯态分解性质，并将 Knight 定理推广至混合态情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域测量在量子场论中产生的态是否具有严格局域性尚需澄清，尤其是非选择性测量结果与混合态的可分解性缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用 Licht 映射（Licht maps）从真空生成严格局域态，考察局域投影测量（projective measurements）选择性与非选择性结果的局域性质，并分析非锐利局域测量（unsharp local measurements）产生的态，最后推广 Knight 定理。

Result: 局域投影测量单次结果对应的态并非严格局域，但非选择性态是严格局域的，且可分解为严格局域纯态；然而存在无法写成同一区域严格局域纯态混合的严格局域混合态，此类态可源自非锐利局域测量。

Conclusion: 推广后的 Knight 定理表明，严格局域玻色子混合态（bosonic mixed states）包含粒子数任意高的项。

Abstract: We study strictly localized mixed states in quantum field theory: states that are indistinguishable from vacuum outside some spacetime region. Such states are generated from the vacuum by so-called Licht maps. While the states associated with individual outcomes of local, projective measurements on the vacuum are not strictly localized, the corresponding non-selective states are strictly localized and even admit decompositions into strictly localized pure states. Remarkably, however, there are strictly localized mixed states that cannot be written as mixtures of pure states strictly localized to the same region. Such states can arise from unsharp local measurements. Finally, we extend Knight's theorem to mixed states, showing that strictly localized bosonic mixed states contain terms with arbitrarily high particle numbers.

</details>


### [141] [Generalised quantum Stein's lemma more robust than ever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17309)
*Filippo Girardi,Kuan-Yi Lee,Masahito Hayashi,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作建立了广义量子斯坦引理（generalised quantum Stein's lemma）的一个更简洁、更普适的版本，适用于在归一化一阶量子 Wasserstein 距离（normalised quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1）意义下渐近接近独立同分布（i.i.d.）态的任意源，并据此求解了零假设任意变化情形的斯坦指数（Stein exponent）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义量子斯坦引理连接量子假设检验（quantum hypothesis testing）与量子资源操控（quantum resource manipulation），但既有证明局限于 i.i.d. 序列或按 Mazzola、Sutter 与 Renner 的严格定义下"接近" i.i.d. 的源。许多应用需要更一般的源，故需推广该引理。

Method: 以归一化一阶量子 Wasserstein 距离刻画源与 i.i.d. 态之间的渐近接近性，建立适用于此类一般源的广义量子斯坦引理版本；该表述在概念上更为简单且更具一般性。

Result: 得到适用于渐近接近 i.i.d. 态的任意源的广义量子斯坦引理；作为直接推论，求解了零假设（null hypothesis）任意变化（arbitrarily varying）情形的斯坦指数，并用零假设基集的凸包（convex hull）中任意态所对应的 i.i.d. 斯坦指数予以表达。

Conclusion: 所提引理版本在概念上更简洁、适用源范围更广，并将任意变化零假设的斯坦指数归约为凸包内各态的 i.i.d. 斯坦指数，拓展了该基本结果在量子信息处理与资源理论中的应用范围。

Abstract: The generalised quantum Stein's lemma is a key result in quantum hypothesis testing, and connects this fundamental primitive of quantum information processing with quantum resource manipulation, a task that is central for technological applications. Prior works have proved this statement in the idealised setting of independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) sequences of quantum states, and recent extensions consider also sources that are 'close' to i.i.d., according to the strict notion put forth by Mazzola, Sutter, and Renner. For several applications, however, one would need to consider yet more general sources. We establish a version of the generalised quantum Stein's lemma that is conceptually much simpler and general, as it applies to any source that is asymptotically close to an i.i.d. state with respect to the normalised quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1. As an immediate consequence, we solve the Stein exponent of a scenario where the null hypothesis is arbitrarily varying, expressing it in terms of i.i.d. Stein exponents corresponding to arbitrary states in the convex hull of the null hypothesis base set.

</details>


### [142] [Optimal Linear-Rate Conversion of Unknown Mixed Qubit States via SWAP Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17311)
*Sujay Kazi,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过消耗未知量子比特态的多个副本，可在保持布洛赫矢量方向不变的前提下改变其纯度；论文确定了不同纯度量子比特态之间相互转换的最大线性速率，并给出以复数右对数导数（RLD）Fisher信息矩阵特征值表达的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知量子比特态的纯度与方向在资源转换中具有不同地位，需明确在允许随副本数增加而消失的误差下，不同纯度态之间转换的极限速率。该问题也为复数RLD Fisher信息矩阵（complex right-logarithmic-derivative Fisher information matrix）提供操作性解释。

Method: 以多个未知量子比特态副本为资源，允许由迹距离（trace distance）等量化的非零误差在无穷多副本极限下趋于零，求解纯度转换的最大线性速率；并将最优速率与量子比特态SU(2)旋转对应的复数RLD Fisher信息矩阵的两个特征值相联系。

Result: 当输出纯度提高（浓缩，concentration）时，最优速率由输入与输出RLD矩阵最大特征值之比给出；当输出纯度降低（稀释，dilution）时，最优速率由两者最小特征值之比给出。浓缩与稀释均可以SWAP检验（SWAP test）作为唯一非平凡双量子比特测量原语，配合初态为最大混态的辅助量子比特实现，无需额外双量子比特门。

Conclusion: 研究为完整的复数RLD Fisher信息矩阵提供了新的操作性解释：其反对称的纯虚部编码了超出密度算子统计距离的几何信息，并在决定最优态转换速率中起关键作用。

Abstract: By consuming multiple copies of an unknown qubit state, one can modify its purity while preserving the direction of its Bloch vector. We determine the maximum linear rate at which qubit states of different purities can be interconverted, allowing a nonzero error, quantified, for instance, by the trace distance, provided that it vanishes in the limit of infinitely many copies. Interestingly, the optimal conversion rate is determined by the two eigenvalues of the complex right-logarithmic-derivative (RLD) Fisher information matrix associated with $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ rotations of the qubit state. When the output qubits have higher purity, corresponding to concentration, the optimal rate is given by the ratio of the maximum eigenvalues of the input and output RLD matrices. In contrast, when the output qubits have lower purity, corresponding to dilution, the optimal rate is given by the ratio of their minimum eigenvalues. Remarkably, both concentration and dilution can be implemented using SWAP tests as the only nontrivial two-qubit measurement primitive, together with ancillary qubits initially prepared in maximally mixed states, without requiring any additional two-qubit gates. Our work thus provides a novel operational interpretation of the full complex RLD Fisher information matrix. Crucially, its antisymmetric, purely imaginary part encodes geometric information beyond the statistical distance between density operators and plays an essential role in determining the optimal state-conversion rates.

</details>


### [143] [Evolution of quantum imaginarity in black hole quantum atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17314)
*Ruopu Sun,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究静态施瓦茨希尔德黑洞量子大气中双体狄拉克场约化态的量子虚数性（quantum imaginarity），揭示霍金热辐射对三种主流虚数性度量的调制规律及其径向分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子虚数性作为量化量子态内禀非实相干性的关键资源度量，在弯曲时空背景下呈现非平凡演化行为，但其在黑洞量子大气中的具体调制机制尚待阐明。

Method: 以静态施瓦茨希尔德黑洞量子大气中的双体狄拉克场约化态为研究对象，考察相对熵虚数性（relative-entropy imaginarity）、几何虚数性（geometric imaginarity）与虚数性鲁棒性（robustness of imaginarity）三种度量在霍金热辐射下的变化。

Result: 完全可及态（fully accessible state）的虚数性呈谷形径向分布，在量子大气内部存在局部极小值；交叉耦合态（cross-coupling state）与完全不可及态（fully inaccessible state）则呈相反的峰形趋势。Hartle-Hawking 常数显著增强虚数性在两区域的再分配效应，而事件视界半径增大会削弱该再分配并平缓极值特征。

Conclusion: 研究结果为解读黑洞量子大气的信息结构及霍金辐射的内禀量子本质提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum imaginarity, as a critical resource metric for quantifying intrinsic nonreal coherence encoded in quantum states, exhibits nontrivial evolutionary behaviors in curved spacetime backgrounds. This work focuses on bipartite Dirac field reduced states in the quantum atmosphere of a static Schwarzschild black hole, aiming to explore the modulation of three mainstream imaginarity measures by Hawking thermal radiation. We reveal that the relative-entropy imaginarity, the geometric imaginarity and robustness of imaginarity, the fully accessible state presents a valley-shaped radial profile with a local minimum inside the quantum atmosphere, while the cross-coupling and fully inaccessible states follow opposite peak-shaped trends. Further analysis demonstrates that the Hartle-Hawking constant significantly strengthens the redistribution effect of imaginarity across both regions, whereas an increase in the event horizon radius weakens such redistribution and flattens the extremum features. These findings offer a new perspective for decoding the information structure of black hole quantum atmospheres and the intrinsic quantum nature of Hawking radiation.

</details>


### [144] [Designs without disorder: unitary $k$-designs from a single-site bulk defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16140)
*Samudra Sur,Pratik Nandy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在可积（integrable）量子多体系统中，单个 O(1) 局域缺陷（local defect）即可生成近似酉 k-设计（approximate unitary k-designs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究可积系统本身缺乏设计生成能力的情况下，最小规模的局域破坏（defect）是否足以驱动近似 k-设计的形成，并厘清其与混沌（chaotic）和可积极限的关系。

Method: 采用带有时间采样（temporal sampling）的双泡利踢（two-Pauli-kick, 2PK）协议，在确定性 XXZ 自旋链（XXZ spin chains）的固定磁化（fixed-magnetization）子空间中引入单一可积性破坏体缺陷；推导泡利踢的解析选择定则（selection rule），并对所有可容许泡利字符串（Pauli strings）进行穷举搜索。

Result: 选择定则表明，由耦合常数确定的、具有特定畴壁数（domain-wall count）的对称保持字符串主导设计形成；穷举搜索在混沌区间找到一大类可生成设计的踢，而在可积极限（含边界缺陷）中不存在此类踢；弱体缺陷（weak bulk defects）随系统尺寸增大仍保持该机制有效。

Conclusion: O(1) 局域缺陷足以从可积多体系统生成近似酉 k-设计，该机制在混沌区间成立、在可积极限消失，且对弱体缺陷在大尺寸下保持稳健。

Abstract: We show that an $O(1)$ local defect is sufficient to generate approximate unitary $k$-designs from an otherwise integrable quantum many-body system. Using a two-Pauli-kick (2PK) protocol with temporal sampling, we demonstrate this mechanism in a fixed-magnetization sector of deterministic XXZ spin chains with a single integrability-breaking bulk defect. We derive an analytical selection rule for the Pauli kicks and show that symmetry-preserving strings with a specific domain-wall count, fixed by the couplings, govern design formation. An exhaustive search over all admissible Pauli strings identifies a broad family of kicks that produce designs in the chaotic regime, whereas no such kicks exist in the integrable limit, including for boundary defects. We further show that the design-forming mechanism remains effective for weak bulk defects as the system size increases.

</details>


### [145] [One Gate at a Time: Complexity Growth in Random Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17457)
*Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 随机酉量子电路（random unitary quantum circuit）的常数误差电路复杂度（circuit complexity）随时间近乎线性增长，达到 Ω(T/log T)，且该界在全部 2≤T≤4^n 范围内成立，不引入额外 n 依赖，较以往谱隙与酉设计（unitary design）所得下界提升 poly(n) 因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机酉电路被认为在指数长时间内不可压缩，然而此前复杂度下界多依赖谱隙（spectral gap）与酉设计，结果中包含额外 n 依赖，限制了下界的强度。本文旨在获得更紧的时间增长下界。

Method: 该工作借鉴随机微积分（stochastic calculus）、几何泛函分析（geometric functional analysis）与随机线性代数（randomized linear algebra）的思想，通过分析电路对单个门（gate）扰动的响应来建立下界，且无需控制电路收敛到高阶酉设计。

Result: 证明随机酉电路的常数误差复杂度满足 Ω(T/log T)，对全部 2≤T≤4^n 成立（n 为系统规模），且不含其他 n 依赖；该界较谱隙与酉设计导出的既有下界改进了 poly(n) 因子。

Conclusion: 结果表明随机酉电路在极长时间尺度上仍保持近乎线性的复杂度增长，为随机电路的不可压缩性提供了更强证据，并展示了对门级扰动响应分析这一方法的有效性。

Abstract: A random unitary quantum circuit is expected to be incompressible for exponentially long times. We show that the constant-error circuit complexity of a random unitary circuit grows almost linearly with time as $Ω(T/\log T)$. The bound holds for all $2\leq T\leq 4^n$ where $n$ is the system size, and involves no other $n$-dependence. This improves previous lower bounds derived from spectral gaps and unitary designs by a factor of $\mathrm{poly}(n)$. Drawing on insights from stochastic calculus, geometric functional analysis, and randomized linear algebra, our approach exploits the circuit's response to variations of individual gates and requires no control over convergence to high-order unitary designs.

</details>


### [146] [Regularized barycentric Rényi divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17517)
*Milán Mosonyi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了：对任意选取的可加（additive）且单调（monotone）的量子相对熵（quantum relative entropy），其正则化重心 Rényi 散度（regularized barycentric Rényi divergence）在严格正输入上与最小重心 Rényi 散度重合，从而说明唯一可加的重心 Rényi 散度即为最小者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重心 Rényi 散度（barycentric Rényi divergences）作为标准 Kubo-Ando 构造的替代方案，用于定义多变量量子 Rényi 散度，其可加性此前仅在全部 D^{q_x} 取 Umegaki 相对熵时得到确立，且该情形也是唯一具有显式表达式的情形。本文旨在解决一般情形下的可加性问题。

Method: 通过研究任意选取的可加且单调的量子相对熵所对应的正则化重心 Rényi 散度，将其与最小重心 Rényi 散度（minimal barycentric Rényi divergence）在严格正输入（strictly positive inputs）上进行比较。

Result: 对于任意可加且单调的量子相对熵的选择，正则化重心 Rényi 散度在严格正输入上与最小重心 Rényi 散度一致；由此推出唯一可加的重心 Rényi 散度就是最小重心 Rényi 散度。

Conclusion: 本文解决了重心 Rényi 散度的可加性问题，表明在可加且单调的相对熵选取下，正则化后的结果唯一地退化为最小重心 Rényi 散度，从而排除了其他可加构造的可能性。

Abstract: Barycentric Rényi divergences were introduced in [Mosonyi, Bunth, Vrana, Linear Algebra and its Applications, 2024] as an alternative to standard Kubo-Ando constructions to define multivariate quantum Rényi divergences. They are defined via a variational expression and depend on a finite collection of quantum relative entropies $D^{q_x}$. When all the relative entropies are monotone under CPTP maps then so are the corresponding barycentric Rényi divergences, and when all the relative entropies are additive then the corresponding barycentric Rényi divergences are subadditive under tensor product. Additivity has only been established before for the case where all $D^{q_x}$ are chosen to be the Umegaki relative entropy, which is also the only case where the barycentric Rényi divergence (called the minimal one) admits an explicit expression.
  Here we settle the problem of additivity by showing that for any choice of additive and monotone quantum relative entropies, the regularized barycentric Rényi divergence coincides with the minimal barycentric Rényi divergence on strictly positive inputs. This in turn implies that the only additive barycentric Rényi divergence is the minimal one.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum synchronization: A tutorial guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14375)
*Sourabh Lahiri,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教程式综述（tutorial review），系统构建了分析经典与量子动力系统中同步（synchronization）现象的统一框架，从振幅-相位动力学出发，逐步过渡到量子极限环振荡器（quantum limit-cycle oscillator）的Lindblad主方程与相空间描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统中同步的描述需要将极限环振荡器的经典动力学与量子涨落（quantum fluctuations）、耗散（dissipation）及相位概念相连接，而现有知识分散。本文旨在提供自包含的教程式框架，供学生学习基本概念、供研究者获取分析工具与方法论。

Method: 从振幅-相位动力学（amplitude-phase dynamics）出发，依次引入锁相（phase locking）、Adler方程、Arnold舌（Arnold tongues）与Kuramoto模型，用于分析耦合振子及其稳定性；随后用Lindblad主方程（Lindblad master equations）与相空间方法（phase-space methods）建立量子极限环振荡器的对应描述，并给出两个及多个耦合振子的实例。

Result: 形成了一套可识别同步态、判定其稳定性并刻画量子系统中同步的解析方法学，涵盖噪声（noise）、同步度量（synchronization measures）以及极端量子区域（extreme quantum regime），包括自旋系统（spin systems）中的同步。

Conclusion: 本综述作为自包含指南，强调解析推导与实用方法论，帮助读者识别同步态、确定其稳定性并刻画量子同步，为处理新的同步问题提供概念工具与方法洞见。

Abstract: Synchronization is a collective phenomenon in which interacting nonlinear oscillators develop a persistent relation between their phases and frequencies. In quantum systems, its description requires connecting the classical dynamics of limit-cycle oscillators with quantum fluctuations, dissipation, and the notion of phase. In this tutorial review, we offer a systematic framework for analyzing synchronization in classical and quantum dynamical systems. Starting from amplitude-phase dynamics, we introduce successively topics of relevance, such as phase locking, the Adler equation, Arnold tongues, and the Kuramoto model, and use these concepts to analyze coupled oscillators and their stability. We then formulate the corresponding description for quantum limit-cycle oscillators using Lindblad master equations and phase-space methods, with explicit examples of two and many coupled oscillators. We further discuss noise, synchronization measures, and the extreme quantum regime, including synchronization in spin systems. The emphasis throughout is on analytical derivations and a practical methodology for identifying synchronized states, determining their stability, and characterizing synchronization in quantum systems. The review is designed as a self-contained guide from which we hope that students can learn the essential concepts and techniques, while researchers can readily find the analytical tools and methodological insights needed to approach new synchronization problems.

</details>


### [148] [Direct Observation of Dipolar-Driven Anisotropic Quantum Projection Noise in a Solid-State Spin Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16106)
*Tasuku Ono,Weijie Wu,Haopu Yang,Lillian B. Hughes Wyatt,Benjamin Brenner,Che Liu,Collin Fan,Chris R. Laumann,Jonathan N. Hallén,Emily J. Davis,Ania C. Bleszynski Jayich,Norman Y. Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作实现了对强相互作用二维氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）色心系综的量子投影噪声（quantum projection noise）分辨读出，并观测到偶极相互作用将该噪声演化为各向异性分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心是重要的量子传感平台，将读出提升至量子投影噪声极限并结合强偶极相互作用有望显著提高灵敏度，但实验上进入该区间长期是难题。

Method: 利用NV色心内禀的15N核自旋记忆，在约0.3 T的中等磁场下实施重复读出（repetitive readout），将读出保真度提升近一个数量级。

Result: 读出保真度的提升使得相干自旋态（coherent spin state）的量子投影噪声被直接分辨，并观测到系综内禀偶极相互作用将该噪声剪切为各向异性剖面。

Conclusion: 该结果为固态体系中自旋压缩（spin squeezing）与纠缠增强传感（entanglement-enhanced sensing）的直接测量开辟了道路。

Abstract: The nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center in diamond is a prominent quantum-sensing platform. Combining readout at the quantum projection noise limit with strong dipolar interactions promises substantial gains in sensitivity. However, experimentally accessing this regime has remained a longstanding challenge. In this work, we demonstrate quantum-projection-noise-resolved readout of a strongly-interacting, two-dimensional ensemble of NV centers. Our approach leverages repetitive readout via the NV's intrinsic $^{15}$N nuclear memory at a moderate magnetic field ($\sim 0.3$ T), improving the readout fidelities by nearly an order of magnitude. This enables us to directly resolve the quantum projection noise of a coherent spin state and to watch the ensemble's intrinsic dipolar interactions shear this noise into an anisotropic profile. Our results open the door to direct measurements of spin squeezing and entanglement-enhanced sensing in the solid state.

</details>


### [149] [Large-scale quantum simulations of dissipative spin-1/2 Heisenberg chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16108)
*João C. Getelina,Andrew Cox,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Omar Alsheikh,Ryan S. Bennink,James K. Freericks,Alexander F. Kemper*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用超导量子处理器 ibm_kingston 模拟多达 50 个格点的耗散自旋-1/2 海森堡链 Lindblad 动力学，并通过 Stinespring 膨胀（Stinespring dilation）实现耗散，测得静态结构因子并绘制出 $J_x$–$J_y$ 平面相图，基本厘清该基准系统的稳态相图争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与环境耦合的量子多体系统会弛豫至无非平衡对应物的非平衡稳态（nonequilibrium steady state），但其求解比封闭系统更难：密度矩阵问题使希尔伯特空间维度平方增长，且没有自由能可用来选择稳态。耗散自旋-1/2 海森堡链是该领域的基准模型，既有各类方法给出的相图互不一致，且大系统尺寸下的可控确定一直难以实现。

Method: 在超导处理器 ibm_kingston 上模拟最长 50 个格点的 Lindblad 动力学，最多同时使用 100 个活跃量子比特、纠缠门深度达 1700，并通过 Stinespring 膨胀（Stinespring dilation）实现耗散。该方法依赖耗散演化本身作为自纠错机制，使硬件噪声仅以微弱竞争耗散子的形式进入。作者测量静态结构因子（static structure factors），并在 $J_x$–$J_y$ 平面上以 117 个量子硬件数据点绘制相图。

Result: 实验分辨出铁磁（ferromagnetic）、反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）、自旋密度波（spin-density-wave）与顺磁（paramagnetic）稳态，得到具有丰富有序相的非平衡相图：其中仅保留平均场（mean-field）序的残余，尖锐转变在一维中让位于交跨（crossover）。此外还发现存在一种初始的（由 Trotter 化诱导的）自旋密度波相。

Conclusion: 该量子模拟在很大程度上解决了关于该基准系统正确相图的长期不确定性，并表明量子计算机已成为研究耗散量子系统相关科学问题的可行工具。研究还提示受控 Trotter 化（Trotterization）可作为在耗散自旋系统中工程化各种磁相的手段。

Abstract: A quantum many-body system coupled to an environment relaxes to a nonequilibrium steady state that can sustain order with no equilibrium counterpart. Computing such steady states is harder than closed-system dynamics as the density matrix problem squares the Hilbert-space dimension, and no free energy selects the steady state. The dissipative spin-1/2 Heisenberg chain is a benchmark example for nonequilibrium steady state physics; various methods have each calculated its phase diagram but do not agree, and a controlled determination at large system size has remained out of reach. Here we simulate the Lindblad dynamics of chains of up to 50 sites on the superconducting processor ibm_kingston -- 100 simultaneously active qubits at up to 1700 entangling-gate depths -- realizing the dissipation via Stinespring dilation. The system's dissipative evolution is a self-correcting mechanism that effectively erases errors, so hardware noise enters only as a weak competing dissipator. We measure static structure factors and resolve ferromagnetic, antiferromagnetic, spin-density-wave, and paramagnetic steady states, mapping the phase diagram with 117 quantum hardware data points across the $J_x$--$J_y$ plane. We uncover a rich non-equilibrium phase diagram of ordered phases with only remnants of the mean-field order, and where sharp transitions give way to the crossovers expected in one dimension. We also find the existence of an incipient (Trotter-induced) spin density wave phase, highlighting the potential of controlled Trotterization as a tool to engineer various magnetic phases in dissipative spin systems. Our quantum simulations largely settle the lingering uncertainty regarding the correct phase diagram of this benchmark system. Moreover, they show that quantum computers are now a feasible tool for addressing scientific questions involving dissipative quantum systems.

</details>


### [150] [Investigating Interacting Fermionic Models with Locality-Preserving Qubit Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16142)
*Ashutosh P. Tripathi,Debasish Banerjee,Sandip Maiti,Nilmani Mathur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将保持局域性的 Derby-Klassen（DK）费米子-量子比特映射与约束保持的 Clifford 态制备及对称性保持的变分线路相结合，在 VQE 框架下准确复现二维 t-V 与 Fermi-Hubbard 模型的低能性质，并在非零化学势下相对 Jordan-Wigner（JW）方案展现优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维相互作用费米子体系的量子模拟中，传统 Jordan-Wigner（JW）变换会产生越来越长的 Pauli 串与相应线路开销，因此需要考察保持局域性的费米子-量子比特映射的实际效用。DK 映射虽能保持费米子相互作用的局域性，但需引入扩展希尔伯特空间及额外约束来定义物理子空间，其可用性有待验证。

Method: 采用保持局域性的 DK 映射处理二维 t-V 与 Fermi-Hubbard 模型，通过 Clifford 门态制备将定义物理扇区的约束直接并入 Hamiltonian Variational Ansätz（HVA），并使用变分量子本征求解器（VQE）求解所得量子比特哈密顿量；同时利用拟设固有的粒子数守恒区分不同对称性扇区，并在非零化学势下与基于 JW 的变分线路进行基准比较。

Result: DK-HVA 能够准确复现量子比特哈密顿量的低能性质，并借助粒子数守恒可靠获取简并态。在粒子-空穴对称性及半填充无符号问题条件不存在的非零化学势情形下，该方案相对 JW 变分线路取得基准优势；在更高维费米子体系中，保持局域性的映射相较产生长 Pauli 串的 JW 变换体现出优势。扩展到自旋ful Fermi-Hubbard 模型时，发现费米子模式放置与跳跃项、在位相互作用项局域性之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个结合保持局域性费米子-量子比特映射、约束保持的 Clifford 态制备与对称性保持变分线路的实用框架，以辅助量子比特与 stabilizer 制备开销换取算符非局域性的降低，为高维相互作用费米子体系的资源高效量子模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate the utility of the locality-preserving Derby-Klassen (DK) fermion-to-qubit mapping [arXiv:2003.06939] for variational quantum simulation of two-dimensional $t$-$V$ and Fermi-Hubbard models. The DK mapping preserves the locality of fermionic interactions with an enlarged Hilbert space, thereby requiring additional constraints that define the physical sector. We incorporate these constraints directly into a Hamiltonian Variational Ansätz (HVA) through Clifford-gate state preparation and use the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) to show that the low-energy properties of the resulting qubit Hamiltonian are accurately reproduced. We further exploit particle-number conservation inherent in the ansätz to resolve distinct symmetry sectors and reliably access degenerate states. Consequently, we benchmark the DK-HVA against Jordan-Wigner-based variational circuits at nonzero chemical potential, where particle-hole symmetry and the associated half-filled sign-free condition are absent. Finally, we demonstrate the advantage of locality-preserving mappings in higher-dimensional fermionic systems, where the conventional Jordan-Wigner (JW) transformation generates increasingly long Pauli strings and corresponding circuit overheads. We further extend the framework to the spinful Fermi-Hubbard model and identify a tradeoff between fermionic-mode placement and the locality of hopping and on-site interaction terms. These results establish a practical framework combining locality-preserving fermion-to-qubit mappings, constraint-preserving Clifford state preparation, and symmetry-preserving variational circuits, that trades auxiliary-qubit and stabilizer-preparation overhead for reduced operator nonlocality, providing a practical route toward resource-efficient quantum simulation of higher-dimensional interacting fermionic systems.

</details>


### [151] [Noise-induced classical phases in optimally-unraveled random quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16160)
*Lorenzo Fioroni,Filippo Ferrari,Emanuele Tirrito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用掺杂非 Clifford 相位旋转（non-Clifford phase rotations）并受局域噪声影响的随机 Clifford 电路（random Clifford circuits），考察开放量子动力学（open quantum dynamics）的经典可模拟性（classical simulability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清噪声随机电路中经典性（classicality）的出现是否依赖于探测所采用的测量方案（measurement scheme）。

Method: 方法将动力学展开（unravel）为随机量子轨迹（stochastic quantum trajectories），以 Clifford 增强矩阵乘积态（Clifford-augmented matrix product states）模拟，并引入量化经典资源需求的模拟成本（simulation cost），再对随机展开优化该成本；同时借助量子信道（quantum channel）的几何表示解析求解最优展开，并进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 结果识别出噪声诱导的经典相（noise-induced classical phases），即任意电路深度下动力学均可被 Clifford 操作完全解纠缠（disentangle）的扩展参数区域，其存在同时取决于噪声模型（noise model）与展开方式；最优成本与非稳定化度（nonstabilizerness）相关联，并可结合轨迹分辨的纠缠（trajectory-resolved entanglement）与非稳定化度对动力学区间分类。

Conclusion: 结论表明这些经典相在平均密度矩阵（averaged density-matrix）描述中消失，说明噪声随机电路中经典性的出现依赖于探测所用的测量方案，并为驱动-耗散动力学（driven-dissipative dynamics）的经典可模拟性研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We study the classical simulability of open quantum dynamics using random Clifford circuits doped with non-Clifford phase rotations and subject to local noise. We unravel the dynamics into stochastic quantum trajectories simulated with Clifford-augmented matrix product states, and introduce a simulation cost that quantifies the classical resources required. Optimizing this cost over stochastic unravelings, we identify noise-induced classical phases: extended parameter regions in which the dynamics can be fully disentangled by Clifford operations at arbitrary circuit depth. Their existence depends on both the noise model \emph{and} the unraveling. Using a geometric representation of quantum channels, we analytically determine optimal unravelings for a broad class of noise models, with numerical simulations confirming the predicted phase boundaries. We further relate the optimal cost to the unraveling-independent nonstabilizerness of the channel and show that, together with trajectory-resolved entanglement and nonstabilizerness, it classifies distinct dynamical regimes. Finally, we show that these classical phases disappear in the averaged density-matrix description, where no unraveling freedom remains. Our results show that the emergence of classicality in noisy random circuits depends on the measurement scheme adopted to probe it, and paves the way to further studies on the classical simulability of driven-dissipative dynamics.

</details>


### [152] [Geometric View of Iterative Fixed-Node Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16308)
*Pranav Kairon,Bryan K. Clark*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究格点固定节点（lattice fixed-node）方法中迭代映射的几何与稳定性结构，证明其不动点为哈密顿量与本征态相关的本征态，且基态不动点稳定、其符号腔（sign chamber）边界排斥；其他腔内的激发态不动点不稳定并流向低能边界不动点，从而说明自洽迭代下振幅依赖（amplitude dependence）消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续电子结构哈密顿量中正确的符号结构（sign-structure）足以精确求解基态，而格点固定节点方法在保留符号结构的同时仍依赖试探波函数（trial wave-function）振幅，且振幅仅被非最优地改进。作者希望澄清这种振幅依赖在自洽迭代下的命运，并为费米子符号问题（Fermion sign problem）这一核心方法奠定理论基础。

Method: 构造一个迭代映射：反复用格点固定节点哈密顿量的基态替换固定节点试探态（不涉及实际算法的可实现性）。随后分析该映射的不动点、符号腔内部不动点数目，以及基态腔及其边界的稳定性与方向性稳定特征。

Result: 该映射的不动点是哈密顿量与约化哈密顿量（reduced Hamiltonian）的本征态，每个符号腔内部至多存在一个不动点；基态不动点被证明稳定，基态符号腔的边界为排斥的。其他符号腔内能量下降受所施加符号结构阻碍，使迭代将部分振幅驱向零，产生“支撑坍缩（support collapse）”至腔边界并对应约化哈密顿量，故激发态不动点可证不稳定并流向低能边界不动点。腔边界具有方向性稳定：某一方向吸引的边界点在符号翻转后变为排斥。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了迭代固定节点动力学的几何与稳定性结构，表明在自洽迭代下，超出正确基态符号结构的振幅依赖会消失；这为费米子符号问题的核心方法提供了理论基础，并可能启发新的算法策略。

Abstract: The sign-structure of a quantum many-body ground state is a central quantity in fixed-node approaches to mitigating the Fermion sign problem. For continuum electronic-structure Hamiltonians, the correct sign-structure is sufficient to compute exact ground state properties; on the other hand, lattice fixed-node approaches retain an additional dependence on trial wave-function amplitudes which are improved upon non-optimally while preserving their sign structure. Here we consider an iterative map obtained by repeatedly replacing the fixed-node trial state with the ground state of its associated lattice fixed-node Hamiltonian (independent of whether and how a practical algorithm could implement this iteration). We show the fixed points of this map are eigenstates of the Hamiltonian and reduced Hamiltonian resulting in at most one fixed point in the interior of each sign chamber. We prove that the ground state fixed point is stable and that the boundary of the ground state sign chamber is repulsive. This shows the amplitude dependence beyond having the correct ground state sign-structure disappears under self-consistent iteration. In other sign chambers, energy descent can be obstructed by the imposed sign structure; this tension leads to the iteration driving select amplitudes to zero producing `support collapse' onto chamber boundaries and corresponding to reduced Hamiltonians. Excited-state fixed points are therefore provably unstable and flow toward lower-energy boundary fixed points. We show sign chamber boundaries have a directional stability; boundary points attractive in one direction are repulsive under a sign flip. By revealing the geometry and stability structure of iterative fixed-node dynamics, our results clarify the foundations of a central approach to the Fermion sign problem and motivate potential new algorithmic strategies.

</details>


### [153] [Floquet Dressing and Bath Spectral Effects on the Geometric Phase of a Driven Dissipative Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16609)
*Chirag Arora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本工作以数值精确的过程张量-时间演化矩阵乘积算符（PT-TEMPO）方法，研究周期驱动对称自旋-玻色模型（SBM）中的耗散几何相位（GP）动力学，并引入非加性GP贡献以分离驱动-浴协同效应。结果表明周期驱动可依驱动与浴时间尺度关系抑制或增强浴诱导的GP形变，其机制可由Floquet谱调控解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Wang等人关于自旋-玻色模型中耗散几何相位研究的基础上，需进一步刻画周期驱动与耗散共同作用下GP的演化规律。核心动机在于区分并量化驱动与浴的协同效应，而非仅关注单一驱动或单一耗散贡献。

Method: 采用数值精确的过程张量-时间演化矩阵乘积算符（process-tensor time-evolving matrix product operator, PT-TEMPO）方法，研究对象为耦合Ohmic玻色环境的对称二能级系统，施加沿同一系统坐标的纵向单色驱动。为分离协同效应，引入非加性（non-additive）GP贡献，将驱动耗散响应拆分为驱动-幺正（driven-unitary）与非驱动-耗散（undriven-dissipative）组分。并借助Floquet谱分析（Floquet spectral steering）进行解释。

Result: 周期驱动会抑制或增强浴诱导的GP形变，取决于驱动与浴时间尺度的相互作用。Floquet分析显示，驱动将相干动力学重新分配到准能边带（quasienergy sidebands）上，其跃迁频率与傅里叶分辨的系统-浴矩阵元共同决定其与环境谱的耦合强度。主导耗散路径由Floquet矩阵元权重与浴谱重叠共同决定，而非由其中任一单独因素决定。

Conclusion: 该研究将Floquet工程（Floquet engineering）、量子耗散（quantum dissipation）与几何相位动力学联系起来，确立了周期驱动作为调控开放量子系统（open quantum systems, OQSs）几何相位的手段。

Abstract: Building on recent work by Wang et al. on the dissipative geometric phase (GP) in the spin-boson model (SBM), the GP dynamics of a periodically driven symmetric SBM are investigated using the numerically exact process-tensor time-evolving matrix product operator (PT-TEMPO) method. A symmetric two-level system subjected to a longitudinal monochromatic drive along the same system coordinate that couples to an Ohmic bosonic environment exhibits rich GP dynamics arising from the interplay between driving and dissipation. To isolate cooperative drive-bath effects, a non-additive GP contribution is introduced that separates the driven dissipative response from its driven-unitary and undriven-dissipative components. Periodic driving is found to either suppress or enhance bath-induced GP deformation depending on the interplay between drive and bath timescales. These trends are interpreted through Floquet spectral steering, whereby the drive redistributes the coherent dynamics among quasienergy sidebands whose transition frequencies and Fourier-resolved system-bath matrix elements determine their coupling to the environmental spectrum. Floquet analysis shows that the dominant dissipative pathways are governed jointly by Floquet matrix-element weights and bath spectral overlap, rather than by either alone. These results connect Floquet engineering, quantum dissipation, and GP dynamics and establish periodic driving as a means of controlling the GP of open quantum systems (OQSs).

</details>


### [154] [Initial-Slip Dynamics Enables the Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16781)
*M. Crotti,F. Cavaliere,D. Ferraro,G. Benenti,M. Sassetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子谐振子（quantum harmonic oscillator）线性耦合玻色环境中的量子姆潘巴效应（quantum Mpemba effect），即初始偏离平衡更远的系统反而弛豫更快。作者针对不同温度的因子化热态推导出精确解析判据，并指出该效应由相互作用突然开启引发的初始滑移动力学（initial-slip dynamics）所驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应表现为初始偏离平衡更远的系统弛豫快于初始更接近平衡的系统。既有研究多局限于弱耦合（weak-coupling）与马尔可夫近似（Markovian approximation），其适用边界与物理起源尚不清晰，因此需要超越这些近似的精确刻画。

Method: 研究对象为线性耦合玻色环境的量子谐振子，初态取不同温度的因子化热态（factorized thermal states）。作者推导了量子姆潘巴效应发生的精确解析判据（exact analytical criteria），该判据同时适用于弱耦合与非马尔可夫情形。

Result: 所推导的解析判据在超出弱耦合与马尔可夫近似的范围内依然成立。结果表明，该效应由系统-环境相互作用突然开启所产生的初始滑移动力学所促成；若忽略该贡献，反常弛豫被完全抑制。

Conclusion: 瞬态系统-环境关联（transient system-bath correlations）是量子姆潘巴效应背后的关键要素，初始滑移动力学构成其产生机制。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum Mpemba effect, whereby a system initially farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to equilibrium, emerging in a quantum harmonic oscillator linearly coupled to a bosonic environment. Starting from factorized thermal states at different temperatures, we derive exact analytical criteria for the occurrence of the quantum Mpemba effect valid also beyond the typically considered weak-coupling and Markovian approximations. Most importantly, we show that the effect is enabled by the initial-slip dynamics generated by the sudden switch-on of the system-bath interaction. Neglecting this contribution completely suppresses the anomalous relaxation, identifying transient system-bath correlations as the key ingredient underlying the quantum Mpemba effect.

</details>


### [155] [Preparation-protocol-dependent quantum Mpemba dynamics in a magnetically tunable graphene nanotorus qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17176)
*J. Furtado*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究石墨烯纳米环量子比特（graphene nanotorus qubit）中的量子姆潘巴效应（quantum Mpemba effect），表明其强度强烈依赖初始态制备方式：裸吉布斯制备（bare Gibbs preparation）产生强反常弛豫，而驱动稳态制备（driven steady-state preparation）几乎完全抑制该效应；机制源于慢活性子空间（slow active sector）中模态权重差异，而非衰减率层级的改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应（即初始较"热"态比较"冷"态更快弛豫至稳态的反常现象）已在多种开放量子系统中被报道，但初始态制备方式是否以及如何调控该效应的强度尚不明确。该文以弯曲石墨烯量子比特为平台，旨在厘清制备协议对反常弛豫的调控作用。

Method: 在共享相同末态哈密顿量（final Hamiltonian）、热浴（thermal bath）与马尔可夫林德布拉德算符（Markovian Liouvillian）的前提下，对比两种制备协议：裸吉布斯制备（相干驱动仅在最终淬火时开启）与驱动稳态制备（初始态为驱动动力学的稳态）。采用到最终稳态的距离及积分姆潘巴参数（integrated Mpemba parameter）进行量化，并结合林德布拉德模式分析（Liouvillian-mode analysis）考察慢活性子空间中的模态权重。

Result: 裸吉布斯制备在广泛参数区域呈现强反常弛豫，姆潘巴参数 $M_B$ 趋近于 1；驱动稳态制备则几乎完全抑制该效应。模式分析显示，裸吉布斯协议通常给出热态在慢活性子空间权重小于冷态（$R_s<1$），而驱动稳态协议主要给出 $R_s>1$。由于在对应参数点上两种协议的最终林德布拉德谱（Liouvillian spectrum）完全相同，差异源自制备依赖的模态权重，而非衰减率层级（decay-rate hierarchy）的变化。

Conclusion: 该研究将态制备（state preparation）确认为弯曲石墨烯量子比特中反常量子弛豫的一个可控参数，并表明在谱结构不变的情形下，模态权重的制备依赖性即可决定量子姆潘巴效应的强弱。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum Mpemba effect in a graphene nanotorus qubit and show that its strength depends strongly on how the initial state is prepared. We compare two protocols that share the same final Hamiltonian, thermal bath, and Markovian Liouvillian: bare Gibbs preparation, in which the coherent drive is switched on only at the final quench, and driven steady-state preparation, in which the initial states are stationary states of the driven dynamics. Using the distance to the final stationary state and an integrated Mpemba parameter, we find broad regions of strong anomalous relaxation for bare Gibbs preparation, with $M_B$ approaching unity, whereas the driven steady-state protocol almost completely suppresses the effect. A Liouvillian-mode analysis reveals the mechanism: the bare Gibbs protocol typically yields a smaller hot-state weight in the slow active sector than the cold state, $R_s<1$, while the driven steady-state protocol predominantly gives $R_s>1$. Since the final Liouvillian spectrum is identical for both protocols at corresponding parameter points, the contrast originates from preparation-dependent modal weights rather than from changes in the decay-rate hierarchy. These results identify state preparation as a control parameter for anomalous quantum relaxation in a curved graphene qubit.

</details>


### [156] [Exceptional Topology Survives Strong Hermitian Fields in Radiative Atomic Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17313)
*Tian-Shu Gou,Yi-Cheng Wang,Ya-Tang Yu,Guin-Dar Lin,Jhih-Shih You,H. H. Jen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在二维亚波长原子阵列中，通过方-三角连续形变与磁场的协同调控，观测到例外点（exceptional points）反常地不被强厄米磁场移除，而是被驱向光锥，并揭示趋肤局域化的非单调响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界双带体系中例外点通常会被足够强的厄米场（Hermitian field）消除，本文旨在探索是否存在可抵御该效应的例外情形，并将晶格几何、光锥奇异性与磁场统一为拓扑调控手段。

Method: 采用二维亚波长原子阵列作为平台，在固定磁场下沿连续的方-三角（square-to-triangular）形变进行调控；该形变使体能带在带陈数（band Chern numbers）为 (C_1,C_2)=(2,-2) 的线隙拓扑区之间，经过无隙例外区。

Result: 在有限形变区间内，增强磁场仅将例外点驱向光锥（light cone），归因于奇异的辐射偶极耦合（radiative dipolar couplings）；同时，边界趋肤局域化（skin localization）对同一磁场呈非单调响应：中等磁场引发双极趋肤局域化，体模积聚于相对边缘，而更强磁场抑制边界局域化。

Conclusion: 晶格几何、光锥奇异性与厄米磁场的相互作用构成一种超越常规强场极限的机制，可用于设计陈拓扑与例外拓扑（Chern and exceptional topology）。

Abstract: Exceptional points are topological defects of complex band structures that are stable against weak perturbations, yet in bounded two-band systems they are ultimately removed by a sufficiently strong Hermitian field. Here we demonstrate a striking exception in a two-dimensional subwavelength atomic array. Along a continuous square-to-triangular deformation at fixed magnetic field, the bulk passes between line-gapped topological regions with band Chern numbers (C_1,C_2)=(2,-2) through a gapless exceptional region. Within a finite interval of lattice deformation, increasing the magnetic field merely drives the exceptional points toward the light cone because of the singular radiative dipolar couplings. We further show that skin localization toward open boundaries responds non-monotonically to the same field. An intermediate field drives a bipolar skin localization, with bulk modes accumulating at opposite edges, whereas stronger fields suppress boundary localization. Our results establish the interplay of lattice geometry, light-cone singularity, and Hermitian magnetic field as a route to engineering Chern and exceptional topology beyond the conventional strong-field limit.

</details>


### [157] [Adaptive Relational Learning on Multi-instance Quantum Data with Photonic Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17352)
*Marcin Jastrzebski,Shang Yu,Raj B. Patel,Oleksandr Kyriienko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应关系学习框架（adaptive relational learning framework），可将多个量子态并行加载到量子机器学习（quantum machine learning, QML）模型中，通过SWAP/CYCLE测试获取全局测量以评估n态Bargmann不变量，并结合作用于各输入态的浅层可训练变换，直接学习态间关系特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键信息可能存在于多个量子态之间的关系而非单个态内部，因此需要并行加载多态并访问成对及高阶关系的QML模型；同时，非自适应的“先测量”（measure-first）方案在恢复此类关系特征时成本高昂。

Method: 面向多实例量子数据构建自适应关系学习框架：利用SWAP或CYCLE测试进行全局测量以评估n态Bargmann不变量，并对每个输入态局部施加浅层可训练变换；在连续变量（continuous-variable, CV）光子系统中实现，并与基于连续变量经典阴影（classical shadows）的非自适应“先测量”方法进行基准比较。

Result: 在隐藏关系检测、几何相位分类以及存在未知共享干扰相互作用下的传感任务中完成求解；阴影估计成本随n快速上升，而所提模型避免该依赖。n=2时，用500次推断采样即达到完美测试精度A=1.0，较阴影方法平均测试精度提升ΔA=0.15，且每个数据点所需采样数少100倍。

Conclusion: 自适应光子QML可访问非自适应“先测量”模型难以低成本恢复的关系特征，为传感与量子数据应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Loading multiple quantum states in parallel into a quantum machine learning (QML) model can unlock learning tasks where key information resides in the \emph{relations} between states rather than in individual states. We introduce an adaptive relational learning framework for such multi-instance quantum data that accesses pairwise and higher-order relations. Our model combines global measurements via SWAP or CYCLE tests for evaluating an $n$-state Bargmann invariant with shallow trainable transformations applied locally to each input state. We demonstrate the approach for continuous-variable (CV) photonic systems, which naturally provide access to quantum data and necessary computing operations. We solve tasks involving hidden relationship detection, geometric phase classification, and sensing in the presence of an unknown shared nuisance interaction. We benchmark the adaptive model against a non-adaptive ``measure-first'' approach based on continuous-variable classical shadows, and show that the cost of shadow estimation grows rapidly with $n$, while our model avoids this dependence. Already for $n=2$, we achieve perfect test accuracy $A=1.0$ with $500$ inference shots, improving average test accuracy over the shadow-based method by $ΔA=0.15$ while using $100$ times fewer shots per data point. Our work opens routes to sensing and quantum-data applications where adaptive photonic QML can access relational features that are costly to recover with non-adaptive, measure-first models.

</details>

### [158] [Weighted heat traces of the Dirichlet Laplacian on Lipschitz domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16746)
*Lucas Kersten*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了有界 Lipschitz 区域上 Dirichlet Laplacian 加权热迹的渐近展开，权重为到边界的距离的幂乘以在边界附近连续的有界函数，并依据权重指数给出单项或双项渐近式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画带边界奇异权重的热迹渐近行为，并由此导出加权 Riesz 平均的渐近式与 Laplace 特征函数局部化测度的收敛结果。

Method: 方法为对有界 Lipschitz 区域（Lipschitz domain）上的 Dirichlet Laplacian（Dirichlet Laplacian）加权热迹（weighted heat trace）进行渐近展开分析，权重取为到边界的距离的幂次与边界附近连续有界函数之积；所得渐近项数取决于权重指数。

Result: 依照权重指数取值，得到单项渐近式（one-term asymptotics）或双项渐近式（two-term asymptotics）；并给出两项应用：任意阶加权 Riesz 平均（weighted Riesz means）的单项渐近式版本，以及描述 Laplace 特征函数局部化的测度族的收敛结果。

Conclusion: 加权热迹的渐近展开可统一处理权重指数的不同情形，并为加权 Riesz 平均渐近与特征函数局部化测度收敛提供工具。

Abstract: We prove an asymptotic expansion of a weighted heat trace of the Dirichlet Laplacian on a bounded Lipschitz domain. The weight is given by a power of the distance to the boundary times a bounded function that is continuous near the boundary. Depending on the exponent of the weight, we obtain one-term or two-term asymptotics. We present two applications, namely a version of one-term asymptotics of weighted Riesz means for arbitrary orders, as well as convergence results for a family of measures that describes localization of Laplace eigenfunctions.

</details>


### [159] [Irreducibility of the Bloch variety for periodic Schrödinger operators in arbitrary dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16763)
*Wencai Liu*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在维度 $d\ge1$、周期势 $V\in C(\mathbb{T}^d;\mathbb{C})$ 的条件下，Schrödinger 算子 $-\Delta+V$ 的 Bloch 簇（Bloch variety）在模周期性意义下不可约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期 Schrödinger 算子能带结构的整体连通性，即 Bloch 簇在模周期性下的不可约性，这一问题关系到谱带分支是否可能分裂为彼此独立的解析分量。

Method: 证明结合了谱带函数（spectral band functions）的全纯延拓（holomorphic continuation）与准动量（quasimomentum）的整数平移；并沿虚射线方向运用解析几何（analytic geometry）与扰动估计（perturbation estimates）。

Result: 证明表明，经适当的整数平移后，Bloch 簇的所有不可约分量（irreducible components）都包含同一个全纯谱分支（holomorphic spectral branch），从而得到模周期性意义下的不可约性。

Conclusion: 对于任意 $d\ge1$ 及连续复值周期势，$-\Delta+V$ 的 Bloch 簇在模周期性下不可约；该结论依赖于整数平移下谱分支的一致性。

Abstract: Let $d\ge1$ and $V\in C(\mathbb{T}^d;\mathbb{C})$. We prove that the Bloch variety of $-Δ+V$ is irreducible modulo periodicity. The proof combines holomorphic continuation of spectral band functions with integer translations of quasimomentum. Using analytic geometry and perturbation estimates along imaginary rays, we show that, after suitable integer translations, all irreducible components contain the same holomorphic spectral branch.

</details>

### [160] [A Differential Algebraic Framework for Boundary Layer $α$-Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16442)
*C. Valencia-Negrete,J. Flores,M. Perez,M. Romero de Terreros,M. Favela*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该研究通过刻画Prandtl与Leray-α边界层饱和幺半群（saturation monoids）之间的差异，并在代数上区分Helmholtz算子（Helmholtz operator）的作用，说明当向量场修正被限制在由微分滤波器（differential filter）确定的最小尺度时，可避免在有限时间内构造出达到无限速度解（infinite velocity）的悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于描述并区分Prandtl边界层与Leray-α边界层的饱和幺半群差异，并寻求从代数角度区分Helmholtz算子的作用，以处理与该算子相关的、有限时间内速度趋于无穷的解构造悖论。

Method: 方法上从饱和幺半群（saturation monoids）的比较与Helmholtz算子的代数区分入手，考察向量场（vector field）修正被微分滤波器（differential filter）在最小尺度处中止的情形。

Result: 结果表明，当向量场修正被中止于由微分滤波器确定的最小尺度时，构造在有限时间内达到无限速度的解这一悖论得以避免；同时可得到施加了Helmholtz算子的边界层在壁面附近的Taylor展开（Taylor expansion）系数。

Conclusion: 结论是：对饱和幺半群差异的刻画与对Helmholtz算子作用的代数区分，既能规避有限时间无限速度解构造的悖论，也能给出相应边界层壁面附近Taylor展开的系数。

Abstract: By addressing the problem of describing the differences between the saturation monoids of the Prandtl and Leray-$α$ boundary layers, and algebraically distinguishing the action of the Helmholtz operator, we can show that, when the modification of the vector field is halted at a minimum scale determined by the differential filter, the paradox of constructing a solution that reaches infinite velocity in a finite time is avoided. Furthermore, it allows us to obtain the coefficients of the Taylor expansion near the wall for boundary layers where the Helmholtz operator has been applied.

</details>


### [161] [Asymptotic Stability of Multi-Solitons for Coupled Nonlinear Schrödinger Equations via the $\bar{\partial}$-Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16480)
*Yubin Huang,Liming Ling,Huajie Su*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文围绕聚焦型耦合非线性薛定谔（focusing coupled nonlinear Schrödinger, CNLS）方程，构建其黎曼-希尔伯特问题（Riemann-Hilbert problem, RHP），并借助 Darboux 变换与 $\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法（$\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method）研究长时间渐近行为与多孤子稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是刻画聚焦型 CNLS 方程的长时间渐近行为与多孤子动力学。相较改进的非线性最速下降法（improved nonlinear steepest descent method），本文旨在提升误差估计精度，并揭示多孤子碰撞的代数结构。

Method: 基于相应的 $3\times3$ 矩阵谱问题（matrix spectral problem）建立 CNLS 方程的 RHP，并用 Darboux 变换消除初始 RHP 的离散谱（discrete spectrum）。随后采用 $\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法，对加权 Sobolev 空间（weighted Sobolev space）中的初值推导解的长时间渐近行为。

Result: 建立了加权 Sobolev 空间中 CNLS 方程解的长时间渐近行为，并将误差估计提升至 $\mathcal{O}\left(t^{-3/4}\right)$ 量级。同时获得 CNLS 方程多孤子解（multi-soliton solutions）的渐近稳定性，并从 Yang-Baxter 映射（Yang-Baxter map）视角分析多孤子碰撞规律。

Conclusion: 结果显示 $\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法可有效提升 CNLS 方程长时间渐近分析的误差阶。多孤子解的渐近稳定性及其碰撞规律可用 Yang-Baxter 映射刻画，表明孤子碰撞具有可积结构特征。

Abstract: The Riemann-Hilbert problem for the focusing coupled nonlinear Schrödinger (CNLS) equation is formulated on the basis of the corresponding $3\times3$ matrix spectral problem. We remove the discrete spectrum of initial RHP with the aid of Darboux transformations. Based on the $\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method, we establish the long-time asymptotic behavior of solutions to the CNLS equation for initial condition in the weighted Sobolev space. Compared to the improved nonlinear steepest descent method, we improve the error estimate up to order $\mathcal{O}\left(t^{-3/4}\right)$. Furthermore, we obtain the asymptotic stability of multi-soliton solutions for CNLS equation and analyze the law of multi-soliton collision in the view-point of Yang-Baxter map.

</details>


### [162] [A Gauge-Covariant Semiclassical Limit from the magnetic Hartree--Fock equation to the magnetic Vlasov--Poisson equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16511)
*Jacky J. Chong,Zhuohui You*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究三维空间中、在依赖于空间的非均匀磁场（nonuniform magnetic field）下，从磁性 Hartree–Fock 方程（magnetic Hartree–Fock equation）到磁性 Vlasov–Poisson 方程（magnetic Vlasov–Poisson equation）的半经典极限（semiclassical limit），并在规范协变（gauge covariant）框架下给出显式收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在非均匀、纯空间依赖磁场时，半经典极限如何从量子多体方程过渡到经典动理学方程尚需刻画。已有处理往往依赖特定矢势（vector potential）的选择，缺乏规范协变性，因此需要建立以磁场本身表述、且具规范协变性的收敛估计。

Method: 首先，在指数加权（exponentially weighted）假设下建立磁性 Vlasov–Poisson 方程解的正则性传播（propagation of regularity）。随后，基于该结果与规范协变磁性 Weyl 量子化（gauge-covariant magnetic Weyl quantization）框架，在半经典 Schatten 范数（semiclassical Schatten norms）下推导显式收敛界。

Result: 获得在半经典 Schatten 范数意义下的显式收敛界；该估计以磁场而非特定矢势表述，因而具有规范协变性。所得半经典收敛性对合适的初值类（class of initial data）在任意固定有限时间区间上成立。

Conclusion: 该工作将磁性 Hartree–Fock 到磁性 Vlasov–Poisson 的半经典极限推广至非均匀、纯空间依赖磁场情形，并通过规范协变的量子化框架给出以磁场表述的显式收敛估计。

Abstract: We study the semiclassical limit from the magnetic Hartree--Fock equation to the magnetic Vlasov--Poisson equation in three spatial dimensions in the presence of a nonuniform, purely space-dependent magnetic field. We first establish propagation of regularity for solutions of the magnetic Vlasov--Poisson equation under exponentially weighted assumptions. Building on this result and a gauge-covariant magnetic Weyl quantization framework, we then derive explicit convergence bounds for the semiclassical limit in semiclassical Schatten norms. In particular, the estimates are formulated in terms of the magnetic field rather than a particular choice of vector potential and are therefore gauge covariant. The resulting semiclassical convergence holds for a suitable class of initial data on any fixed finite time interval.

</details>


### [163] [Nonlinear Phase Mixing in Einstein-Vlasov System near Schwarzschild Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13394)
*Saehoon Eo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在球对称条件下研究 Schwarzschild 时空附近的 Einstein-Vlasov 系统，聚焦物质支撑于有界测地线（bounded geodesics）的情形。此时物质衰减由相位混合（phase mixing）而非色散（dispersion）驱动。作者在线性问题中获得定量相位混合，并证明周期函数的单调性；在非线性耦合问题中，证明相位混合可维持至非线性回声机制（nonlinear echo mechanism）预期的时标。该结果依赖特定规范选择：等距坐标系（isotropic coordinate system）与指定平均曲率叶状结构（foliation of prescribed mean curvature）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解 Schwarzschild 时空附近 Einstein-Vlasov 系统中物质的衰减机制。当物质支撑于有界测地线时，色散机制失效，衰减由相位混合主导，因此需要新的分析工具刻画该机制及其非线性可维持时标。

Method: 方法上，线性问题通过证明周期函数（period function）的单调性以获得定量相位混合。非线性耦合问题则借助动力学作用量-角变量（dynamical action angle variables）分析，并结合向量场方法（vector field method）的积分估计。规范选择采用等距坐标系与指定平均曲率叶状结构，使 Einstein 方程因其椭圆性质（elliptic nature）相对物质获得导数。

Result: 线性问题中得到定量相位混合，核心新要素为周期函数的单调性。非线性耦合问题中证明相位混合可持续至非线性回声机制所预期的时标。上述结果在所选规范下得以实现，该规范使 Einstein 方程对物质获得导数增益。

Conclusion: 结论为：在有界测地线支撑情形下，Einstein-Vlasov 系统的物质衰减由相位混合主导；通过周期函数单调性、动力学作用量-角变量与向量场积分估计，并结合等距坐标与指定平均曲率叶状结构的规范，可将相位混合结果推广至非线性层面，直至回声机制预期时标。

Abstract: We study the Einstein-Vlasov system near the Schwarzschild spacetime in spherical symmetry. In particular, we consider the case when the matter is supported on bounded geodesics. In this regime, the decay of the matter is driven by phase mixing rather than dispersion. For the linearized problem, we obtain quantitative phase mixing; the key new ingredient proved here is a monotonicity property of the period function. For the nonlinear coupled problem, we prove phase mixing up to the timescale expected from the nonlinear echo mechanism; this is achieved by analysis on dynamical action angle variables and the integral estimates with the vector field method. This construction is made possible by our gauge choice, an isotropic coordinate system together with a foliation of prescribed mean curvature, in which the Einstein equations gain derivatives over the matter due to its elliptic nature.

</details>


### [164] [Uniform many-particle spectral gap inequality and heat kernel bounds for strong attractive interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17333)
*S. E. Boutiah,D. Kinzebulatov,K. R. Madou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类由奇异吸引型对相互作用（singular attractive pair interactions）修正的高斯测度（Gaussian measures）满足谱隙不等式（spectral gap inequality），其常数显式且对粒子数与正则化参数一致；作为应用，借助截断相互作用权重的相应不等式，得到吸引对数气体（attractive logarithmic gas）转移密度（transition density）的双侧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于处理奇异吸引型相互作用下高斯测度的谱隙性质，并希望在粒子数与正则化参数上获得一致可控的估计。其应用目标是分析吸引对数气体转移密度这一难以直接刻画的对象。

Method: 方法上，首先证明经奇异吸引型对相互作用修正的高斯测度满足谱隙不等式，并给出显式、对粒子数和正则化参数一致的谱隙常数；随后对截断相互作用权重（cutoff interaction weight）建立相应的谱隙不等式，用于导出目标量的界。

Result: 得到的主要结果为：谱隙不等式的常数显式且关于粒子数与正则化参数一致；并由此获得了吸引对数气体转移密度的双侧界（two-sided bounds）。

Conclusion: 结论表明，谱隙不等式可作为研究奇异吸引型相互作用系统的有效工具，既能提供一致的常数控制，也能用于推导吸引对数气体转移密度的定量双侧估计。

Abstract: We prove a spectral gap inequality for Gaussian measures modified by singular attractive pair interactions. The spectral gap constant is explicit and uniform in the number of particles and the regularization parameter. As an application, we use a corresponding spectral gap inequality for a cutoff interaction weight to obtain two-sided bounds on the transition density of the attractive logarithmic gas.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Molecular representation shapes the balance between target fidelity and exploration in flow based polymer generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16028)
*Tianren Zhang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 PolyLatentFlow（潜空间连续时间流匹配框架）与 LlamaUni（融合聚合物序列与三维结构的多模态表示），用于无条件与条件聚合物生成；LlamaUni 在有效性、多样性与低训练集复现率方面表现最优，并在 CO₂/N₂ 条件任务中取得最高单次尝试的目标命中产出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限标注数据下设计具有目标性质的聚合物，需在庞大的化学空间中搜索，属于典型的小样本逆向设计难题。作者同时关注分子表示对目标控制与探索能力的影响，而不仅限于性质预测精度。

Method: 基于潜空间（latent space）中的连续时间流匹配（continuous-time flow matching），构建无条件与条件生成框架 PolyLatentFlow；并提出多模态表示 LlamaUni，联合聚合物序列（sequence）与三维结构（3D structural information）信息。

Result: 无条件生成中，PolyLatentFlow 结合 LlamaUni 在相比 PolyInfo 的新颖（novel）有效候选产出量上为所评估生成器中最优，同时保持高多样性；T_g 条件生成中，生成性质分布随 200 °C 目标区间系统性移动；多性质任务中，不同分子表示在代理目标保真度（surrogate target fidelity）相近，但在有效性、训练集复现（training-set replay）与结构邻近性上差异显著。

Conclusion: PolyLatentFlow 结合 LlamaUni 持续实现高有效性与低复现率，并在 CO₂/N₂ 条件任务中获得最大的单次尝试非复现目标命中产出。结果表明潜空间流匹配可用于聚合物逆向设计（inverse design），且分子表示是决定目标控制与超越已知化学空间探索能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Designing polymers with targeted properties requires navigating vast chemical spaces from limited labeled data. Here we introduce PolyLatentFlow, a framework based on continuous-time flow matching in latent space for unconditional and conditional polymer generation, together with LlamaUni, a multimodal representation combining polymer sequence and 3D structural information. In unconditional generation, PolyLatentFlow with LlamaUni produced the largest yield of valid candidates novel relative to PolyInfo among the evaluated unconditional generators while maintaining high diversity. For $T_g$ conditioning, generated property distributions shifted systematically across a 200 °C target range. In multi-property tasks, molecular representations showed similar surrogate target fidelity but differed markedly in validity, training-set replay, and structural proximity to labeled polymers. PolyLatentFlow with LlamaUni consistently combined high validity with low replay and achieved the largest per-attempt yield of nonreplayed target hits for CO$_2$/N$_2$ conditioning. These results demonstrate latent space flow matching for polymer inverse design and identify molecular representation as a key determinant of target control and exploration beyond labeled chemistry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Anderson localization in topological metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16124)
*A. A. Burkov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究拓扑金属（topological metals, TM）在无序（disorder）存在时是否仍保留其非平庸特性。作者指出无序下“无隙性（gaplessness）”概念失去严格意义，转而以长程纠缠（long-range entanglement）为判据，并证明三维外尔半金属（Weyl semimetal）中存在这种拓扑保护，而二维/三维（I型）狄拉克半金属（Dirac semimetal）与三维节点线半金属（nodal line semimetal）中则不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在干净极限下，拓扑金属的无隙性由能带接触节点（band-touching nodes）保护，但无序引入的态会填充谱隙，使无隙性失去明确含义，因此需要重新表述拓扑金属中“拓扑非平庸性”的判据。核心动机在于厘清拓扑是否能够保护拓扑金属免于安德森局域化（Anderson localization）。

Method: 方法上，将非量子化反常（unquantized anomaly）视为拓扑金属的拓扑响应（topological response），并结合“装饰畴壁”（decorated domain wall）构造——该构造此前用于讨论平均对称保护拓扑绝缘相（average symmetry-protected topological insulating phases）——来分析无序下的拓扑保护问题。

Result: 结果表明，此类拓扑保护在三维外尔半金属中存在，但在二维与三维（I型）狄拉克半金属以及三维节点线半金属中并不存在。

Conclusion: 拓扑金属在无序下是否保持非平庸拓扑，取决于具体类型：仅三维外尔半金属的无序态仍受拓扑保护、免于安德森局域化，而非所有拓扑金属均具备此保护。该结论通过非量子化反常与装饰畴壁构造相结合的方式得以建立。

Abstract: We address the question of whether topological metals (TM) retain their nontrivial characteristics in the presence of disorder. While in the clean case, the issue is what protects the gaplessness of the spectrum, i.e. the band-touching nodes in TM, this question needs to be reformulated in the presence of disorder. The concept of gaplessness loses its sharp meaning in this case, since spectral gaps can be filled in by disorder-induced states. What is still meaningful is entanglement: one may ask whether the long-range entanglement of the clean gapless TM survives in the presence of disorder or, in other words, whether nontrivial topology protects the TM from Anderson localization. We demonstrate that such a protection exists in the case of three-dimensional (3D) Weyl semimetals, but not in 2D and 3D (type-I) Dirac or 3D nodal line semimetals. We establish this by combining the idea of unquantized anomaly as the topological response of TM with the decorated domain wall construction, used previously to discuss average symmetry-protected topological insulating phases.

</details>


### [167] [An improved auxiliary boson method for quantum dots with finite Coulomb interaction U](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16311)
*Juan Pablo Ramos-Andrade,Patricia Almeida,Ana María Calle,Gustavo Lara*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出对 Kotliar-Ruckenstein 四辅助玻色子（four auxiliary bosons）方法的改进版本，用于描述具有有限电子-电子相互作用 U 的 Kondo 区量子点（quantum dot）；基准计算与数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group）结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Kotliar-Ruckenstein 四辅助玻色子表述在描述量子点 Kondo 区时，对强电子关联（strong electronic correlations）的处理精度有限，需在保持原方法解析可处理性的同时改进其准确性。

Method: 在 Kotliar-Ruckenstein 原始四辅助玻色子框架基础上提出改进表述，适配于具有有限电子-电子相互作用 U 的 Kondo 区量子点，并保留原方法的解析可处理性（analytical tractability）。

Result: 改进方法对强电子关联给出了更精确的处理，可可靠描述纳米尺度系统（nanoscale systems）的输运性质；基准计算（benchmark calculations）与数值重整化群结果吻合良好，支持该改进表述的有效性。

Conclusion: 该改进框架为研究强关联量子点（strongly correlated quantum dots）中的输运过程提供了高效且稳健（efficient and robust）的方法。

Abstract: In this work, we present an improved version of the formulation of the four auxiliary bosons originally proposed by Kotliar and Ruckenstein, adapted to describe a quantum dot in the Kondo regime with finite electron-electron interaction U. The proposed refinement provides a more accurate treatment of strong electronic correlations while preserving the analytical tractability of the original approach, thereby enabling a reliable description of transport properties in nanoscale systems. Benchmark calculations show good agreement with the results of the numerical renormalization group, supporting the validity of the improved formulation. Our approach therefore provides an efficient and robust framework for investigating transport through strongly correlated quantum dots.

</details>


### [168] [Probing proximity-induced superconductivity in bilayer graphene using gate-defined quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16320)
*Philipp Schmidt,Mathies Funke,Till Tenger,Katarina Stanojević,Hubert Dulisch,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Fabian Hassler,Christian Volk,Szabolcs Csonka,Christoph Stampfer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作基于超导 NbSe$_2$ 邻近化的双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene, BLG）构建全二维（all-2D）混合平台，利用局域静电栅定义隧穿势垒与量子点，作为邻近化 BLG 沟道的局域谱学探针，观测到最高约 80 μeV 的邻近诱导超导能隙并追踪其随距离的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德瓦尔斯异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）可将具有不同电子性质的二维材料集成于单一器件；BLG 因电可调带隙而适合局域调控隧穿势垒与量子点，但需要可调控的超导-半导体混合平台来研究邻近效应及其空间演化。

Method: 以超导 NbSe$_2$ 对 BLG 进行邻近化，构成全二维混合平台；通过局域静电栅（local electrostatic gates）在与横向超导-半导体界面不同距离处定义隧穿势垒与量子点，形成可调的超导体-量子点-正常导体结（superconductor-quantum dot-normal conductor junction）器件；采用库仑阻塞（Coulomb blockade）与有限偏压谱（finite-bias spectroscopy）进行测量。

Result: 测量揭示最高约 80 μeV 的邻近诱导超导能隙（proximity-induced superconducting gap），并可追踪其随与 NbSe$_2$ 接触距离增加而变化的演化；局域态密度（local density of states）在超过 1 μm 的距离上仍保持抑制，与通过高度弹道（highly ballistic）BLG 沟道的超导邻近效应一致。

Conclusion: BLG-超导体混合体系提供了可操控平台，可将量子点与量子点接触（quantum point contacts）与超导电性良好结合。

Abstract: Van der Waals heterostructures offer a direct way of combining two-dimensional (2D) materials with different electronic properties, such as 2D semiconductors, metals, and superconductors, in a single device. Bilayer graphene (BLG) is particularly attractive in this context, as its electrically tunable band gap enables local control of tunnel barriers and quantum dots. Here, we realize an all-2D hybrid platform based on BLG proximitized by superconducting NbSe$_2$. Using local electrostatic gates, we define tunnel barriers and quantum dots at different distances from the lateral superconductor-semiconductor interface. The quantum dots serve as local spectroscopic probes of the proximitized BLG channel segment, forming tunable superconductor-quantum dot-normal conductor junction devices. Coulomb blockade and finite-bias spectroscopy reveal a proximity-induced superconducting gap of up to $80\,\mathrm{μeV}$ and allow to track its evolution with increasing distance from the NbSe$_2$ contact. We find that the local density of states remains suppressed over distances exceeding 1 $μ$m, consistent with superconducting proximity through a highly ballistic BLG channel. Our results show that BLG-superconductor hybrids offer a controllable platform where quantum dots and quantum point contacts can be well combined with superconductivity.

</details>


### [169] [Entropy-driven transitions between extended integer and fractional quantum Hall regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16483)
*Kyung-Su Kim,Steven A. Kivelson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文分析共存Wigner晶体序的电子态可在有限密度范围内表现出扩展量子霍尔（quantum Hall, QH）平台，并可与其他QH态竞争；Goldstone模式或软能隙模式的熵可驱动二者之间的一阶热转变，并被用于解释魔角菱面体石墨烯中的实验演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注无序缺失条件下仍可出现扩展量子霍尔平台这一现象，以及该扩展QH态与同一密度范围内其他QH态之间的竞争关系。作者试图理解有限温度下竞争QH区制之间的转变机制。

Method: 通过分析若干情形，考察Goldstone模式（如磁振子magnon或声子phonon）以及软能隙模式（如磁振子–旋子magnetoroton）所携带的熵如何驱动竞争QH区制之间的有限温度转变。并将该框架应用于魔角菱面体石墨烯（moiré rhombohedral graphene）。

Result: 分析表明，在分数量子反常霍尔（fractional quantum anomalous Hall）态中，软磁振子–旋子可为从扩展整数QH到分数QH区制的热演化提供一种可能的体态机制。

Conclusion: 共存Wigner晶体序的电子态，其金德斯通或软能隙模式的熵可驱动竞争QH区制间的一阶热转变；该框架可合理解释魔角菱面体石墨烯中观测到的热演化行为。

Abstract: Electronic states with coexisting Wigner-crystal order can sometimes exhibit a quantum Hall effect over a finite range of electron densities---i.e., exhibit ``extended'' quantum Hall (QH) plateaus in the absence of disorder. Such an extended quantum Hall state can then compete with other QH states over the same density range, allowing a first-order thermal transition between them. Here, we analyze several settings in which the entropy associated with Goldstone modes (e.g., magnons or phonons) or soft gapped modes (e.g., magnetoroton) drives a finite-temperature transition between competing QH regimes. Applying this framework to moiré rhombohedral graphene, we argue that a soft magnetoroton in a fractional quantum anomalous Hall state provides a plausible bulk mechanism for the observed thermal evolution from an extended integer QH to a fractional QH regime.

</details>


### [170] [Accurate noise spectroscopy using quantum sensing ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16643)
*Dhilan T Vallury,Nikolai Dontschuk,Alexander M Jakob,Alexander J Healey,David A Simpson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种利用系综相干性从动力学解耦（dynamical decoupling）测量中还原真实源噪声谱（source noise spectrum）的分析流程，解决了传感系综（sensing ensemble）内耦合可变导致的输出难解释问题。以金刚石浅层氮-空位色心（nitrogen-vacancy centres）为模型系统，从两个深度分布不同的传感系综中获得了 MHz 范围表面噪声谱特征的一致信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子传感器噪声谱学（noise spectroscopy）可用于表征传感器环境性质并探测临界磁现象，而以大规模系综替代单个传感器有望显著提升测量灵敏度与视场（field of view）。但当系综内各传感器与目标的耦合存在差异时，输出信号难以解释，这构成了量化噪声谱的主要障碍。

Method: 提出一套分析流程，基于动力学解耦测量所得的系综相干性（ensemble coherence）来获取真实源噪声谱。分析中指出序列时序（sequence timing）在系综噪声谱学中具有双重作用：既决定频率选择性（frequency selectivity），又决定系综内信号的加权采集方式。方法核心是引入耦合分布（coupling distribution）以实现噪声参数的准确提取。

Result: 将该流程应用于模型系统——金刚石中的浅层氮-空位色心，结果表明可从两个具有不同深度分布的传感系综中，获得关于 MHz 范围表面噪声谱特征的一致信息。

Conclusion: 该结果为使用大规模量子传感器系综开展定量、宽带（broadband）噪声谱学铺平了道路，并可直接推广至其他固态与分子传感平台。

Abstract: Noise spectroscopy with quantum sensors is a powerful tool for characterising the properties of the sensor environment and detecting critical magnetic phenomena. While replacing single sensors with large ensembles promises substantial gains in measurement sensitivity and field of view, the output becomes difficult to interpret when there is a variable coupling to targets across the sensing ensemble. In this work we present an analysis procedure for accessing the true source noise spectrum using ensemble coherence from dynamical decoupling measurements. We show that sequence timing plays a dual role in ensemble noise spectroscopy, setting both the frequency selectivity and the weighted collection of signal across the ensemble, and demonstrate how incorporation of the coupling distribution allows accurate parameter extraction of the noise. We apply this procedure to a model system, shallow nitrogen-vacancy centres in diamond, and show that consistent information about the spectral character of MHz-range surface noise can be obtained from two sensing ensembles featuring distinct depth distributions. Our results pave the way for using large quantum sensor ensembles for quantitative, broadband noise spectroscopy, with straightforward extension to other solid-state and molecular sensing platforms.

</details>


### [171] [1/f frequency noise in mechanical resonators scales inversely with volume, not with quality factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16668)
*M. L. Roukes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要指出机械频移传感器（mechanical frequency-shift sensor）始终未能达到其热机械（thermomechanical）或量子极限（quantum limit），并通过对已发表数据的统计检验，提出闪烁噪声（flicker noise）系数与器件体积（volume）之间存在不变量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有各类材料、尺寸与换能方案（transduction scheme）的机械频移传感器普遍存在比理论极限高出 1–3 个数量级的 $1/f$ 噪声底（$1/f$ floor），其闪烁噪声系数 $h_{-1} \propto 1/V_{\varepsilon}$，即噪声与体积相关的行为尚未被统一解释。

Method: 通过调研已发表记录（published record），对闪烁噪声系数 $h_{-1}$ 与有效体积 $V_{\varepsilon}$ 的乘积不变量 $h_{-1}V_{\varepsilon} \equiv \sigma_A^2 V_{\varepsilon}/(2\ln 2)$ 进行跨尺度统计检验，包括对体积无关噪声（volume-independent noise）假设和位点平均（site-averaging）假设的显著性检验，并考察品质因数（quality factor, $Q$）依赖性的 $Q^{-n}$ 规律。

Result: 在 13 个数量级（13 decades）范围内，$h_{-1}V_{\varepsilon}$ 不变量与器件尺寸无关；体积无关噪声假设被 9$\sigma$ 排除，位点平均假设被 3.8$\sigma$ 排除；未观察到 $Q$ 依赖性，当 $Q$ 与体积协变（co-vary）时 $Q^{-n}$ 规律等效缩放，而当二者不协变时仅体积缩放关系成立。质量分辨率（mass resolution）满足 $|\delta m| \propto \sqrt{h_{-1}V_{\varepsilon}\cdot V}$。

Conclusion: 机械频移传感器的 $1/f$ 噪声由体积标度的不变量主导，而非品质因数或位点平均机制；该不变量可作为预测质量分辨率的统一依据。

Abstract: Mechanical frequency-shift sensors have never reached their fundamental thermomechanical or quantum limits. Every material, size, and transduction scheme yet examined yields a $1/f$ floor 1-3 orders higher, with a flicker-noise coefficient $h_{-1} \propto 1/V_{\varepsilon}$. Surveying the published record, I find the invariant $h_{-1}\,V_{\varepsilon} \equiv σ_A ^2\, V_{\varepsilon}/(2\ln 2)$ independent of device size across 13 decades; volume-independent noise is excluded by 9$σ$, site-averaging by 3.8$σ$. No $Q$ dependence appears: reported $Q^{-n}$ laws scale equivalently when $Q$ and volume co-vary; only volume scaling survives when they do not. Mass resolution follows as $|δm| \propto \sqrt{h_{-1}\,V_{\varepsilon} \cdot V}$.

</details>


### [172] [Room-temperature 1/3 suppression of diffusive shot noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16783)
*Henri Vo Van Qui,Ignacio Madrid,Simon Grall,Thibaut Jonckheere,Akira Fujiwara,Laurent Jalabert,Masayuki Hashisaka,Christophe Demaille,Soo Hyeon Kim,Nicolas Clément*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作表明，微流控间隙中的二茂铁（ferrocene）氧化还原循环（redox cycling）是扩散导体普适 1/3 散粒噪声（shot noise）抑制的室温实现，并给出经实验验证的电流与噪声解析模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散导体（diffusive conductors）普遍存在 1/3 的散粒噪声抑制，可由量子散射（quantum scattering）、玻尔兹曼-朗之万动力学（Boltzmann-Langevin kinetics）或对称简单排他过程（symmetric simple exclusion process）导出；但相关实验局限于低温条件（cryogenic conditions），该普适性的理解仍不完整。该研究旨在室温条件下实现并验证这一普适性。

Method: 采用微流控间隙（microfluidic gap）中的二茂铁氧化还原循环体系，建立电流及其噪声的解析模型（analytical model），并与实验测量结果进行对照验证。

Result: 在扩散受限区（diffusion-limited regime）中重现了普适的 1/3 散粒噪声抑制，且解析模型与实验相符。

Conclusion: 该结果在介观物理（mesoscopic physics）与电化学（electrochemistry）之间建立了桥梁，为通过室温噪声测量探测分子动力学（molecular dynamics）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Diffusive conductors share a universal shot noise reduction of 1/3. This result can be obtained from quantum scattering, Boltzmann-Langevin kinetics, or by symmetric simple exclusion process, but experiments have been limited to cryogenic conditions and the understanding of this universality remains incomplete. Here we show that ferrocene redox cycling in a microfluidic gap is room temperature realization of this universality. An analytical model of the current and its noise is derived and confirmed experimentally. The universal 1/3 shot noise suppression is recovered in the diffusion-limited regime, providing a bridge between mesoscopic physics and electrochemistry, opening a new route for probing molecular dynamics through room-temperature noise measurements.

</details>


### [173] [Generation of pure spin currents via nonadiabatic quantum pumping in an antiferromagnetic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16819)
*Leila Eslami,Fatemeh Bourbour,Somaieh Ahmadi,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究基于紧束缚模型与非平衡格林函数方法，探讨反铁磁链在含相位差时变势驱动下的量子自旋泵浦，发现非绝热区可实现自旋通道分离，并可调控出近纯自旋流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索在无外部偏压条件下产生并控制自旋流（spin current）的可能性，并考察交换场（exchange field）与周期性驱动在自旋向上、自旋向下电流分离中的作用。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）描述系统，并运用Keldysh非平衡格林函数（Keldysh non-equilibrium Green's function）形式计算自旋分辨电流；在链两端施加具有特定相位差的两个时变势，同时令两电极化学势相等。

Result: 在绝热区（低频率）两个自旋通道响应几乎相同；随驱动频率升高进入非绝热区，能量量子吸收与发射被激活，自旋向上与向下电流出现显著差异。泵浦电流强烈依赖化学势，可据此调控自旋流的大小与方向；频率升高使自旋流增强，并可调节参数使电荷电流近于消失而保留可观自旋流。

Conclusion: 结果表明在反铁磁链中实现无净电荷转移的近纯自旋泵浦（pure spin pumping）具有可行性，为基于反铁磁系统的自旋泵浦器件设计提供了有前景的视角。

Abstract: In this study, quantum spin pumping in an antiferromagnetic chain driven by time-dependent potential is investigated. The aim is to explore the possibility of generating and controlling spin currents in the absence of external bias and to examine the role of exchange field and periodic driving in the separation of spin-up and spin-down currents. The system is described using a tight-binding model, and spin-resolved currents are calculated employing the Keldysh non-equilibrium Green's function formalism. Two time-dependent potentials with a specific phase difference are applied to the two ends of the chain, while the chemical potentials of both electrodes are set equal. The results demonstrate that in the adiabatic regime (low frequencies), the response of the two spin channels is nearly identical. However, as the driving frequency increases and the system enters the nonadiabatic regime, absorption and emission processes of energy quanta become activated, leading to significant differences between spin-up and spin-down currents. The pumped current exhibits strong dependence on the chemical potential, allowing for the control of both magnitude and direction of the spin current through its adjustment. With increasing frequency, the spin current enhances, and parameters can be tuned such that the charge current nearly vanishes while a considerable spin current persists. This finding indicates the feasibility of achieving nearly pure spin pumping without net charge transfer in the antiferromagnetic chain. The results provide a promising perspective for designing spin-pumping devices based on antiferromagnetic systems.

</details>


### [174] [Propulsion of magnetic domain walls via elastic damping in magnetostrictive materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16904)
*Miguel Moreno,Rocio Yanes,Luis Lopez-Diaz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过全耦合微磁-弹性动力学（micromagnetic-elastodynamic）框架与解析集体坐标（collective coordinates）模型，揭示了机械阻尼（mechanical damping）可作为非磁耗散通道，在无本征吉尔伯特阻尼（Gilbert damping，α=0）时仍维持磁畴壁（domain wall）的稳态运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性与弹性自由度（elastic degrees of freedom）的相互作用在先进自旋电子学（spintronics）技术中日益重要，磁致伸缩材料（magnetostrictive materials）中运动畴壁携带局域弹性形变，但其弹性形变及对畴壁动力学的反作用（back-action）机制尚待深入探究。

Method: 采用全耦合微磁-弹性动力学框架，并结合解析集体坐标模型，计算随畴壁共同移动的局域应力张量（stress tensor）分布，分析机械耗散对应力空间对称性的影响。

Result: 机械阻尼的作用等效于磁损耗（magnetic losses），可映射为吉尔伯特阻尼参数的有效磁弹贡献（α_me）；机械耗散打破应力分布的空间对称性，畴壁核心处局域应力梯度提供直接驱动力，平衡场致磁进动（magnetic precession），从而实现稳态运动。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了磁性与弹性子系统间基本的能量传递与弛豫机制，为通过应变工程（strain engineering）与机械阻尼设计调控磁结构动力学、发展声学自旋电子学（acoustic spintronic）器件提供了重要启示。
