Abstract: The interaction between magnetic and elastic degrees of freedom is increasingly central to advanced spintronics technology. In magnetostrictive materials, a moving domain wall carries a localized elastic deformation. Here, we investigate these elastic deformations and their back-action on field-driven domain wall dynamics using a fully coupled micromagnetic-elastodynamic framework and an analytical collective coordinates model. We show that mechanical damping acts as a non-magnetic dissipation channel, extracting energy from the magnetic texture and sustaining translational domain wall motion even in the complete absence of intrinsic Gilbert damping ($α=0$). Specifically, elastic damping impacts domain wall dynamics in a manner equivalent to magnetic losses, allowing us to map it onto an effective magnetoelastic contribution to the Gilbert damping parameter, $α_\text{me}$. We calculate the localized stress tensor profiles co-moving with the domain wall and show that mechanical dissipation breaks their spatial symmetry. Using our collective coordinates model, we demonstrate that these asymmetric stress profiles-specifically the localized stress gradients across the wall core-exert direct propelling forces that balance field-induced magnetic precession, enabling steady-state motion. Overall, these findings reveal a fundamental energy transfer and relaxation mechanism between magnetic and elastic subsystems, providing valuable insights for controlling magnetic texture dynamics via strain engineering and mechanical damping design in acoustic spintronic devices.

</details>


### [175] [Nonlinear Hall Effect in Altermagnetic Warped Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17001)
*Debashree Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过将六角翘曲（hexagonal warping）的拓扑绝缘体表面态与d波交替磁体（d-wave altermagnet）近邻耦合，打破空间镜像对称，使量子度规偶极（quantum metric dipole）成为非线性霍尔响应的唯一驱动，并可经门控与取向角连续调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维狄拉克系统（low-dimensional Dirac systems）为量子几何非线性输运提供平台，但镜像与时间反演对称性严格限制贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与量子度规偶极为零，制约了相关响应。

Method: 将具有六角翘曲的拓扑绝缘体（TI）表面态与d波交替磁体近邻耦合，借助C_{3v}翘曲与d波自旋劈裂的相互作用解除空间镜像对称；并通过旋转交替磁取向角φ进行调控分析。

Result: 对称性破缺产生有限贝里曲率偶极分量，但在电荷中性点附近受强烈抑制；量子度规偶极成为非线性霍尔响应的唯一来源，该响应与散射无关，在μ=0处消失并随化学势反演呈奇宇称；旋转φ可连续调节量子度规偶极并诱导符号反转，在φ=0附近达峰值、在φ=π/4处消失。

Conclusion: TI-交替磁体界面可作为门控与取向可调的量子几何自旋电子学（quantum metric spintronics）理想候选体系。

Abstract: Low-dimensional Dirac systems offer a versatile platform for quantum-geometric non-linear transport. Hexagonal warping in topological insulator (TI) surface states generates rich momentum-space geometric textures. However, mirror and time-reversal symmetries strictly constrain both the Berry curvature and quantum metric dipoles to zero. Here, we show that proximity-coupling a hexagonally warped TI to a d-wave altermagnet lifts spatial mirror symmetry via the interplay of C_{3v} warping and d-wave spin-splitting. This symmetry breaking enables finite Berry curvature dipole components, which, however, remain heavily suppressed near charge neutrality, establishing the intrinsic quantum metric dipole as the sole driver of the non-linear Hall response. This scattering-independent response vanishes at μ= 0 and exhibits odd parity under chemical potential inversion. Importantly, rotating the altermagnetic orientation angle φcontinuously tunes quantum metric dipole and induces a sign reversal, peaking near φ=0 and vanishing at φ= π/4. Our findings highlight TI-altermagnet interfaces as ideal candidates for gate- and orientation-tunable quantum metric spintronics.

</details>


### [176] [Self-balancing luminescence kinetics of charged excitons in WS$_2$ monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17289)
*A. I. Veretennikov,A. I. Galimov,M. M. Glazov,T. V. Shubina*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过WS$_2$单层的发射动力学（emission kinetics）测量，证明其丰富的激子与双激子谱线并非独立实体，而是构成一个随温度协同变化的单一相互作用系综（interacting ensemble）。暗三子（dark trion）以200–300皮秒时间尺度持续补充亮三子，并促成带电双激子（charged biexciton）的强发光；自洽速率方程模型（self-consistent rate equation model）成功复现了动力学随温度的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维钨基过渡金属二硫化物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMD）具有包含中性/带电激子（exciton）与双激子（biexciton）多条谱线的发射谱，但通常被视为彼此独立的实体。本研究旨在检验这些态是否实际构成一个相互作用的整体，并理解其在温度变化下的粒子数协调机制。

Method: 针对WS$_2$单层开展发射动力学研究，分析不同激子态在温度变化下的布居（population）与耗竭（depletion）行为，并建立自洽速率方程模型（self-consistent rate equation model）对实验动力学进行拟合与解释。

Result: 暗三子（dark trion）在200–300皮秒时间尺度上对亮三子（bright trion）产生长寿命贡献，且来自暗态的补充促进了带电双激子（charged biexciton）的强发光；所建自洽速率方程模型良好再现了动力学的温度依赖关系。

Conclusion: WS$_2$单层中的激子与双激子态构成一个随温度相互协调的单一相互作用系综，而非独立实体。该结果为在此类材料中调控激子态（controlling exciton states）提供了途径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional tungsten-based transition metal dichalcogenides exhibit a rich emission spectrum with distinct lines of exciton and biexciton states, neutral and charged, which are typically considered as independent entities. Here, we present a study of the emission kinetics of a WS$_2$ monolayer, demonstrating that these states together form a single interacting ensemble, with their population and depletion mutually coordinated with temperature. Dark trions make a long-lived contribution to the bright trion on a time scale of 200-300 picoseconds, and replenishment from dark states promotes intense luminescence of charged biexcitons. The developed self-consistent rate equation model reproduces the temperature dependence of the kinetics well, opening the way to controlling exciton states in such materials.

</details>


### [177] [Exact solutions of nonreciprocal Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model with domain walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17378)
*Tong Wang,Mengjie Yang,Flore K. Kunst*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作解析研究非厄米 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型中由界面拼接产生的畴壁物理，给出复波数的闭式量子化条件与局域态解，并推广至二维 Lieb 晶格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect）背景下畴壁（domain wall）界面态的解析刻画尚不充分的问题，本文旨在获得开边界条件下本征态的闭式解。

Method: 将两条耦合比相反的 SSH 链粘接，构造两种不同界面几何；基于对称性拟设（ansatz）推导复波数与波函数的闭式量子化条件，并用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）验证；进一步推广至二维 Lieb 晶格。

Result: 除常规趋肤模与拓扑零模外，畴壁还产生脱离体能开边界连续谱的有限能量局域态；二维情形下相互竞争的非互易性（nonreciprocity）形成可调的漏斗（funneling）区域，指向余维一与余维二界面。

Conclusion: 结果给出了非厄米畴壁态的可解框架，并将界面构造拓展至更高维，揭示非互易性调控的定向局域与漏斗效应。

Abstract: We investigate domain-wall physics in a non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model featuring the non-Hermitian skin effect and its higher-dimensional extensions, focusing on analytical eigenstate solutions under open boundary conditions. By gluing two Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains with inverted coupling ratios together, we identify two distinct interface geometries, and derive closed-form quantization conditions for the complex wave number and wave functions using symmetry-based ansatzes. Besides conventional skin modes and topological zero modes, the domain walls generate additional localized states at finite energy that detach from the bulk open-boundary-condition continuum. We validate the analytical results by exact diagonalization, and further generalize the interface construction to a two-dimensional Lieb lattice, where competing nonreciprocities produce tunable funneling regimes towards codimension-one and codimension-two interfaces.

</details>


### [178] [Visualizing Berry curvature in a Floquet-Chern insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17500)
*Younsik Kim,Rishi Acharya,Hannah E. Aguirre,Lucca Figari,Daniel P. Shoemaker,Fahad Mahmood*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用圆二色性时间与角分辨光电子能谱（circular-dichroism time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, Tr-ARPES），首次直接解析了 Bi₂Se₃ 表面 Floquet-Bloch 态的光诱导贝里曲率（Berry curvature），确立了动量分辨贝里曲率映射作为光诱导拓扑与相干 Floquet 能带形成的直接探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振光可在无带隙狄拉克（Dirac）体系中打开拓扑能隙并形成 Floquet-Chern 绝缘体，但其贝里曲率此前尚未被直接解析，缺乏对光诱导拓扑的直接实验探测手段。

Method: 采用圆二色性 Tr-ARPES 测量 Bi₂Se₃ 表面 Floquet-Bloch 态；通过比较相反螺旋度（helicity）中红外泵浦下的谱线，分离出诱导的几何响应，并与周期性驱动狄拉克哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的计算结果进行对照。

Result: 在 Floquet 能隙处观测到交替符号的贝里曲率特征，其重复间隔由泵浦光子能量决定，并随泵浦场强重新分布，与计算一致；诱导贝里曲率与 Floquet 狄拉克能隙的衰减均快于复制谱（replica）强度，表明相干 Floquet 杂化减弱后谱复制仍可存续。

Conclusion: 动量分辨的贝里曲率映射可作为光诱导拓扑与相干 Floquet 能带形成的直接探测手段。

Abstract: Circularly polarized light can open a topological gap in a gapless Dirac system, creating a Floquet-Chern insulator. However, its Berry curvature has not been directly resolved. Here we use circular-dichroism time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (Tr-ARPES) to map the light-induced Berry curvature of Floquet-Bloch states on the surface of Bi$_2$Se$_3$. By comparing spectra for opposite helicities of the mid-infrared pump, we isolate the induced geometric response. We observe alternating-sign Berry-curvature features at Floquet gaps that repeat at intervals set by the pump photon energy and redistribute with pump field strength in agreement with calculations for a periodically driven Dirac Hamiltonian. Both the induced Berry-curvature and the Floquet Dirac gap decay faster than the replica intensity, showing that spectral replicas can persist even after coherent Floquet hybridization weakens. These results establish momentum-resolved Berry-curvature mapping as a direct probe of light-induced topology and coherent Floquet band formation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Two-Dimensional Materials toward CMOS-Compatible Scalable Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16172)
*Mara Lieberegts,Ye Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以展望（Perspective）形式评估二维（2D）材料在CMOS兼容量子硬件中的应用机会与局限，聚焦其能否缓解制造工艺对量子比特性能的制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展固态量子处理器（solid-state quantum processors）面临可制造性（manufacturability）、量子比特性能、控制、连通性、低温运行与容错等多重约束；制造过程中引入的化学残留、结构无序与非均匀界面会损害量子比特相干性（qubit coherence）。

Method: 采用展望（Perspective）类分析，结合二维材料原子级薄晶体与范德华界面（van der Waals interfaces）的特性，并以其作为下一代CMOS沟道材料（channel materials）的并行研发为背景，考察相关工艺能否迁移至量子芯片。

Result: 二维材料的原子级薄结构与范德华界面被视为应对部分制造相关挑战的潜在途径，且其作为下一代CMOS沟道材料的同步发展，为工艺向量子芯片转移提供了动机与可行性讨论基础。

Conclusion: 该展望系统评估了二维材料用于CMOS兼容量子硬件的机遇与局限，指出其与CMOS工艺协同发展的潜在价值，但未给出具体实验结论。

Abstract: Manufacturability is one of several constraints on developing solid-state quantum processors, along with qubit performance, control, connectivity, cryogenic operation, and fault tolerance. Chemical residues, structural disorder, and non-uniform interfaces introduced during fabrication can compromise qubit coherence. Two-dimensional (2D) materials, with their atomically thin crystals and van der Waals interfaces, offer routes to address some of these challenges. Their concurrent development as next-generation CMOS channel materials motivates an examination of whether relevant processes can be transferred to quantum chips. This Perspective evaluates the opportunities and limitations of 2D materials for CMOS-compatible quantum hardware.

</details>


### [180] [Breaking the Wiedemann-Franz limit in thermoelectrics via separated flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16261)
*Illia Serhiienko,Fabian Garmroudi,Stefan Enzner,Izem Dural,Giorgio Sangiovanni,Andrej Pustogow*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出通过能量依赖散射（energy-dependent scattering）调控电子输运，使电子热导（thermal conductivity）相对电导（electrical conductivity）被抑制；以单层 Ni₃In 的平带（flat band）为例，并通过对约 5×10⁴ 组数据的 Wiedemann-Franz 图分析识别 L≪L₀ 的报道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体中电荷与自旋自由度的调控是凝聚态物理（condensed-matter science）的核心问题，而金属导电的标志性规律 Wiedemann-Franz 定律（Wiedemann-Franz law）意味着移动电荷携带熵，使电荷与热输运难以解耦。尽管研究已久，如何将电荷输运与热输运分离仍未解决。

Method: 通过能量依赖散射（energy-dependent scattering）将电子输运约束在费米能（Fermi energy）附近非对称的箱车型（boxcar-type）分布中，从而在抑制电子热导的同时兼顾大 Seebeck 系数与电导。以含两个费米能附近平带（flat bands）的单层 Ni₃In 为例，提出平带体系作为散射相空间工程（scattering phase space engineering）的可调平台；并对约 5×10⁴ 组数据构建 zT 对 S² 的 Wiedemann-Franz 图进行大规模评估。

Result: 在非对称箱车型分布约束下，可实现大 Seebeck 系数与高电导的同时并存，而电子热导被抑制。基于单层 Ni₃In 的双平带特征，平带体系被证明是散射相空间工程的有前景且可调的平台；Wiedemann-Franz 图分析为从文献中识别 L≪L₀ 的报道提供了有效工具，可指向新的物理机制或被忽视的测量假象。

Conclusion: 研究提出通过能量依赖散射降低电子热导相对电导之比的直接路径，并将平带体系定位为散射相空间工程的可调平台。同时指出 Wiedemann-Franz 图（zT vs. S²）可作为甄别 L≪L₀ 报道的规模化工具，区分新物理机制与测量假象。

Abstract: Controlling the charge and spin degrees of freedom of electrons in solids has been at the heart of condensed-matter science for centuries. One hallmark of metallic conduction is the Wiedemann-Franz law which implies that a moving charge carries entropy. Despite centuries of dedicated research, disentangling charge and heat transport has remained an unsolved issue so far. Here we present a direct route to reduce thermal conductivity of conduction electrons with respect to their electrical conductivity via energy-dependent scattering. By constraining electronic transport to a boxcar-type distribution asymmetrically around the Fermi energy, a large Seebeck coefficient and electrical conductivity can be realized simultaneously while electronic heat conduction is suppressed. Based on the case of monolayer Ni$_3$In, which comprises two flat bands around the Fermi energy, we propose flat-band systems as a promising, tunable platform for scattering phase space engineering. Besides this novel approach, our large-scale assessment of $\approx 5\times 10^4$ data sets in a 'Wiedemann-Franz plot' of $zT$ vs. $S^2$ provides an effective tool to identify reports of $L\ll L_0$ from literature - pointing towards either new and interesting physical mechanisms - or overlooked measurement artifacts.

</details>


### [181] [Domain-wall energy governs coercivity in ordered and disordered additively manufactured Fe--49Co--2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16343)
*Dennis Boakye,Chuang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过在同一晶粒尺寸下对比增材制造（additive manufacturing）与变形（wrought）Fe–Co 合金，证明其过量硬度并非来自 B2 化学有序（chemical ordering），而源自残余缺陷（retained defect content）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造软磁（soft-magnetic）Fe–Co 始终比同成分变形材料更硬，这一过量硬度（excess）是打印电机（electrical machines）的核心障碍。文献将其归因于 B2 化学有序，但该假设未经验证：有序态的各向异性（anisotropy）未曾测量，且打印材料与变形材料从未在匹配晶粒尺寸（matched grain size）下比较。

Method: 以已知有序度（order parameter）的变形 Fe–49Co–2V 进行标定，由此确定有序态畴壁能（domain-wall energy），并对所有独立于晶粒尺寸的钉扎通道（pinning channel）设定上限；随后在匹配晶粒尺寸下对比打印与变形材料，并考察反相畴（antiphase-domain）尺寸变化对矫顽力（coercivity）的影响。

Result: 有序化对硬度的贡献比实测过量低数个数量级（orders of magnitude）；反相畴尺寸十倍变化不改变矫顽力。有序化所控制的是畴壁能，且该效应小且有界（bounded）。

Conclusion: 过量硬度归因于残余缺陷含量，因此工艺优化方向应从打印后退火（post-build annealing）转向打印过程（in-build）中的缺陷控制。

Abstract: Additively manufactured soft-magnetic Fe--Co is consistently harder than wrought material of the same composition, and controlling that excess is the central obstacle to printing electrical machines from these alloys. The literature attributes it to B2 chemical ordering, untested because the anisotropy of the ordered state had not been measured for this alloy and because printed material had never been compared with wrought material at matched grain size. In this study we supply both. Calibrating on wrought Fe--49Co--2V at a known order parameter fixes the ordered-state domain-wall energy and caps every pinning channel independent of grain size. Ordering then falls orders of magnitude short of the measured excess, and a tenfold change in antiphase-domain size leaves coercivity unchanged. What ordering controls is the wall energy, and that effect is small and bounded. The excess is retained defect content, which moves process optimization from post-build annealing to in-build defect control.

</details>


### [182] [Full Minimal Coupling All-Electron Real-Time TDDFT for X-Ray-Matter Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16441)
*Daniel Schacher,Tod A. Pascal,Craig P. Schwartz,Keith V. Lawler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在 FHI-aims 中实现了全电子、以数值原子为中心的轨道基组下的完整最小耦合（full minimal coupling）实时含时密度泛函理论（RT-TDDFT），保留矢势的空间结构，并扩展至周期体系，用于 X 射线波段的光-物质相互作用模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RT-TDDFT 通过空间均匀（偶极）矢势耦合光与物质，当光子波长接近原子间距时（即 X 射线波段）该近似失效，因此需要超越偶极近似、保留光子动量的处理方法。

Method: 采用全电子数值原子中心轨道实现完整最小耦合，在顺磁（paramagnetic）与抗磁（diamagnetic）耦合中均保留矢势空间结构；实现倒空间分辨的电流诊断量；并通过微扰光子动量边带耦合（photon-momentum sideband coupling）将传播扩展至周期体系，以恢复光子波矢处的密度响应。

Result: 四个能量范围从 100 eV 到 5 keV 的基准测试验证了该实现并检验其适用范围；保留的光子动量在整个过程中具有物理意义，既带来可测量的超越偶极修正，也开启了偶极极限下禁戒的通道。

Conclusion: 在全电子基础上，该实现为跨分子、表面与固体的 X 射线光-物质相互作用通用从头算处理奠定了基础。

Abstract: Standard real-time time-dependent density functional theory (RT-TDDFT) couples light to matter through a spatially uniform (dipole) vector potential, an approximation that breaks down in the X-ray regime, where the photon wavelength approaches interatomic scales. We present an all-electron, numeric-atom-centered-orbital implementation of full minimal coupling in FHI-aims that retains the spatial structure of the vector potential in both the paramagnetic and diamagnetic couplings, implements a reciprocal-space-resolved current diagnostic, and extends the propagation to periodic systems through a perturbative photon-momentum sideband coupling that recovers the density response at the photon wavevector. Four benchmarks ranging in energy from 100 eV to 5 keV validate the implementation and exercise its reach. The retained photon momentum proves physically consequential throughout, from measurable beyond dipole corrections to the enabling of channels forbidden in the dipole limit. On an all-electron footing, the implementation lays the groundwork for a general ab initio treatment of X-ray light-matter interaction across molecules, surfaces, and solids.

</details>


### [183] [Scaling LLM Agents for Materials Design through Hierarchical Collective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16466)
*Jaehwan Choi,Yousung Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: HiMatGen 是一个基于 GPT-5.6 Terra 构建的分层集体推理（hierarchical collective reasoning）框架，通过 100 名研究者组成讨论小组（discussion pods）与工具增强的领域代表进行双向证据交换，实现按自然语言设计指令生成满足功能与稳定性约束的晶体材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料设计需在相互竞争的功能需求与稳定性、合成约束之间取得平衡；生成模型虽能产出稳定新颖晶体，但难以遵循详细的自然语言设计指令，且随着智能体（agent）规模增长，科学知识与计算证据的持续交换成为难题。

Method: HiMatGen 以 GPT-5.6 Terra 为基础，将 100 名研究者按十个科学领域组织为讨论小组，并配备工具增强的领域代表；代表负责调查提案、跨领域交换证据，并将发现与未解决问题反馈至小组，该双向交流将专家分歧转化为结构性修改与替代设计。

Result: 在六个化学体系中，HiMatGen 产生的最终晶体候选数量为工具增强的单智能体基线（GPT-6 Astra）的 1.9 倍，稳定、独特且新颖（stable, unique and novel, SUN）结构数量为 1.7 倍；在性质导向任务中，完整工作流优于同模型单智能体与独立生成对照；与 MatterGen 和 Chemeleon2 的比较显示其能生成满足联合功能与化学要求的多样化晶体；在相同提案初始化的配对比较中，分层讨论使辩论阶段 token 使用量相对完全辩论减少 22 倍。

Conclusion: HiMatGen 提供了一种在智能体规模增长过程中持续交换科学知识与计算证据的迭代式材料设计方法，可将自然语言设计指令与功能、化学约束结合，支撑多样化晶体的开发。

Abstract: Materials design must reconcile competing functional requirements with stability and synthesis constraints. Generative models can produce stable, novel crystals, but accommodating detailed natural-language design instructions remains challenging. Here we introduce HiMatGen, a framework for scaling large language model agents through hierarchical collective reasoning. Built from GPT-5.6 Terra, HiMatGen connects 100 investigators in discussion pods across ten scientific domains with tool-enabled domain representatives. Representatives investigate proposals, exchange evidence across domains and return findings and unresolved questions to their pods. This bidirectional exchange turns specialist disagreements into structural revisions and alternative designs. Across six chemical systems, HiMatGen produces 1.9 times as many final crystal candidates and 1.7 times as many stable, unique and novel (SUN) structures as a tool-enabled GPT-6 Astra single-agent baseline. In property-directed tasks, the complete HiMatGen workflow outperforms same-model single-agent and independent-generation controls. Comparisons with MatterGen and Chemeleon2 demonstrate its ability to develop diverse crystals satisfying joint functional and chemical requirements. In a paired comparison initialized from the same proposals, hierarchical discussion reduces debate-stage token usage by a factor of 22 relative to full debate. HiMatGen provides an approach to materials design that sustains the exchange of scientific knowledge and computational evidence throughout iterative exploration as the agent population grows.

</details>


### [184] [Symbolic Ensemble Learning Enables Discovery of Fast Accurate Physics-Based Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16526)
*Bilvin Varughese,Aditya Koneru,Adil Muhammad,Troy D. Loeffler,Sukriti Manna,Jan Michael Y. Carrillo,Orcun Yildiz,Thomas Peterka,Subramanian K. R. S. Sankaranarayanan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出混合符号-神经网络（symbolic-neural）框架，将嵌入原子法（EAM）的可解释性与数据驱动学习结合，用符号回归得到铝的多组可解释势，并通过加权符号集成获得优于单一模型的复合势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习势（machine learning force fields）虽具第一性原理精度，但高维回归与物理可解释性之间存在鸿沟；传统解析势（analytical potentials）透明却难以描述远离基态（far-from-ground state）的复杂区域。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（DFT）数据训练方程学习神经网络（Equation Learner Neural Networks, EqNNs），采用三种训练协议获得铝的符号模型：随机初始化结合蒙特卡洛树搜索（Monte Carlo Tree Search, MCTS）与梯度下降，以及两种由铜势初始化的迁移学习（transfer learning）策略（MCTS+梯度下降、仅梯度下降）；再以加权符号集成（weighted symbolic ensemble）整合这些函数形式。

Result: 三种符号模型均达到低于 10 meV/atom 的精度，但处于函数景观中不同的局部极小（local minima），在声子色散（phonon dispersion）、表面能学与弹性响应上呈互补权衡；集成后的复合势在状态方程曲率、声子谱（phonon spectra）与熔化动力学（melting dynamics）上对 DFT 基准的一致性优于各组成模型。

Conclusion: 将迁移学习与集成符号回归（ensemble symbolic regression）结合，可得到紧凑、透明的势函数，在平衡与非平衡态下均具稳健预测能力，同时缓解个体偏差。

Abstract: Machine learning has transformed materials simulation by delivering force fields with ab initio accuracy, yet bridging the gap between high-dimensional regression and physical interpretability remains a grand challenge. Conventional analytical potentials offer transparency but often fail to capture the complexity of far-from-ground state regimes. Here, we introduce a hybrid symbolic-neural framework that unifies the interpretability of the Embedded Atom Method (EAM) with the adaptability of data-driven learning. Using Equation Learner Neural Networks (EqNNs) trained on density functional theory (DFT) data, we obtain interpretable models for aluminum through three distinct training protocols: random initialization trained via Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and gradient descent, and two transfer-learning strategies initialized from a copper potential - one employing MCTS followed by gradient descent, and the other using gradient descent only. We find that while all three resulting symbolic models achieve sub-10 meV/atom accuracy, they occupy distinct local minima in the functional landscape, exhibiting complementary trade-offs across phonon dispersion, surface energetics, and elastic response. By integrating these diverse functional forms through a weighted symbolic ensemble, we derive a composite potential that surpasses the fidelity of its constituent models. The resulting ensemble effectively mitigates individual biases, delivering superior consistency with DFT benchmarks across equation-of-state curvature, phonon spectra, and melting dynamics. This approach demonstrates that combining transfer learning with ensemble symbolic regression yields compact, transparent potentials capable of robust prediction across equilibrium and non-equilibrium states.

</details>


### [185] [Orbital-resolved superexchange and topological magnon bands in MXene Fe$_2$C](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16530)
*Qiang Gao,Wei Wang,Shaojin Qi,Guimei Shi,Anbang Guo,Xin Jin,Chen Shen,Xing-Qiu Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以第一性原理结合线性自旋波方法计算 MXene Fe$_2$C 的磁振子色散，揭示了由层间交换耦合解除简并的能带中 K 点 Dirac 点及拓扑性质，并通过微扰论与紧束缚模型阐明轨道分辨的各向异性超交换机制与磁振子拓扑之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性 MXene（magnetic MXenes）在自旋电子学（spintronics）中具有应用前景，但其磁振子能带（magnon bands）的拓扑性质与超交换机制（superexchange mechanism）仍研究不足。该工作旨在填补这一空白，明确轨道分辨的各向异性超交换与磁振子拓扑之间的关系。

Method: 采用线性自旋波方法（linear spin wave method）计算 MXene Fe$_2$C 的自旋波色散，交换耦合参数由第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）获得；利用两能带模型（two-band model）计算贝里曲率（Berry curvature）、谷陈数（valley Chern number）与边缘态（edge states），并推导受 $C_{3v}$ 旋转对称性保护的有效哈密顿量；进一步结合微扰论（perturbation theory）与紧束缚方法（tight-binding approach）构建精细模型以追溯交换耦合的微观起源。

Result: 两个 Fe 子晶格（Fe sub-lattices）的错位堆叠使层间交换耦合解除铁磁磁振子模式的简并；在磁振子能带 $K$ 点识别出 Dirac 点。拓扑量（贝里曲率、谷陈数与边缘态）被成功计算，且由 $C_{3v}$ 旋转对称性保护。机制上，铁磁超交换耦合由两个不同 Fe 位的 d 轨道经由桥接 C 位的一对正交 p 轨道的虚跳跃介导：层内交换耦合由面内 $p_x$、$p_y$ 轨道主导，层间交换耦合则源自面外 $p_z$ 轨道。

Conclusion: 研究在磁性 MXene Fe$_2$C 中建立了轨道分辨的各向异性超交换与磁振子能带拓扑性质之间的直接联系，可为未来自旋电子学应用提供理论指导。

Abstract: Magnetic MXenes are promising candidates for future spintronics due to their intriguing properties. Nevertheless, the topological properties of magnon bands and superexchange mechanism remain relatively underexplored. In this work, we have calculated the spin wave dispersions for MXene Fe$_2$C using the linear spin wave method, with the exchange coupling parameters obtained from first-principles calculations. Owing to the staggered stacking of two Fe sub-lattices, the interlayer exchange coupling lifts the degeneracy of the ferromagnetic magnon modes. A Dirac point is identified in the magnon bands at the $K$ point. The topological properties of magnon bands, including Berry curvature, valley Chern number and edge states, are further computed by means of a two-band model. We also derive an effective Hamiltonian to explain the magnonic topology, which is protected by the $C_{3v}$ rotational symmetry. To understand the microscopic origin of the exchange couplings that govern the magnon bands, we then construct a refined model integrating perturbation theory and the tight-binding approach. The ferromagnetic superexchange coupling is mediated by the virtual hopping between the d orbitals of two distinct Fe sites via a pair of orthogonal p orbitals at the bridging C site. In detail, the intralayer exchange coupling is governed by the in-plane $p_x$ and $p_y$ orbitals, while the out-of-plane $p_z$ orbital is responsible for the interlayer exchange coupling. Our results establish a direct link between the orbital-resolved anisotropic superexchange and topological properties of the magnon bands in magnetic MXene Fe$_2$C, providing theoretical guidance for future spintronics applications.

</details>


### [186] [Exotic centrosymmetric phase of acentric urea under high pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16545)
*Haw-Tyng Huang,Yedukondalu Neelam,Mei-Shuan Cheng,Zhenxian Liu,Lkhamsuren Bayarjargal,Rachel Husband,Anna Pakhomova,John B. Parise,Lars Ehm*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合粉末X射线衍射、红外与拉曼光谱、二次谐波产生（second harmonic generation, SHG）测量（最高20 GPa）及从头算晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP），揭示了尿素在高压下的多晶型序列，并证明高压相V'为中心对称结构，同时提出相X为相III与相V'之间的量子无序中间态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尿素作为一种简单的超分子晶体原型，因N-H-O氢键的断裂与重建在低压下呈现丰富的多晶型。其非中心对称高压相V'被推测结晶为中心对称结构，且可能具有类似对称冰相X的奇异结构，因此成为具有吸引力的研究对象。

Method: 采用粉末X射线衍射、红外与拉曼光谱、二次谐波产生（SHG）测量（压力至20 GPa），并结合基于约束进化方法的从头算晶体结构预测（ab initio CSP）。

Result: SHG信号在10 GPa转变点显著下降，证实高压相为中心对称，并获得晶格振动模式选择定则的支持。结构演化序列为：相I（P421m；Z=2）0-0.5 GPa，相III（P212121；Z=4）0.5-5.2 GPa，相V'（P21/m；Z=6）10.0 GPa以上，后者在能量上与理论预测的相V（Pnma；Z=4）具竞争力。相X在5.2-10.0 GPa间形成，具独特光谱与衍射特征。N-H振动软化及氢键相关振动强度变化为相X中的质子隧穿与电荷转移相互作用提供了证据。

Conclusion: 尿素高压多晶型演化经由相I、相III、相X至相V'，其中相X可解释为相III与相V'之间的量子无序中间态，归因于极端压缩环境下氢键网络难以被破坏。

Abstract: Urea is a simple prototype supramolecular crystal that exhibits rich polymorphism at low pressure due to broken and restored N-H-O hydrogen bonds. The high pressure polymorph (phase V') of acentric urea crystallizes in a centrosymmetric structure, which presents an appealing target because of its potential exotic structure, analogous to the symmetric ice phase X. The pressure-induced polymorphism of urea was studied using powder X-ray diffraction, infrared and Raman spectroscopy, second harmonic generation (SHG) measurements up to 20 GPa and ab initio crystal structure prediction (CSP) based on the constrained evolutionary approach. A strong decrease of the SHG signal at the transition pressure 10 GPa reveals that the high-pressure polymorph is indeed centrosymmetric, further confirmed by the selection rules observed in the lattice vibration modes, in contrast to chemical intuition for acentric urea. The structural evolution sequence obtained from X-ray diffraction, SHG and CSP calculations is as follows: phase I (P421m; Z=2) from 0 to 0.5 GPa, Phase III (P212121; Z=4) from 0.5 to 5.2 GPa, and phase V' (P21/m; Z=6) beyond 10.0 GPa which is energetically competitive with the theoretically predicted phase V (Pnma; Z=4). A phase X with distinct spectral and diffraction features forms between 5.2 and 10.0 GPa, which could be explained by a quantum disorder intermediate state between phase III and V', that is ascribed to the difficulty to disrupt the H-bonding network under extremely compressed environment. The softening of N-H vibrations and the change in intensity of the vibrations associated with the hydrogen bonding provide evidence for proton tunneling and charge-transfer interaction in phase X.

</details>


### [187] [Origin and Reduction of Coercive Fields in ZnO-based Wurtzite Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16570)
*Abhijeet Dhakane,Alireza Sepehrinezhad,William Prudnick,Jon-Paul Maria,Bobby Sumpter,Adri C. T. van Duin,Kyle P. Kelley,P. Ganesh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用大规模反应分子动力学（reactive molecular dynamics）模拟 ZnO 的场驱动极化反转，指出矫顽场由反转边界丝状体前端（filament head）的未屏蔽局域极序决定，并通过电荷调控与异质结构设计将 E$_c$ 降低约 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤锌矿铁电体（wurtzite ferroelectrics）可兼容半导体工艺，但大矫顽场（coercive field, E$_c$）压缩了极化反转、漏电与介电击穿之间的工作窗口；尽管理解到原子级局域、非经典开关前沿，决定矫顽场的微观特征仍未明确。

Method: 采用以第一性原理（first-principles）数据参数化的大规模反应分子动力学，追踪本征、Mg 修饰及异质结构 ZnO 中的场驱动反转；并分别施加仅应变与仅电荷掺杂的扰动，以及构建 ZnO/ZnMgO 层状结构进行对比分析。

Result: 反转通过崎岖的反转边界丝状体推进，与其他纤锌矿铁电体的观测一致；开关所需电场由推进中的丝状体前端控制，该前端是欠配位重构的反转边界区域，具有较大的场取向局域极序振幅，仅被反向取向的周围壳层部分补偿。电荷重分布比应变更有效降低矫顽场，因其抑制前端未屏蔽局域极序，二者联合使 E$_c$ 下降约 50%；层状 ZnO/ZnMgO 架构还产生埋入式成核位点并缩短丝状体传播长度。

Conclusion: 研究将原子尺度开关拓扑（switching topology）与材料设计原则相联系，为显著降低 ZnO 基纤锌矿铁电体的矫顽场提供依据。

Abstract: Wurtzite ferroelectrics offer a route to semiconductor-compatible non-volatile devices, but their large coercive fields (E$_c$) constrain the operating window between polarization reversal, leakage and dielectric breakdown. Atomically localized and non-classical switching fronts have recently been identified in wurtzite nitrides, yet the microscopic feature that sets the coercive field remains unresolved. Here we use large-scale reactive molecular dynamics, parameterized against first-principles data, to follow field-driven reversal in pristine, Mg-modified and heterostructured ZnO. Reversal proceeds through rugged inversion-boundary filaments in agreement with recent observations in other wurtzite ferroelectrics. We find that the field required for switching is controlled by the advancing filament head, which is a under-coordinated reconstructed inversion-boundary region during the transient switching dynamics with a large field-aligned local polar-order amplitude that is only partially compensated by an oppositely oriented surrounding shell. Controlled strain-only and charge-doping-only perturbations show that charge redistribution lowers the coercive field more effectively than strain because it suppresses this field-setting unscreened local polar order at the filament head, with the combined effect lowering E$_c$ by $\sim$ 50 \%. Layered ZnO/ZnMgO architectures further create buried nucleation sites and shorten filament propagation lengths. These results connect atomistic switching topology to a materials-design principle for substantially reducing coercive fields in ZnO-based wurtzite ferroelectrics.

</details>


### [188] [Nonlinear Photocurrent Spectroscopy and Polarization-Tunable Shift Current in the Layered Semiconductor $\mathrm{CuScP_2S_6}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16577)
*Alexander M. Blackston,Md Kazi Rokunuzzaman,Ryan P. Siebenaller,Shashu Tomar,Enam Chowdhury,Michael A. Susner,Roberto C. Myers*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报告了层状金属硫代/硒代磷酸盐（metal thiophosphates and selenophosphates）材料 CuScP₂S₆ 的单光子吸收（1PA）与双光子吸收（2PA）诱导光电流，确认其为准电子学活性的非中心对称半导体，并揭示其体光伏效应（BPVE）源于移位电流（shift current）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状金属硫代磷酸盐与硒代磷酸盐正成为非线性光子学与光电子学应用的潜在候选材料，但需进一步验证具体化合物的光电活性及其物理机制。研究动机在于确立 CuScP₂S₆ 作为光电活性非中心对称半导体的地位，并阐明其光电流的产生机理。

Method: 采用光谱分辨的单光子吸收与双光子吸收光响应测量，并结合光致发光（photoluminescence）与差分反射（differential reflectance）测量；同时构建垂直石墨/CuScP₂S₆/石墨器件，考察零偏压光电流及其对光子能量与 ab 面内光学偏振取向的依赖。

Result: 测得带隙为 2.35 eV，并观测到若干更高能量的带间跃迁以及亚带隙多光子激子跃迁。垂直石墨/CuScP₂S₆/石墨器件表现出零偏压光电流，其极性由光子能量与 ab 面内光学偏振取向共同调控。该极性依赖性与从线性到平方根的特征幂律转变，与移位电流机制导致的体光伏效应相符。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了 CuScP₂S₆ 作为偏振敏感非线性光电子学多功能平台的地位。

Abstract: The class of layered metal thiophosphates and selenophosphates are emerging as promising candidates for non-linear photonic and optoelectronic applications. Here we report one-photon absorption (1PA) and two-photon absorption (2PA) induced photocurrents in $CuScP_2S_6$, establishing it as an optoelectronically-active non-centrosymmetric semiconductor. Spectrally-resolved 1PA and 2PA photoresponsivity paired with photoluminescence and differential reflectance measurements reveal a band gap of 2.35 eV, several higher energy interband transitions, and sub-band gap multiphoton excitonic transitions. Vertical graphite/$CuScP_2S_6$/graphite devices exhibit zero-bias photocurrent with a polarity controlled by both photon energy and optical polarization orientation within the ab-plane. This sign-dependence combined with a characteristic power-law transition from linear to square-root scaling are consistent with a shift-current origin to this bulk photovoltaic effect (BPVE). These findings establish $CuScP_2S_6$ as a multi-functional platform for polarization-sensitive non-linear optoelectronics.

</details>


### [189] [Development of a Physics-Informed Neural Framework, MEOWN, for Rapid Prediction of Muon Stopping Sites in Crystalline Materials, for understanding Quantum Magnet employing Muon Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17063)
*A. Pandey,K. Sharma,S. Ghosh,G. Roy,T. Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出 MEOWN（Muon Engine for Optimized Weighted Networks），一种基于机器学习、融入物理信息的计算框架，用于预测晶体固体中能量上有利的 μ 子（muon）停止位点。通过结合可极化未扰动静电势（P-UEP）模型、机器学习引导优化与对称性驱动弛豫，该方法在保持物理一致性的同时显著降低计算成本，并在 MnSi、CoF2、CaF2、LiF、NaF 等基准材料上验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: μ 子自旋旋转/弛豫（muon-spin rotation/relaxation）是研究量子材料磁序、自旋动力学与超导性的重要微观探针，其定量解释关键取决于 μ 子停止位点的准确识别。传统方法依赖密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）结构弛豫，计算需求高、耗时长且需大量计算，因而亟需更高效可靠的替代方案。

Method: MEOWN 是一个基于机器学习且融入物理信息的计算框架，将可极化未扰动静电势（Polarizable Unperturbed Electrostatic Potential, P-UEP）模型与机器学习引导优化、对称性驱动弛豫相结合，用于预测晶体固体中的 μ 子停止位点。该工作流显式纳入静电相互作用、电子屏蔽、极化效应与零点运动（zero-point motion），以提供物理可解释的预测。

Result: 在 MnSi、CoF2、CaF2、LiF 和 NaF 等成熟基准材料上，MEOWN 预测的 μ 子停止位点与偶极场（dipolar fields）与既有 DFT+mu 结果高度一致，表明该方法在化学多样性体系中具有可靠性与可迁移性。MEOWN 可在数分钟内预测平衡 μ 子停止位点，相较传统 DFT 计算实现显著加速。

Conclusion: 该文呈现了 MEOWN 的理论基础、计算方法与验证结果，表明其可作为通用软件用于晶体材料中快速且可靠的 μ 子停止位点预测。

Abstract: Muon-spin rotation/relaxation is one of the most powerful microscopic probes for understanding magnetic order, spin dynamics, superconductivity, and complex magnetic phases in emerging quantum materials. Quantitative interpretation depends critically on the accurate identification of the muon stopping site, a problem traditionally addressed using density functional theory-based structural relaxation. However, conventional DFT approaches are computationally demanding, time-consuming, and require extensive calculations. Here, we develop MEOWN (Muon Engine for Optimized Weighted Networks), a machine-learning-based, physics-informed computational framework that combines a Polarizable Unperturbed Electrostatic Potential (P-UEP) model with machine-learning-guided optimization and symmetry-driven relaxation to predict energetically favorable muon stopping sites in crystalline solids. MEOWN explicitly incorporates electrostatic interactions, electronic screening, polarization effects, and zero-point motion into the learning workflow, providing physically interpretable predictions with substantially reduced computational cost while maintaining physical consistency and scientific rigor. To validate the framework, we investigated several well-established benchmark materials, including MnSi, CoF2, CaF2, LiF, and NaF. The predicted muon stopping sites and dipolar fields are in close agreement with previously reported DFT+mu results, demonstrating the reliability and transferability of the approach across chemically diverse systems. Notably, MEOWN predicts the equilibrium muon stopping site within a few minutes, offering a significant speedup over conventional DFT-based calculations. This manuscript presents the theoretical foundations, computational methodology, and validation of MEOWN as a general-purpose software for rapid and reliable muon stopping-site prediction in crystalline materials.

</details>


### [190] [Application of Regional Chemical Potential Analysis to Si Adsorption on the Diamond (001) Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16620)
*Masahiro Fukuda,Arath E. Marin Ramirez,Yoshiaki Sugimoto,Taisuke Ozaki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论（DFT）与区域化学势（RCP）分析，研究碳二聚体与硅原子在重构金刚石(001)表面的吸附行为，揭示RCP可用于描述表面成键重排、解释一维链状生长并预测高覆盖度下的Si条纹与正方点阵结构及带隙演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解实验观测到的碳二聚体带状结构的位点选择性生长机制，并寻找能够直观解释表面共价成键与吸附驱动结构形成的物理判据，从而为候选吸附结构的筛选提供理论指导。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算结合区域化学势（regional chemical potential, RCP）分析，考察单分子与多原子吸附的吸附能、几何优化结构、表面相与能带结构。

Result: 计算表明单个Si原子优先桥接表面碳二聚体，后续Si原子通过Si-Si成键倾向于相邻二聚体位点；RCP分析识别出有限Si链末端的给电子区域。高覆盖度下几何优化得到Si条纹与平面正方点阵结构，表面相分析显示有效Si化学势升高促使覆盖度递增；能带结构显示随Si覆盖度增加表面带隙逐步减小，正方点阵结构中多条能带穿过费米能级，且两个面内方向的导电态因金刚石(001)衬底缺乏四重旋转对称性而呈现不同的Si与C特征。

Conclusion: 结果确立了RCP分析是解释表面共价成键并指导吸附驱动表面结构探索的有效方法。

Abstract: Adsorption of carbon dimers and Si atoms on the reconstructed diamond (001) surface is inves- tigated using density functional theory and regional chemical potential (RCP) analysis. We first demonstrate that the RCP distribution provides a real-space description of the bonding rearrange- ments responsible for the site-selective growth of experimentally observed carbon-dimer ribbons. We then examine the adsorption of single and multiple Si atoms. The calculated adsorption ener- gies show that a single Si atom preferentially bridges a surface carbon dimer and that subsequently adsorbed Si atoms favor neighboring dimer sites through Si-Si bond formation. The RCP analysis identifies electron-donating regions at the ends of finite Si chains, providing an intuitive explanation for their preferential one-dimensional growth and a physically motivated strategy for selecting candi- date adsorption structures. At higher Si coverages, geometry optimizations yield Si stripe and planar square-lattice structures on the diamond surface. Surface phase analysis indicates that an increase in the effective Si chemical potential favors structures with progressively higher Si coverages, from the Si stripe phase to the Si square-lattice phase. The calculated band structures reveal a progressive reduction of the surface band gap with increasing Si coverage. In the Si square-lattice structure, several bands cross the Fermi level, and the conducting states along the two in-plane directions have distinct Si and C character because the underlying diamond (001) substrate lacks fourfold rotational symmetry. These results establish RCP analysis as a useful approach for interpreting surface covalent bonding and guiding the exploration of adsorption-driven surface structures.

</details>


### [191] [Total-energy-assisted Tight-binding Method Based on Density Functional Theory - Design Principles toward Transferability and Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16649)
*Takeo Fujiwara,Yoshiro Nohara,Susumu Yamamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文将基于总能量的紧束缚方法（tight-binding method derived from the total energy, TE-TB）在局域密度近似（local density approximation, LDA）框架下由双参数扩展为三参数，并在晶体硅上验证其结构稳定性与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有 TE-TB 方法采用双参数表述，对局部堆积效应（local packing effects）的刻画不足，限制了其可迁移性（transferability）。

Method: 新方法引入三个参数以显式描述局部堆积效应，并基于物理考虑对定义紧束缚哈密顿量（tight-binding Hamiltonian）及相关能量泛函的函数施加若干边界条件（归纳偏置，inductive bias）。

Result: 在晶体硅（crystalline silicon）上针对金刚石结构（diamond structure）稳定性、单空位（monovacancy）及 (001) 表面进行基准测试，结果显示该方法具有高可迁移性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 三参数 TE-TB 设计理念相比原双参数表述改善了可迁移性，其可靠性在晶体硅的结构与缺陷基准测试中得到验证。

Abstract: The previously proposed tight-binding method derived from the total energy has been extended within the local density approximation (TE-TB method; J. Phys. Soc. Jpn. 87, 064802 (2018)). Unlike the earlier two-parameter formulation, the present method introduces three parameters. These parameters explicitly capture local packing effects and improve transferability. Furthermore, based on physical considerations, several boundary conditions (inductive bias) are imposed on the functionals defining the tight-binding Hamiltonian and related energy functionals. The revised formalism is tested on crystalline silicon to assess the stability of the diamond structure, a monovacancy, and the (001) surface. These benchmark tests demonstrate the high transferability and reliability of the present three-parameter TE-TB design philosophy.

</details>


### [192] [Electrical Probing of Sub-Néel Spin Dynamics in Two-Dimensional Antiferromagnets Using Graphene Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16725)
*Adrián García-Martín,Sara Gullace,Nicolás Montenegro-Pohlhammer,María José Martínez-Pérez,Rocío Sánchez-de-Armas,Carmen J. Calzado,Enrique Burzurí*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将石墨烯（graphene）与二维反铁磁体 FePS₃ 集成为范德华异质结，利用石墨烯作为超灵敏电学换能器，读出传统磁测量难以探测的隐藏自旋动力学与磁重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维反铁磁范德华材料（van der Waals materials）是研究新奇自旋动力学与磁相的平台，但其净磁化强度近乎为零且高度绝缘，导致电学探测极为困难，亟需有效的电学读出手段。

Method: 将石墨烯与范德华反铁磁体 FePS₃ 集成，在低温下进行输运测量，并结合磁场与栅压调控；辅以密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，比较 FePS₃ 中竞争性反铁磁自旋构型及其对邻近石墨烯电子响应的影响。

Result: 在远低于奈尔温度（Néel temperature）的区间观察到多个电阻异常，其温度、磁场与载流子密度依赖关系可分别归因于磁振子（magnon）激发与反铁磁态的低温柔性磁重构；DFT 结果表明竞争性反铁磁构型能量近乎简并，却在石墨烯层中产生显著不同的电子响应，且这些信号具有明显的静电可调性。

Conclusion: 石墨烯可直接将界面磁动力学转换为电信号，石墨烯/反铁磁范德华异质结（heterostructures）可作为二维磁体自旋动力学电学读出的通用平台。

Abstract: Low-dimensional antiferromagnetic van der Waals materials have emerged as a versatile platform for exploring exotic spin dynamics and magnetic phases, yet electrically accessing these phenomena remains a major challenge because of their vanishing net magnetization and highly insulating nature. Here, we demonstrate that graphene can act as an ultrasensitive electrical transducer of hidden spin dynamics in two-dimensional antiferromagnets. By integrating graphene with the van der Waals antiferromagnet FePS$_3$, low-temperature transport measurements combined with magnetic-field and gate-voltage control reveal multiple resistance anomalies well below the Néel temperature. Their distinct temperature, magnetic-field, and carrier-density dependences enable us to associate these anomalies with magnon excitations and with a low-temperature magnetic reconfiguration of the antiferromagnetic state, both of which remain largely inaccessible to conventional magnetometry. Density functional theory calculations further show that competing antiferromagnetic spin configurations in FePS$_3$ are nearly degenerate in energy while producing markedly different electronic responses in the adjacent graphene layer. The pronounced electrostatic tunability of these signatures demonstrates that graphene directly transduces interfacial magnetic dynamics into an electrical signal. Our work establishes graphene/antiferromagnetic van der Waals heterostructures as a versatile platform for the electrical readout of spin dynamics in two-dimensional magnets.

</details>


### [193] [The Closer, the Better: Engineering RGB Thermometric Performance in Tb3+, Eu3+-Doped MOFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16734)
*M. Szymczak,L. Giang,L. Marciniak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示 Tb³⁺、Eu³⁺ 共掺金属有机框架（metal-organic frameworks, MOFs）中离子间距与能量传递（energy transfer）概率及热敏性能的关联，证实缩短 Eu³⁺–Tb³⁺ 间距可提升免滤光片、相机读出式热传感的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统发光测温依赖光谱分析，而基于发光颜色随温度变化的视觉读出更为简便易用，但缺乏对发光颜色热演化与荧光体结构关系的清晰认识，制约了视觉发光温度计的理性设计。因此有必要建立结构—能量传递—测温性能之间的直接关联。

Method: 以 Tb³⁺、Eu³⁺ 共掺金属有机框架（metal-organic frameworks, MOFs）为研究对象，考察其温度依赖的发光颜色与结构特征的相关性，重点评估其在免滤光片（filter-free）、相机读出式热传感中的应用潜力，并以蓝、红通道发光强度比作为读出信号。

Result: 在所研究材料系列中，减小 Eu³⁺ 与 Tb³⁺ 之间的离子间距会提高能量传递（energy transfer）概率，从而增强基于蓝红通道发光强度比的相机读出热灵敏度。

Conclusion: 研究建立了发光材料结构特征、能量传递过程与测温性能之间的直接关系，为具有预设传感特性的视觉发光温度计的理性设计奠定基础，并为免滤光片、相机读出式热传感平台的结构导向优化提供路径。

Abstract: The effectiveness of luminescence thermometry, which exploits temperature-induced changes in the spectroscopic properties of phosphor materials for temperature sensing, has been extensively demonstrated. However, from a practical perspective, monitoring temperature-induced variations in the color of the emitted light offers a considerably more straightforward and user-friendly alternative to conventional spectral analysis. In this context, establishing a clear relationship between the thermal evolution of the emission color and the structure of the phosphor is essential for the rational design of visual luminescence thermometers. In this work, we investigate the correlation between the temperature-dependent emission color and the structural characteristics of Tb3+, Eu3+-co-doped metal-organic frameworks (MOFs), with particular emphasis on their potential for filter-free, camera-based thermal sensing. The obtained results demonstrate that, within the investigated series of materials, decreasing the interionic distance between Eu3+ and Tb3+ ions increases the probability of energy transfer and consequently enhances the thermal sensitivity of the camera-based readout derived from the ratio of luminescence intensities recorded in the blue and red channels. These findings establish a direct relationship between the structural characteristics of the luminescent material, the underlying energy-transfer processes, and its thermometric performance. More broadly, this study provides a foundation for the rational design of visual luminescence thermometers with predefined sensing characteristics, opening a pathway toward structure-guided optimization of filter-free, camera-based thermal sensing platforms.

</details>


### [194] [Electric-Field-Switchable Altermagnetism via Ligand Rotation in a d0 Metal-Organic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16749)
*Hongjing Wang,Xiuling Li,Xiaojun Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了一种在柔性 d0 金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）中实现电场可切换交替磁性（altermagnetism）的途径：极性取代基将外场耦合到配体旋转，通过翻转 J1/J2 超交换（superexchange）竞争，在约 34° 处发生反铁磁—交替磁转变，并实现无势垒的可逆开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性兼具零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂（spin splitting），虽已在金属有机框架中被观测到，但非侵入式、可逆的调控仍具挑战。该研究旨在实现电场可切换的交替磁性，以服务于有机自旋电子学（organic spintronics）。

Method: 以柔性 d0-MOF 为对象，利用极性取代基将外电场与配体旋转耦合；通过平面态下 pz 轨道介导的 180° 配体—金属—配体超交换（J2）与配体离面旋转后激活的 px/py 轨道增强的 90° 路径（J1）之间的竞争分析机制，并结合从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）在 300 K 下考察热涨落影响。

Result: 反铁磁到交替磁的转变发生在约 34°，90° 时自旋劈裂达 84 meV；90° 交替磁态可由电场开启、撤场关闭，因约束旋转势能面单调下降回 0° 而实现无势垒弛豫与可逆开关。300 K 从头算分子动力学显示热涨落本身无法使配体旋转同步；含氟取代基的临界场约为 0.22 V/Å，与场效应器件相当。

Conclusion: 该研究表明交替磁序的起源为配体 p 轨道而非过渡金属 d 轨道，并证明极性取代基可实现电场调控，为有机自旋电子学提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Altermagnetism combines vanishing net magnetization with momentum-dependent spin splitting. While altermagnetic signatures have been identified in metal-organic frameworks (MOFs), non-invasive reversible control remains challenging. Here we report a route to electric-field-switchable altermagnetism in a flexible d0-MOF, in which polar substituents couple the external field to ligand rotation. In the planar ground state, pz orbitals mediate 180-degree ligand-metal-ligand superexchange (J2), stabilizing antiferromagnetic order. Rotating ligands out of plane activates px/py orbitals, enhances 90-degree pathways (J1), and reverses J1/J2 competition. The antiferromagnetic-to-altermagnetic crossover occurs at approx. 34 degrees, with spin splitting of 84 meV at 90 degrees. The 90-degree altermagnetic state is switched on by the electric field and switched off upon field removal, as the constrained-rotation landscape decreases monotonically back toward 0 degrees, enabling barrierless relaxation to the antiferromagnetic state and reversible on/off operation. Ab initio molecular dynamics at 300 K shows thermal fluctuations alone cannot synchronize ligand rotation. Polar substituents provide the required coupling for electric-field control; for fluorinated substituents, the critical field is approx. 0.22 V/Angstrom, comparable to field-effect devices. This work establishes ligand p orbitals, rather than transition-metal d orbitals, as the origin of altermagnetic order and shows that polar substituents enable electric-field control for organic spintronics.

</details>


### [195] [Floquet Spin-Antiferroelectric Phase in Collinear Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16758)
*Yu-hao Wei,Zheng Qin,Shengpu Huang,Dong-Hui Xu,Da-shuai Ma,Rui Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过对称性分析与 Floquet 理论，在周期驱动的共线反铁磁体中预言了一种 Floquet 自旋反铁电相（Floquet spin-antiferroelectric phase），并在单层 MnPS₃ 中给出可实现平台，为非平衡多铁性（multiferroicity）提供新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多铁相（multiferroic phases）同时具备磁序与极化序，有望实现自旋与电自由度的光学调控。然而，平衡态晶体对称性严格禁止某些自旋分辨的面内电极化，限制了此类功能的实现。

Method: 采用对称性分析（symmetry analysis）与 Floquet 理论（Floquet theory）构建周期驱动共线反铁磁体（collinear antiferromagnets）的有效描述，并结合紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）与基于第一性原理的 Floquet 计算（first-principles-based Floquet calculations）。

Result: 发现与非常规磁性（unconventional magnetism）共存的 Floquet 自旋反铁电相，具有补偿的、自旋分辨的垂直旋转轴面内电极化；紧束缚模型揭示光偏振可调控极性序与自旋响应；第一性原理计算预测该相可在单层 MnPS₃ 中实现。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了超越平衡态对称性约束的自旋反铁电（spin-antiferroelectricity）非平衡路径，并通过光学调控自旋与极性自由度，将 Floquet 工程（Floquet engineering）、非常规磁性与多铁性联系起来。

Abstract: Multiferroic phases combining magnetic and polar orders offer opportunities for optical control of spin and electric degrees of freedom. Here, using symmetry analysis and Floquet theory, we establish a Floquet spin-antiferroelectric phase coexisting with unconventional magnetism in periodically driven collinear antiferromagnets, qualifying it as an unconventional multiferroic. This driven phase supports compensated, spin-resolved in-plane electric polarizations perpendicular to the vertical rotation axis, which are strictly forbidden by crystalline symmetry in equilibrium. Using a tight-binding model, we elucidate how light polarization controls the emergence of polar order and spin responses. Moreover, first-principles-based Floquet calculations predict its realization in monolayer $\text{MnPS}_3$, identifying a realistic two-dimensional antiferromagnetic platform. These findings establish a nonequilibrium route to spin-antiferroelectricity beyond equilibrium symmetry constraints and connect Floquet engineering, unconventional magnetism, and multiferroicity through optical control of spin and polar degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [196] [Interplay between Interlayer Shift and Twist: Twisted van der Waals Nanowires Driven by Rotational Twinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16771)
*Dong-gyu Kim,Kisung Kang,Hani Kang,Kihyun Lee,Yangjin Lee,Jinsub Park,Joong-Eon Jung,Min Kim,Myeongjin Jang,Aloysius Soon,Kwanpyo Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报道了范德华（vdW）晶体中由择优倾斜堆垛与孪晶引发的自发扭转机制：GeSe₂₋ₓTeₓ纳米线沿生长轴的晶面持续旋转，扭转率与纳米线半径系统性相关，并归因于层间堆垛与旋转孪晶应变之间的能量竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间平移与扭转可实现材料性质的调控，但二者相互作用及精细调控机制尚不明确。既有自下而上合成与人工逐层堆叠方法难以揭示平移-扭转耦合规律，因此需要探索内禀的扭转形成途径。

Method: 结合电子衍射（electron diffraction）与原子分辨扫描透射电镜（STEM）成像观测GeSe₂₋ₓTeₓ纳米线的晶面旋转与旋转孪晶界；并通过第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与结构弛豫模拟分析能量来源。

Result: 晶面沿纳米线生长轴连续旋转，扭转率系统性依赖于纳米线半径；在纳米线中心区域观测到连续旋转孪晶界（rotational twin boundary）；计算与模拟确认扭转形变源于择优层间堆垛配准与旋转孪晶应变代价之间的能量竞争。

Conclusion: 旋转孪晶被确立为自发扭转形成的内禀路径，为设计具有相容晶体对称性与堆垛构型的扭转工程范德华晶体提供了设计原则。

Abstract: In van der Waals(vdW) layered materials, interlayer shift and twist have enabled the control of material properties through polytype and moire engineering. Various approaches, including bottom-up synthesis and manual layer-by-layer stacking, have been utilized to engineer targeted stacking configurations. However, the interplay between interlayer shift and twist, as well as reliable mechanisms for fine-tuning these parameters, remains largely unexplored. Here, we report a previously unrecognized twisting mechanism arising from preferred tilted stacking and twinning in vdW crystals. Electron diffraction and atomic-resolution scanning transmission electron microscopy(STEM) imaging reveal that the lattice planes of group-IV chalcogenide GeSe_{2-x}Te_x rotate continuously along the nanowire growth axis, with twist rates depending systematically on nanowire radius. Atomic-scale imaging further identifies a continuous rotational twin boundary extending along the central region of the nanowire. First-principles calculations and structural relaxation simulations confirm that the twisting deformation originates from energetic competition between the preferred interlayer stacking registry and the strain cost imposed by rotational twinning. These findings establish rotational twinning as an intrinsic route to spontaneous twist formation and provide a design principle for realizing twist-engineered vdW crystals with compatible crystal symmetries and stacking motifs.

</details>


### [197] [Topological spin textures in 2D altermagnetic chromium chalcogenides: Interplay between magnetic frustration and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16780)
*Zirui Zhang,Shiwei Zhu,Ruixiao Ma,Qiuyao Zhang,Xiaoping Wu,Changsheng Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合第一性原理计算与原子自旋模拟，系统研究了单层Cr₂X₂及Janus单层Cr₂XY交替磁体（altermagnet）中交换阻挫与DMI的相互作用，揭示了阻挫与DMI主导的不同区域及两类调控机制，为二维交替磁体中拓扑自旋结构的理解与设计提供了通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性（altermagnetism）作为新近提出的磁学范式，其在二维体系中的拓扑自旋结构（topological spin textures）研究仍十分有限，因此有必要系统探究该体系中驱动非共线自旋态的关键相互作用。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与原子自旋模拟（atomistic spin simulations），研究对象为单层Cr₂X₂与Janus单层Cr₂XY交替磁体（X, Y = O, S, Se, Te，且X ≠ Y）；通过相空间扫描与测地线微动弹性带（geodesic nudged elastic band, GNEB）计算分析阻挫与DMI的竞争。

Result: 两类体系均因最近邻与次近邻交换相互作用竞争而产生强内禀交换阻挫；Janus体系中面外镜面对称性破缺在重硫族配体强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）介导下激活显著的面内Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）。阻挫与DMI的相互作用形成阻挫主导与DMI主导两类区域，磁各向异性决定自旋取向偏好，产生多样的非共线态；并揭示协同效应（阻挫降低非共线态出现的临界DMI）与竞争效应（阻挫利于高拓扑荷态、DMI利于低荷态）两种机制。

Conclusion: 研究建立了理解阻挫与DMI相互作用的通用框架，并为二维交替磁体中拓扑自旋结构的理性设计提供指导。

Abstract: Altermagnetism has recently emerged as a distinct magnetic paradigm, yet the exploration of topological spin textures within two-dimensional (2D) altermagnets remains limited. Using first-principles calculations and atomistic spin simulations, we investigate monolayer $\mathrm{Cr_2X_2}$ and Janus monolayer $\mathrm{Cr_2XY}$ altermagnets ($\mathrm{X, Y}=\mathrm{O, S, Se, Te}$, and $\mathrm{X \neq Y}$). Both systems exhibit strong intrinsic exchange frustration arising from competition between nearest-neighbor and further-neighbor exchange interactions. In Janus $\mathrm{Cr_2XY}$, breaking the out-of-plane mirror symmetry additionally activates a substantial in-plane Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI), mediated by the strong spin-orbit coupling of heavy chalcogen ligands. The interplay between exchange frustration and DMI gives rise to distinct frustration- and DMI-dominated regimes, while magnetic anisotropy determines the preferred spin orientation, leading to a diverse range of non-collinear states. Systematic phase-space scans and geodesic nudged elastic band (GNEB) calculations reveal two distinct mechanisms governing this interplay: a synergistic effect, in which frustration lowers the critical DMI for the onset of non-collinear states, and a competitive effect, in which frustration favors high-topological-charge states while DMI favors low-charge states. Our results establish a general framework for understanding the interplay between frustration and DMI and guiding the design of topological spin textures in two-dimensional altermagnets.

</details>


### [198] [SpectraFit XPS: a free, browser-based application for X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy peak fitting and quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16789)
*Yongsup Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SpectraFit XPS 是一款完全在浏览器中运行的免费 XPS 数据分析网络应用，无需安装、无服务器端处理，光谱数据不离开用户计算机；其拟合法与定量分析能力经验证可精确复现 KherveFitting 参考值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XPS 数据分析通常依赖本地安装软件或服务器端处理，存在部署门槛与数据隐私顾虑。该工作旨在提供免安装、数据不出本地即可完成拟合与定量分析的解决方案。

Method: 采用交互式 Levenberg–Marquardt 拟合引擎，支持真 Voigt、Doniach–Šunjić 及高斯–洛伦兹加和线形；本底包括 Shirley（静态与动态）、线性与 Tougaard（双参数与四参数）；提供自旋–轨道双峰生成与基于表达式的峰间约束。定量分析支持十种相对灵敏度因子（relative-sensitivity-factor）集、含 TPP-2M 非弹性平均自由程在内的动能校正方案、角度校正及原子与重量百分比，底层数据库与算法改编自开源 KherveFitting 项目。

Result: 支持读写 ISO 14976（VAMAS）文件，涵盖多区域、深度剖面数据及载入时透射函数校正，并可导入常见厂商与电子表格格式；附带可导出期刊级 SVG/PNG 图形的发表用绘图器。定量流程在 30 例回归套件中精确复现 KherveFitting 参考值，该套件属于含 100 项测试的自动化测试集。

Conclusion: 该应用连同图文双语用户指南与合成演示数据集，均可免费获取于 https://spectrafit-xps.web.app/，为 XPS 分析提供隐私友好、零安装的可用工具。

Abstract: SpectraFit XPS is a free web application for the analysis of X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS) data that runs entirely in the browser, with no installation and no server-side processing: spectra never leave the user's computer. It combines an interactive Levenberg--Marquardt fitting engine with true Voigt, Doniach--Šunjić, and summed Gaussian--Lorentzian line shapes; Shirley (static and dynamic), linear, and Tougaard (two- and four-parameter) backgrounds; spin--orbit doublet generation; and expression-based inter-peak constraints. Quantitative analysis supports ten relative-sensitivity-factor sets, several kinetic-energy correction schemes including TPP-2M inelastic mean free paths, angular correction, and atomic and weight percentages, with the underlying database and algorithms adapted from the open-source KherveFitting project. The application reads and writes ISO~14976 (VAMAS) files---including multi-region and depth-profile data and load-time transmission-function correction---and imports common vendor and spreadsheet formats. A publication plotter exports journal-ready SVG/PNG figures. The quantification pipeline reproduces KherveFitting reference values exactly in a 30-case regression suite, part of an automated test set of 100 tests. The application, an illustrated bilingual user guide, and a synthetic demonstration dataset are freely available at https://spectrafit-xps.web.app/.

</details>


### [199] [Fractional-Quantum Ferroelectrics: A Route to High-Mobility Ferroelectric Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16799)
*Rong-Tian Pang,Wenjie Hu,Youning Liu,Jin-Jian Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于第一性原理声子限制输运计算， monolayer In$_2$Se$_3$ 的载流子迁移率受铁电性微观起源支配：位移型 $β'$ 相受低频铁电模散射限制（室温约数 cm$^2$/(V s)），而分数量子铁电 $α$ 相因不存在软模凝聚而无铁电模散射，室温迁移率超 70 cm$^2$/(V s)，掺杂屏蔽后可达 300 cm$^2$/(V s) 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电半导体（ferroelectric semiconductors）在多功能电子学中具有应用前景，但其载流子迁移率（carrier mobility）普遍偏低，构成主要限制。该工作旨在从微观层面厘清铁电性起源与载流子散射（carrier scattering）之间的关联，探索突破该限制的途径。

Method: 采用第一性原理声子限制输运计算（first-principles phonon-limited transport calculations），针对单层（monolayer）In$_2$Se$_3$ 的两种铁电相——位移型（displacive）$β'$-In$_2$Se$_3$ 与分数量子铁电（fractional-quantum ferroelectric）$α$-In$_2$Se$_3$——对比分析其散射机制与迁移率。

Result: 在位移型 $β'$ 相中，低频铁电模（ferroelectric modes）主导载流子散射，纵光学声子（longitudinal-optical, LO phonons）亦有附加贡献，室温电子迁移率被限制在数 cm$^2$/(V s)。在分数量子铁电 $α$ 相中，极化源于离散的晶格尺度原子位移而非软模凝聚（soft-mode condensation），铁电模散射缺失，输运由 LO 声子主导，室温迁移率高于 70 cm$^2$/(V s)；载流子掺杂进一步屏蔽长程电子-LO 声子相互作用，在实验可及密度下迁移率提升至 300 cm$^2$/(V s) 以上。

Conclusion: 结果表明，分数量子铁电性可将稳健极化与强本征载流子散射解耦，为高迁移率铁电半导体（high-mobility ferroelectric semiconductors）提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Ferroelectric semiconductors are promising for multifunctional electronics, yet their typically low carrier mobilities remain a major limitation. Using first-principles phonon-limited transport calculations for monolayer In$_2$Se$_3$, we show that this limitation depends critically on the microscopic origin of ferroelectricity. In displacive $β'$-In$_2$Se$_3$, low-frequency ferroelectric modes dominate carrier scattering, with additional contributions from longitudinal-optical (LO) phonons, limiting the room-temperature electron mobility to a few cm$^2$/(V s). By contrast, in fractional-quantum ferroelectric $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$, ferroelectric-mode scattering is absent because polarization arises from discrete lattice-scale atomic displacements rather than soft-mode condensation. Transport is therefore dominated by LO phonons, yielding a room-temperature mobility above 70 cm$^2$/(V s). Carrier doping further screens long-range electron-LO-phonon interactions and raises the mobility beyond 300 cm$^2$/(V s) at experimentally accessible densities. These results establish that fractional-quantum ferroelectricity can decouple robust polarization from strong intrinsic carrier scattering, offering a route toward high-mobility ferroelectric semiconductors.

</details>


### [200] [ZStar: An automated toolkit for polarization, Born effective charges, dielectric response, and infrared and Raman spectra calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16802)
*Xudong Zhu,Junhong Li,Gan Jin,Zheng Guan,Lixin He*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ZStar 是一个开源 Python 工具包（toolkit），可自动化计算极化（polarization）、玻恩有效电荷（Born effective charges, BEC）、声子（phonons）、静/频依赖介电响应（dielectric response）以及红外与拉曼光谱（infrared and Raman spectra），并支持从体相晶体到零维分子的全维度体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEC 将原子位移与极化、晶格振动与介电响应及红外活性联系起来，但相关计算流程分散、跨维度支持不足。ZStar 旨在提供覆盖输入准备、执行与响应分析的集成化、自动化工作流。

Method: 采用基于贝里相位（Berry-phase）极化与实空间偶极（real-space dipoles）分别描述周期方向与开放方向的响应；提出统一且对称适配的有限位移（finite-displacement）框架，从同一次计算中联合确定 BEC 与区中心力常数（zone-center force constants）；在 ABACUS+PYATB 路径中复用电子哈密顿量（electronic Hamiltonian）以省去额外自洽计算。

Result: 该框架在保持数值精度的同时提升计算效率，并可在无额外自洽计算条件下完成拉曼计算；工具提供可复现示例与打包的智能体技能（agent skill），支持常规与智能体辅助两种使用方式。

Conclusion: ZStar 实现了跨全维度的极化、BEC、介电响应与振动光谱的高效自动化探索。

Abstract: Born effective charges (BEC) link atomic displacements to polarization, connecting lattice vibrations to dielectric response and infrared activity. We present ZStar, an open-source Python toolkit that automates calculations of polarization, BEC, phonons, static and frequency-dependent dielectric response, and infrared and Raman spectra. ZStar supports calculations across all dimensionalities, including bulk crystals, two-dimensional materials, one-dimensional structures, and molecules, through an integrated workflow spanning input preparation, execution, and response analysis. Berry-phase polarization and real-space dipoles describe responses along periodic and open directions, respectively. Our unified, symmetry-adapted finite-displacement framework jointly determines BEC and zone-center force constants from the same calculations, improving computational efficiency while preserving numerical accuracy. Reusing the electronic Hamiltonian in the ABACUS+PYATB route enables Raman calculations without additional self-consistent calculations. Reproducible examples and a packaged agent skill support conventional and agent-assisted use. ZStar enables efficient, automated exploration of polarization, BEC, dielectric response, and vibrational spectra across all dimensionalities.

</details>


### [201] [Probabilistic Deep Learning Framework for Phase Transformation Forecasting aided by In Situ High temperature Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16940)
*Ioannis Kouroudis,Niki Balestrieri,Stefan Rotzsche,Niccolo Radice,Peter Mayr,Alessio Gagliardi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个概率性深度学习（probabilistic deep-learning）框架，将基于图像的表征、时间预测与微结构预报统一起来，用于原位高温共聚焦激光扫描显微镜（in situ high-temperature confocal laser scanning microscopy）数据，实现对魏氏体铁素体（Widmanstatten ferrite）比例的概率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构材料的力学性能受其微结构（microstructure）支配，而微结构由加工过程中的热历史（thermal history）决定，但沿任意热轨迹预测微结构演化仍是难题。传统连续冷却转变图（continuous-cooling-transformation diagrams）仅提供静态描述且并非对所有材料都存在；现有深度学习方法则将图像表征、时间预测与微结构预报割裂为独立任务。

Method: 框架先用 beta-变分自编码器（beta-variational autoencoder）将初始表面图像压缩为低维潜在表示（latent representation），并将其与冷却速率和完整温度历史一并输入时间融合变换器（temporal fusion transformer），输出魏氏体铁素体比例的分位数预测（quantile forecast）。同时，高斯过程（Gaussian process）预测终态魏氏体铁素体比例并修正预测的平衡水平，从而给出概率预测区间。

Result: 在 S235 钢的魏氏体铁素体演化上，跨五种冷却制度（cooling regimes）验证了该方法，并展现出显著的准确性。

Conclusion: 结果验证了该框架的相关性，为在热处理下对材料演化开展简化且高度加速的研究铺平了道路。

Abstract: The mechanical performance of structural materials is governed by their microstructure, which is itself set by the thermal history imposed during processing. Predicting how that microstructure evolves along an arbitrary thermal trajectory, however, remains a challenging problem. Conventional continuous-cooling-transformation diagrams, for instance, provide only a static description, and they do not exist for all materials, especially emerging and novel ones. Deep-learning approaches address this issue, but they treat image-based characterization, temporal prediction, and microstructure forecasting as separate tasks. Here, we introduce a probabilistic deep-learning framework that unifies these tasks for in situ high-temperature confocal laser scanning microscopy data. The initial surface image is compressed with a beta-variational autoencoder into a low-dimensional latent representation, which, together with the cooling rate and the full temperature history, is passed to a temporal fusion transformer that produces a quantile forecast of the Widmanstatten ferrite ratio. In parallel, a Gaussian process predicts the end-state Widmanstatten ferrite ratio and corrects the forecast equilibrium level, yielding probabilistic prediction intervals. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on Widmanstatten ferrite evolution in S235 steel across five cooling regimes and showcase significant accuracy. This verifies the relevance of our framework and paves the way for a streamlined and highly accelerated investigation of material evolution under thermal treatment.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond the Binary Hull: Higher-Order Thermodynamic Stabilization in Inorganic Ternary Compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17098)
*Pravan Omprakash*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出“涌现三元稳定化能”（emergent ternary stabilization energy, delta E3）这一指标，用于区分稳定三元化合物的稳定性究竟继承自有利的二元相，还是源于三元组成本身。对 Materials Project 中 19,209 个 GGA 框架下的化合物计算显示，delta E3 中位数为 63.3 meV/atom，并在不同化学家族间呈现系统差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统凸包（convex hull）仅表明稳定三元化合物低于所有竞争相，却无法揭示其稳定性是来自有利二元相的直接继承，还是三元素共同进入同一晶体后产生的新增稳定化。该研究旨在为“组成复杂性（compositional complexity）的热力学价值”提供可量化的度量。

Method: 定义 delta E3 为稳定三元化合物在相同组成下、相对于完整一元–二元凸包（unary-binary hull）的能量下降幅度。将该指标应用于 Materials Project 中 19,209 个在 GGA 框架下计算的化合物，并按化学家族、阴离子类型及实验/理论来源进行分布分析；同时将其与阳离子电负性差、带隙打开及配位与键几何重构等特征关联。

Result: delta E3 的中位数为 63.3 meV/atom，分布从接近二元简并的相到被数百 meV/atom 稳定化的化合物。金属间化合物（intermetallics）的稳定化中位数最小，单阴离子化合物处于较宽的中间区间，多阴离子化合物（polyanion compounds）获得最大增益。在单阴离子家族内，较大的 delta E3 与阳离子电负性对比、带隙打开，以及相对于实际二元分解产物的配位、键几何和局域近邻化学的重构相关。实验关联化合物的形成能中有 4.92%（中位数）来自三元稳定化，理论化合物为 3.53%，其中硫化物与硒化物区分最明显。

Conclusion: delta E3 为组成复杂性的热力学价值提供了紧凑度量：低值对应稳定性基本可由二元相重现的化合物，高值则标识出显著受益于三元特有的电子与结构组织的化合物。

Abstract: Stable ternary compounds lie below every competing phase on a conventional convex hull, but this does not reveal whether their stability is inherited from already favorable binaries or created by bringing three elements into one crystal. We define the emergent ternary stabilization energy, delta E3, as the energy by which a stable ternary lies below the complete unary-binary hull at the same composition. Applied to 19,209 Materials Project compounds calculated within the GGA framework, delta E3 has a median of 63.3 meV/atom and spans from nearly binary-degenerate phases to compounds stabilized by several hundred meV/atom. The distribution separates recognizable chemical limits: intermetallics have the smallest median stabilization, single-anion compounds occupy a broad intermediate regime, and polyanion compounds receive the largest gains. Within single anion families, large delta E3 is associated with cation electronegativity contrast, bandgap opening, and reconstruction of coordination, bond geometry, and local neighbour chemistry relative to the actual binary decomposition products. Experimentally linked compounds obtain a median 4.92% of their formation energy from ternary stabilization, compared with 3.53% for theoretical compounds, with the clearest separation in sulphides and selenides. delta E3 therefore provides a compact measure of the thermodynamic value of compositional complexity: low values identify phases whose stability is largely reproducible by binaries, while high values identify compounds that gain substantially from ternary specific electronic and structural organization.

</details>


### [203] [AI for Science with GPT-6 Astra: Thermal Design and Electrothermal Analysis of 2D CFET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17123)
*Min-Hui Kim,Khushi Sharma,Sarah Zhang,Ye Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用 AI 智能体工作流（AI agent workflow）在给定的电热模型（electrothermal model）中探索 2D CFET 反相器的热优化结构，发现重分布源极互连与衬底导向散热路径的组合设计可在固定金属体积与 20 μW 条件下将峰值温升降低 1.67 K，并量化了相应电学代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 CFET 反相器的热优化需在结构方案与其电学代价之间进行权衡评估，而此类研究任务适合以 AI 智能体工作流在统一电热模型内系统检验。

Method: 在所提供的电热模型中运行 AI 智能体工作流：在 12 nm 节点由 Astra 选择重分布源极互连几何，并由协调智能体（coordinating agent）提出衬底导向的散热路径；随后开展金属电阻灵敏度分析与有效接触长度缩放分析，并进行结果复现。

Result: 组合设计在固定金属体积和 20 μW 下使峰值温升降低 1.67 K；金属电阻灵敏度分析带来约 0.6 K 的反相器降温，同时 nFET 开态电流（on-current）损失约 2%；有效接触长度缩放显示，当电流下降时更低温度可伴随更高热阻；复现识别出结果一致的实现，并保留一个 104.95 K 的失效案例用于诊断。

Conclusion: 结果表明，AI 科学家工作流（AI scientist workflow）能够提出热学结构、在共同约束下对其进行测试，并量化其电学代价。

Abstract: Thermal optimization of 2D CFET inverters requires testing structural proposals against their electrical costs. We examine these research tasks using an AI agent workflow within a supplied electrothermal model. At 12 nm, Astra selects a redistributed source-interconnect geometry, while a coordinating agent proposes a substrate-directed heat-removal path. The combined design reduces peak temperature rise by 1.67 K at fixed metal volume and 20 μW. A subsequent metal-resistance sensitivity gives about 0.6-K inverter cooling alongside a 2% nFET on-current loss. Effective contact-length scaling further shows that lower temperature can accompany higher thermal resistance when current falls. Reproduction identifies agreeing implementations and retains a 104.95-K failure for diagnosis. These results show that an AI scientist workflow can propose thermal structures, test them under common constraints, and quantify their electrical cost.

</details>


### [204] [A transfer-rod-mounted rotatable sample holder enabling a compact modular vacuum system for ion-beam milling, oblique magnetron sputtering, and co-sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17220)
*Tiago de Oliveira Schneider,Folke Kaiser,Andreas Semrad,Peter Kießlich,Markus Meinert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于紧凑型研究级真空系统的多功能样品台（sample-holder）概念，样品台直接安装于传输杆（transfer rod）上并在整个装载、传输、沉积与离子束刻蚀（ion-beam milling）过程中保持机械连接，无需交接至独立基片台，从而在单一平台上实现标准沉积、任意角度斜沉积、基片旋转与共溅射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规布局需要将样品交接至独立基片台，并依赖独立快门机构与源倾斜机构，限制了紧凑型真空系统的集成度与工艺灵活性。该工作旨在通过样品台自身定义工艺几何，简化结构并扩展可用沉积模式。

Method: 样品台集成轴向传输运动、绕传输杆轴的旋转与独立基片旋转；内置屏蔽板（shielding plate）同时充当基片快门（substrate shutter）与源屏蔽（source shield）。系统采用标准 DN100CF 组件搭建模块化真空系统，集成装载锁（load lock）、磁控溅射（magnetron sputtering）与离子束刻蚀。

Result: 在不烘烤（bakeout）条件下系统本底压强达 $5 \cdot 10^{-8}\,\mathrm{mbar}$；无需源倾斜机构即可径向紧凑排布四个 2 英寸磁控溅射源。功能验证包括：Ta/CoFeB 双层膜的斜沉积呈现显著面内单轴磁各向异性（uniaxial in-plane magnetic anisotropy）；Au–Ag 成分序列共溅射的电阻率符合预期的 Nordheim 型成分依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该样品台概念可在紧凑型真空系统中以简化机构实现多种沉积几何与共溅射工艺，并通过磁性双层膜与合金成分序列的电阻率行为得到实验验证。

Abstract: We present a novel multifunctional sample-holder concept for compact research-scale vacuum systems. The sample holder is mounted directly on the transfer rod and remains mechanically connected to it throughout loading, transfer, deposition, and ion-beam milling. In contrast to conventional layouts, no handover to a separate substrate stage is required. By combining axial transfer motion, rotation around the transfer-rod axis, and independent substrate rotation, the holder itself defines the relevant process geometries and enables standard deposition, oblique deposition at freely selectable incidence angles, substrate rotation, and co-sputtering from adjacent sources.
  A shielding plate integrated into the sample holder acts as both a substrate shutter and a source shield. This reduces the need for separate shutter mechanisms and allows a compact radial arrangement of four 2-inch magnetron sputter sources without source-tilting mechanisms. The concept was implemented in a modular vacuum system assembled from standard DN100CF components, combining a load lock, magnetron sputtering, and ion-beam milling. The system reaches a base pressure of $5 \cdot 10^{-8}\,\mathrm{mbar}$ without bakeout.
  The functionality of the sample-holder concept is validated by oblique deposition of Ta/CoFeB bilayers, which exhibit pronounced uniaxial in-plane magnetic anisotropy, and by co-sputtering of an Au--Ag composition series, whose resistivity follows the expected Nordheim-type composition dependence.

</details>


### [205] [Higher order anharmonicity and polymorphism in the lattice thermal conductivity of transition metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17270)
*Marta Loletti,Qi Ren,Zhuyao Chang,Rajan Kumar,Nemo McIntosh,Martí Raya-Moreno,Ankit Jain,Zhao Liu,Riccardo Rurali*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过从头算（ab initio）方法研究高阶非谐性与多型性（polymorphism）对单层过渡金属硫属化物（transition metal dichalcogenides）晶格热导率 κ 的影响，发现四声子过程显著抑制 2H 相的 κ，而几乎不影响 1T′ 相，二者差异可用于热开关设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清高阶非谐性（higher-order anharmonicity）与多型性对单层过渡金属硫属化物晶格热导率的共同影响，并探索利用不同相之间 κ 差异实现热管理与声子逻辑器件的可能。

Method: 采用综合性的从头算（ab initio）计算，考察高阶非谐性效应（具体为四声子过程）对基态 2H 相与热力学竞争相 1T′ 相晶格热导率 κ 的作用。

Result: 在基态 2H 相中，四声子过程导致 κ 额外被抑制 15% 至 35%；而在热力学上具竞争力的 1T′ 相中，其影响几乎可忽略。

Conclusion: 2H 相（高电导/高热导相）与 1T′ 相（低热导相）之间的 κ 差异可用于设计热开关（thermal switches），有望应用于热管理及基于声子的逻辑元件（phonon-based logic elements），尤其在碲化物（tellurides）中，因实验已证实其场控超快可逆相变。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive {\it ab initio} study of the effect of higher-order anharmonicity and polymorphism on the lattice thermal conductivity, ${\bm κ}$, of single-layer transition metal dichalcogenides. We show that four-phonon processes are responsible for an additional suppression of ${\bm κ}$ ranging from 15\% to 35\% in the ground-state 2H phase. Conversely, their effect is almost negligible on the thermodynamically competitive \1Tp\ phase. The ${\bm κ}$ difference between the 2H higher-conductivity phase and the \1Tp\ lower-conductivity phase can be exploited for the design of thermal switches that may find applications in thermal management and in the development of phonon-based logic elements, particularly in the case of tellurides, where field-controlled ultrafast and reversible phase transitions have already been demonstrated experimentally.

</details>


### [206] [High-Pressure Refractive Indices of NaCl, KCl, CaO, SrO, and MgO Reveal the Dependence of Anion Polarizability on Coordination Number and Bond Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17290)
*Konstantin Solovev,Xiangdong Li,Björn Winkler,Sergio Speziale,Sergey S. Lobanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究测量了 NaCl、KCl、CaO、SrO 在高压下 B1 与 B2 结构的折射率（refractive index），通过 Lorentz-Lorenz 分析得到应变极化率参数（strain polarizability parameter）Λ，确立配位数（coordination number）与阴阳离子间距（cation-anion distance）可作为阴离子极化率（anion polarizability）的可分离预测因子，并发现 $O^{2-}$ 与 $Cl^{-}$ 的迁移性（transferability）存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压下阴离子极化率的变化主导此类典型离子固体（ionic solid）的光学响应，但其对局域结构与阳离子化学（cation chemistry）的依赖在高压下尚缺乏实验约束。

Method: 测量 NaCl、KCl、CaO、SrO 在约 100、100、120、60 GPa 下 B1 与 B2 结构的折射率，结合已发表的 MgO-B1（至 140 GPa）折射率与 DFT 计算，采用 Lorentz-Lorenz 分析提取应变极化率参数 Λ。

Result: Λ 与压力无关，范围从 KCl-B1 的 0.34±0.05 到 MgO-B2 的 1.57±0.02。在固定阴阳离子间距下，阴离子配位数由六增至八使 $Cl^{-}$ 与 $O^{2-}$ 极化率分别降低约 0.34-0.40 $\mathring{A}^3$ 与 0.38-0.47 $\mathring{A}^3$。

Conclusion: 配位数与阴阳离子间距是 B1 和 B2 结构中阴离子极化率的可分离预测因子。$O^{2-}$ 极化率在固定间距下于 MgO-B1 与 CaO-B1 间迁移性可达约 0.06 $\mathring{A}^3$ 以内，并可能延伸至 SrO-B1 与 B2 氧化物；而 $Cl^{-}$ 极化率在 NaCl-B2 与 KCl-B2 间不可迁移，其差异随阴阳离子间距由 2.65 $\mathring{A}$ 处的约 0.2 $\mathring{A}^3$ 变化至约 2.5 $\mathring{A}$ 处的 0。B1-B2 转变中应变极化率参数的变化分别由 $O^{2-}$ 与 $Cl^{-}$ 极化率对致密化（densification）敏感度的降低与升高所主导。

Abstract: Several alkali chlorides and alkaline-earth oxides adopt the B1 structure at ambient conditions and transform to B2 under compression, allowing to change coordination number and cation-anion distance independently across chemically distinct hosts. The change in anion polarizability under high pressure dominates the optical response of these prototypical ionic solids, yet its dependence on local structure and cation chemistry remains experimentally unconstrained at high pressure. Here we report refractive indices of NaCl, KCl, CaO, and SrO to ~100, ~100, ~120, and ~60 GPa, each in both the B1 and B2 structures, complemented by published MgO-B1 indices to 140 GPa and DFT calculations. Lorentz-Lorenz analysis yields pressure-independent strain polarizability parameters $Λ$ ranging from 0.34$\pm$0.05 for KCl-B1 to 1.57$\pm$0.02 for MgO-B2. Our results establish coordination number and cation-anion distance as separable predictors of anion polarizability in both B1 and B2 structures. Increasing anion coordination from six to eight at fixed cation-anion distance lowers the polarizability of both $Cl^{-}$ and $O^{2-}$ by ~0.34-0.40 $\mathring{A}^3$ and ~0.38-0.47 $\mathring{A}^3$ respectively. Our results suggest that the polarizability of $O^{2-}$ is transferable to within ~0.06 $\mathring{A}^3$ between MgO-B1 and CaO-B1 at fixed cation-anion distance, and that this transferability possibly extends to SrO-B1 and the B2 oxides. In contrast, the polarizability of $Cl^{-}$ is not transferable between NaCl-B2 and KCl-B2: the difference between them varies with cation-anion distance, from ~0.2 $\mathring{A}^3$ at 2.65 $\mathring{A}$ to 0 at ~2.5 $\mathring{A}$. The results show that the change of the strain polarizability parameters across the B1-B2 transition is governed by the decreasing and increasing sensitivities of $O^{2-}$ and $Cl^{-}$ polarizabilities to densification, respectively.

</details>


### [207] [Bridging powder and multi-crystal diffraction with basis-adaptive texture tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17299)
*Martin Sæbye Carøe,Mads Allerup Carlsen,Felix Tristan Frankus,Adam André William Cretton,Michela La Bella,Innokentiy Kantor,Mads Ry Vogel Jørgensen,Henning Friis Poulsen,Jakob Sauer Jørgensen,Nils Axel Henningsson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出“基自适应织构层析（basis-adaptive texture tomography）”，用峰索引得到的候选取向替代均匀取向网格，从而在多晶材料“斑驳衍射环”这一中间尺度区间实现更高角分辨率的取向分布重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间分辨X射线衍射（spatially resolved X-ray diffraction）中，细晶弱织构产生平滑德拜-谢乐环（Debye-Scherrer rings），粗晶弱变形产生可逐晶索引的孤立斑点，而塑性变形金属、马氏体与铁弹材料、强织构地质集合体等常落在两者之间，形成峰宽化并重叠的斑驳衍射环。传统织构层析（texture tomography）角分辨率不足，逐晶索引则易引入边界伪影并低估晶内取向差（intragranular misorientation）。

Method: 以峰索引（peak indexing）获得的候选取向替代均匀取向网格，构成基自适应织构层析；沿用织构层析的思路，在每个体素（voxel）内重建完整取向分布函数（orientation distribution function），使体素可保留多个晶粒、亚晶或畴的贡献，而非强制赋予单一取向。

Result: 在模拟铝多晶（aluminum polycrystals）中，相比均匀基织构层析与逐点扫描3DXRD，该方法对晶粒与亚晶边界的刻画更清晰，且晶内取向误差更低；在拉伸变形铝（tensile-deformed aluminum）实验中，可成像出取向展布达数度的块体晶粒与亚晶结构。

Conclusion: 基自适应织构层析在传统织构层析与逐晶索引之间的观测区间内，以更高角分辨率重建取向分布，同时避免体素被强制单一取向，适用于弱变形、复杂孪晶微结构及强织构多晶材料的结构表征。

Abstract: In spatially resolved X-ray diffraction experiments using narrow beams, diffraction patterns from polycrystalline materials often fall between two well-served regimes.
