Fine-grained, weakly textured microstructures produce smooth Debye-Scherrer rings suited to powder- and tensor-tomography methods, whereas coarse, weakly deformed grains produce isolated spots that can be indexed grain by grain. Many important polycrystalline materials, including plastically deformed metals, martensitic and ferroelastic materials containing complex twin microstructures, and geological aggregates with strong texture or heterogeneous grain size, produce spotty diffraction rings with broadened and overlapping peaks between these limits. Texture tomography addresses this regime by reconstructing spatial orientation distributions from diffraction data. Here, conventional texture tomography lacks the angular resolution needed for sharp distributions, while grain-by-grain indexing can introduce boundary artifacts and underestimate intragranular misorientation. This work introduces basis-adaptive texture tomography. Candidate orientations obtained from peak indexing replace the uniform orientation grid, achieving higher angular resolution. Like conventional texture tomography, it benefits from reconstructing a full orientation distribution function in each voxel, allowing voxels to retain contributions from multiple grains, subgrains or domains rather than being forced into a single orientation. Simulated aluminum polycrystals show improved delineation of grain and sub-grain boundaries accompanied by lower intragranular orientation errors compared with uniform-basis texture tomography and point-by-point scanning 3DXRD. An experimental demonstration on tensile-deformed aluminum shows that bulk grain and subgrain structures with orientation spreads of several degrees can be mapped.

</details>


### [208] [Multiproperty Atomistic Characterization of a Synthesized Pyrazinic Nitrogen-Doped Porous Armchair Graphene Nanoribbon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17305)
*Cicera M. V. de Araújo,Isaac de M. Félix,Willian F. Radel,Raphael B. de Oliveira,Guilherme da S. L. Fabris,Douglas S. Galvão,Marcelo L. Pereira Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作对经表面合成（on-surface synthesis）获得的九原子宽、周期穿孔且孔缘含氮的扶手椅型石墨烯纳米带（graphene nanoribbon, GNR）进行了器件相关性质的系统表征，区分了"穿孔"与"氮取代"两类结构修饰的独立作用，并评估了基底吸附与氮构型对磁性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯纳米带兼具宽度可控带隙与原子级精度，其边缘与内部化学修饰是设计电子与光学行为的实用途径；然而穿孔与氮掺杂各自对力学、热学、电子与光学性质的贡献尚未被厘清，需加以解耦。

Method: 采用含吩嗪（phenazine）前驱体的 Ullmann 偶联（Ullmann coupling）制备九原子宽、周期穿孔并在每个孔缘带有两个二配位氮原子的扶手椅型纳米带，并与原始及未掺杂多孔纳米带对照，系统表征其力学、热学、电子与光学性质；同时计算 Au(111)、Ag(111)、Cu(111) 上的吸附能与吸附性质，并比较石墨型与吡嗪型氮构型。

Result: 穿孔主导力学与热学响应：移除承载前沿态的原子行使带隙打开至隧穿谱可测范围，同时显著降低刚度与热导率。氮取代基本不改变力学响应，仅通过声子谱重新加权带来次要的电导率下降；其主导效应在电子方面——选择性窄化导带、将最低激子束缚 414 meV，并使吸收边红移。所得有效声子平均自由程为 9.9 nm，与当前合成所能达到的长度相当，使样品处于弹道—扩散（ballistic-to-diffusive）过渡区。三种金属基底吸附能几乎不可区分且保持范德华（van der Waals）性质，解释了实验观察到的基底容忍性。石墨型而非吡嗪型氮会使纳米带呈金属性，仅当两个原子占据孔缘位点时给出铁磁半导体态，磁矩为每原胞 2 μB。

Conclusion: 穿孔与氮取代在功能上分工明确：前者支配力学与热学行为，后者支配电子与光学行为。基底吸附的弱差异性赋予该纳米带合成与器件集成的基底容忍度，而孔缘氮位点是获得铁磁半导体态的关键条件。

Abstract: Graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) obtained by on-surface synthesis combine a width-controlled band gap with atomic precision, making chemical decoration of their edges and interiors a practical route to designed electronic and optical behavior. Ullmann coupling of a phenazine-bearing precursor yields a nine-atom-wide armchair ribbon that is periodically perforated and carries two two-coordinated nitrogen atoms at every pore rim. Here we characterize this ribbon across the properties relevant to device operation, alongside the pristine and undoped porous ribbon, disentangling the roles of pore formation and nitrogen incorporation. Perforation primarily governs mechanical and thermal response, whereas nitrogen substitution predominantly controls electronic and optical properties. Perforation removes the atomic row carrying frontier states, opening the gap into the range measured by tunneling spectroscopy while substantially reducing stiffness and thermal conductivity. Substitution leaves the mechanical response essentially unchanged and contributes a secondary conductivity reduction through phonon spectrum reweighting. Its primary effect is electronic: it selectively narrows the conduction band, binds the lowest exciton by 414 meV, and displaces the absorption edge into the red. The effective phonon mean free path we obtain, 9.9 nm, matches the length to which the synthesis presently limits these ribbons, placing reported samples at the ballistic-to-diffusive crossover. Adsorption on Au(111), Ag(111), and Cu(111) is energetically nearly indistinguishable and remains van der Waals in character, accounting for the substrate tolerance observed experimentally. Finally, graphitic rather than pyrazinic nitrogen would make the ribbon metallic, except when two atoms occupy pore-rim sites, yielding a ferromagnetic semiconducting state having a magnetic moment of 2 mu_B per unit cell.

</details>


### [209] [Integrated quantum-secured cryptographic memory in customizable single nanodiamonds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17329)
*Tongtong Zhang,Jiaqi Li,Zhiqin Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于单个纳米金刚石（nanodiamond, ND）的一体化本征安全架构，将高维数据编码与多尺度物理加密不可分割地融合，实现确定性存储与物理不可克隆特性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进物理克隆（physical cloning）与网络提取技术扩散，使硬件级信息安全面临挑战；传统架构中确定性存储（deterministic memory）与密码硬件物理分离，导致敏感数据易被截获。物理不可克隆函数（physical unclonable functions, PUFs）虽可认证，但难以在与高密度写入统一的纳米介质中集成。

Method: 通过精确聚焦电子束辐照（focused-electron-beam irradiation）实现单纳米颗粒缺陷工程，在单个纳米金刚石内进行确定性三十六进制（base-36）数据写入；并将该定制存储层永久锁定于介质固有随机性中，结合宏观空间 PUF 与经光学检测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance, ODMR）提取的原子尺度量子指纹。

Result: 实现了单颗粒尺度上的确定性数据写入与不可克隆物理加密的融合；存储层与固有随机性永久绑定，形成宏观空间 PUF 与严格不可复现的原子级量子指纹相结合的安全结构。

Conclusion: 将确定性存储无缝嵌入不可克隆物理环境，可从本质上消除分离式硬件的脆弱性，为防篡改存储芯片、零信任网络（zero-trust networks）与新一代数据保险库提供无条件安全的固态基础。

Abstract: As advanced physical cloning and cyber-extraction techniques proliferate, achieving absolute hardware-level information security has become a paramount global challenge. Conventional architectures systems are fundamentally vulnerable because deterministic memory and cryptographic hardware are physically decoupled, exposing sensitive data to interception. While physical unclonable functions (PUFs) offer robust authentication, integrating them with high-density data writing within a unified nanoscale medium remains elusive. Here, we present an intrinsically secure all-in-one architecture that inextricably fuses high-dimensional data encoding with multiscale physical encryption within single nanodiamonds (NDs). Through precise focused-electron-beam irradiation, we achieve single-nanoparticle defect engineering, enabling deterministic base-36 data writing within individual NDs. Crucially, this customized memory layer is permanently locked within the medium's inherent stochasticity, combining macroscopic spatial PUFs with strictly irreproducible atomic-scale quantum fingerprints extracted via optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR). By seamlessly embedding deterministic memory into an unclonable physical environment, our approach intrinsically eliminates the vulnerabilities of separated hardware, providing an unconditionally secure solid-state foundation for tamper-proof memory chips, zero-trust networks and next-generation data vaults.

</details>


### [210] [Nonlinear electron-phonon interactions from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17433)
*Zhenbang Dai,Feliciano Giustino*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种可在真实材料中计算任意阶非线性电子-声子相互作用（nonlinear electron-phonon interactions）的理论与计算方法，并首次在金刚石、氟化锂和石墨中验证二阶耦合，同时将其推广至从头算极化子方程（ab initio polaron equations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子相互作用研究长期集中于线性一阶耦合（linear, first-order couplings），二阶及更高阶非线性耦合因计算代价过高、且缺乏能同时给出对角与非对角耦合矩阵元的计算框架而被普遍忽略。此空白限制了相关现象的定量准确性。

Method: 方法结合了电子波函数的原胞计算（unit-cell calculations）与声子微扰的超胞计算（supercell calculations）的优势；具有可系统性改进的能力，可兼容半局域或非局域交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functionals），并适用于万尼尔-傅里叶插值（Wannier-Fourier interpolation）。

Result: 作为概念验证，在金刚石、氟化锂和石墨中计算了二阶电子-声子耦合矩阵元（second-order electron-phonon coupling matrix elements），三者分别代表非极性半导体、极性半导体和金属；并将从头算极化子方程推广至二阶耦合，表明二阶耦合对获得极化子形成能（polaron formation energies）与跳跃势垒（hopping barriers）的定量精度不可或缺。

Conclusion: 该方法论可应用于当前在线性电子-声子耦合近似（linear electron-phonon coupling approximation）框架下研究的所有性质与现象，从声子介导超导（phonon-mediated superconductivity）到激发态动力学（excited-states dynamics），并适用于谐性与非谐性体系。

Abstract: Electron-phonon interactions underpin a variety of phenomena, ranging from transport and superconductivity to polarons and ultrafast carrier dynamics. Despite being one of the most intensely studied subjects in condensed matter physics, research on electron-phonon physics mostly focused on linear, first-order couplings. Second- and higher-order nonlinear couplings are commonly ignored because their calculations are too demanding and we lack computational frameworks that can provide both diagonal and off-diagonal coupling matrix elements. In this work, we report a theory and computational method for computing nonlinear electron-phonon interactions of any order in real materials. Our approach combines the advantages of unit-cell calculations of electron wavefunctions and supercell calculations of phonon perturbations, is systematically improvable, can be used with either semilocal or nonlocal exchange-correlation functionals, and is amenable to Wannier-Fourier interpolation. As a first proof of concept, we illustrate this method by computing second-order electron-phonon coupling matrix elements in diamond, lithium fluoride, and graphite as representative nonpolar semiconductors, polar semiconductors, and metals, respectively. Furthermore, we generalize the ab initio polaron equations to second-order electron-phonon couplings, and we show that second-order couplings are essential to achieve quantitative accuracy in polaron formation energies and hopping barriers. The present methodology will find application in the study of all properties and phenomena that are currently being investigated within the linear electron-phonon coupling approximation, from phonon-mediated superconductivity to excited-states dynamics, both in harmonic and anharmonic systems.

</details>


### [211] [Designing Homogeneous Ti-Nb-Fe-Sn $β$ Titanium Alloys by PBF-LB: A Pre-Alloyed Powder Blend Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17476)
*João Felipe Queiroz Rodrigues,Kristína Bartha,Mariano Casas-Luna,Gilberto Vicente Prandi,Márcio Sangali,Kateřina Ficková,Jiří Kozlík,Michaela Šlapáková,Martin Koller,Adam Strnad,Josef Stráský,Miloš Janeček,Rubens Caram*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过激光粉末床熔融（PBF-LB）结合母合金粉末共混、700 μm 大层厚重熔与热处理，制备了组织均匀的单相 β 型 Ti-Nb-Fe-Sn 合金，并揭示微量成分变化对晶粒长大与力学性能的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚稳 β 钛合金（metastable β titanium alloys）因低弹性模量、高比强度与优异耐蚀性而受关注；PBF-LB 可成形复杂形状并借助粉末共混调控成分，但直接使用元素 Ti-Nb 混合粉易导致富 Nb 颗粒未完全溶解的化学不均匀与非平衡相形成。

Method: 采用 Ti-42Nb、Ti-20Nb-15Fe、Ti-20Nb-20Sn 母合金粉末（master-alloy powders）与工业纯 Ti 共混，以不常见的大层厚 70 μm 配合层间重熔（layer remelting）制备 Ti-23Nb-3Fe-4Sn、Ti-26Nb-2Fe-4Sn、Ti-29Nb-1Fe-4Sn 与 Ti-32Nb-4Sn，随后经 1000 °C/2 h 热处理并水淬。

Result: 热处理后所有合金孔隙率低、化学成分分布均匀，呈单 β 相组织，晶粒以等轴为主且织构弱。热力学计算表明凝固描述符无法解释非单调的晶粒尺寸演变，其归因于继承的凝固组织、瞬态 TiFe 类相形成、Nb/Sn 分配及/或溶质拖曳控制的 β 晶粒长大。硬度与屈服强度随 Fe 减少、Nb 增加而下降（268→224 HV，691→468 MPa），共振超声谱测得杨氏模量为 63-81 GPa。

Conclusion: 预合金母合金共混结合重熔与热处理，是制备化学均匀 Ti-Nb-Fe-Sn β 钛合金的有效途径，同时揭示了微小成分变化如何主导晶粒长大行为与力学响应。

Abstract: Metastable $β$ titanium alloys are attractive for biomedical and structural applications owing to their low elastic modulus, high specific strength, and excellent corrosion resistance. Laser powder bed fusion (PBF-LB) enables complex-shape production and controlled compositional variation through powder blending. However, processing elemental Ti-Nb blends often results in chemical heterogeneity from incomplete dissolution of Nb-rich particles and non-equilibrium phase formation. To address this, low-modulus Ti-Nb-Fe-Sn alloys were produced by PBF-LB using Ti-42Nb, Ti-20Nb-15Fe, and Ti-20Nb-20Sn master-alloy powders blended with commercially pure Ti. Ti-23Nb-3Fe-4Sn, Ti-26Nb-2Fe-4Sn, Ti-29Nb-1Fe-4Sn, and Ti-32Nb-4Sn were fabricated using an uncommonly large 70 $μ$m layer thickness with layer remelting, followed by heat treatment at 1000 $°$C for 2 h and water quenching. After heat treatment, all alloys exhibited low porosity, homogeneous chemical distribution, and single $β$-phase microstructures with predominantly equiaxed grains and weak crystallographic texture. Thermodynamic calculations indicated that solidification descriptors alone could not explain the non-monotonic grain-size evolution, which was attributed to inherited solidification structure, transient TiFe-like phase formation, Nb/Sn partitioning, and/or solute-drag-controlled $β$-grain growth. Hardness and yield strength decreased with decreasing Fe and increasing Nb contents, from 268 to 224 HV and 691 to 468 MPa, respectively. Young's modulus, determined by resonant ultrasound spectroscopy, ranged 63-81 GPa. These results demonstrate that pre-alloyed master-alloy blends combined with remelting and heat treatment provide an effective route for producing chemically homogeneous Ti-Nb-Fe-Sn $β$ alloys while revealing how small compositional changes govern grain-growth behavior and mechanical response.

</details>


### [212] [Enhanced thermal stability of SiGeSn by suppressing surface-mediated degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17504)
*Anis Attiaoui,Sebastien Koelling,Lu Luo,Simone Assali,Oussama Moutanabbir*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在550 °C等温退火下，超薄氧化物帽层通过抑制表面Sn交换动力学，显著延缓Si0.08Ge0.83Sn0.04合金的热降解，并将接触电阻率降低25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiGeSn合金是硅兼容红外光子学的有前景材料，但其亚稳特性限制了后生长器件加工的热预算，且其热降解机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用原位光谱椭偏（in situ spectroscopic ellipsometry, SE）在550 °C等温退火过程中监测Si0.08Ge0.83Sn0.04合金的热稳定性，并对比有无超薄氧化物帽层的情形。

Result: 无帽层薄膜在50分钟后发生相分离，伴随空洞形成、厚度减少60%以及E2临界点（critical point, CP）跃迁400 meV蓝移，归因于替代位Sn经表面偏析而耗尽；氧化物帽层薄膜同期成分变化小于1 at.% Sn、光学位移小于20 meV，且空洞形成、应变弛豫与合金分解均受抑制。

Conclusion: 表面Sn输运是SiGeSn的主要降解路径；超薄氧化物帽层可延长亚稳IV族合金的热稳定性，为集成至先进硅光子与电子平台提供实用途径。

Abstract: $\text{SiGeSn}$ alloys are promising silicon-compatible semiconductors for monolithic infrared photonics. However, their metastable nature limits the thermal budgets available for post-growth device processing, and the mechanisms governing their thermal degradation remain unresolved. Here, we investigate the thermal stability of $\text{Si}_{0.08}\text{Ge}_{0.83}\text{Sn}_{0.04}$ alloys using in situ spectroscopic ellipsometry (SE) during isothermal annealing at 550 °C. We show that adding an ultrathin oxide cap kinetically suppresses Sn exchange with the free surface while leaving bulk diffusion pathways largely unaffected. Uncapped films undergo phase separation after 50 min, accompanied by void formation, a 60% thickness reduction, and a 400 meV blueshift of the $E_{2}$ critical point (CP) transition, consistent with substitutional Sn depletion from the probed volume through surface segregation. In contrast, oxide-capped films exhibit a small compositional change (<1 at.% Sn) and optical shift (<20 meV) over the same period, with suppressed void formation, strain relaxation, and alloy decomposition. This surface-kinetic control additionally yields a 25-fold reduction in contact resistivity relative to annealed uncapped alloys. These results identify surface Sn transport as the dominant degradation pathway in SiGeSn and demonstrate that an ultrathin oxide cap extends the thermal stability of metastable group-IV alloys, providing a practical route toward their integration into advanced silicon photonic and electronic platforms.

</details>


### [213] [The chemistry of embrittlement: How hydrogen dwindles cohesion in iron during fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17508)
*Aleksei Egorov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作研究氢（hydrogen）渗入铁（iron）基体后导致严重脆化（embrittlement）的机理，聚焦氢对断裂过程中铁键合强度（bond strength）的影响，并揭示静电排斥（electrostatic repulsion）在降低断裂面间内聚力（cohesion）中的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢（steel）通常强韧，但氢渗入后会发生严重脆化，而对其内在原因的认识尚不充分，因此需要阐明氢致脆化的微观机理。

Method: 该研究考察氢在断裂过程中如何影响铁的键合强度，并分析氢降低断裂面之间内聚力的方式。

Result: 研究发现静电排斥在氢降低断裂面内聚力过程中发挥核心作用。

Conclusion: 氢致铁脆化可归因于其对键合强度的削弱，而断裂面间内聚力的下降由静电排斥主导。

Abstract: Normally strong and tough, steel$\unicode{x2014}$with iron as its base$\unicode{x2014}$falls prey to severe embrittlement when infiltrated by hydrogen; the insights into why are not well understood. This work explores how hydrogen affects bond strength in iron during fracture and why it reduces cohesion between fracture surfaces, laying bare the central role of electrostatic repulsion.

</details>

### [214] [Stratified jet with wall effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16418)
*Hai Duc Vu,Kengo Deguchi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究以夹于两壁之间、位于均匀分层流体中的平行 Bickley 射流（Bickley jet）为典型模型，探讨分层流中不稳定性与相干结构如何与物理边界相互作用。结果表明，短波开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定（Kelvin-Helmholtz instability）产生非对称平均流且远离射流不衰减，长波相干结构可发射内重力波（internal gravity waves）并显著削弱射流中心线速度；辐射发生时，不稳定性的持续性与壁面边界条件密切相关，且存在一种不依赖壁面、可超越 Miles-Howard 判据的黏性模态（viscous mode）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层流中的射流在地球物理中普遍存在。核心问题是：流动所产生的不稳定性与相干结构是否会与物理边界发生相互作用，以及以何种方式相互作用。为此需要一个简单而典型的模型流动来厘清机制。

Method: 采用一个典型模型流动：将平行 Bickley 射流置于两壁之间，流体为均匀分层。分析短波开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性的平均流效应，并考察可发射内重力波的长波相干结构，结合壁面边界条件与雷诺数（Reynolds number）、理查森数（Richardson number）进行稳定性分析。

Result: 短波开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性可在平均流中产生非对称性；尽管脉动在远离射流处快速衰减，非对称平均流却不衰减。长波相干结构能够发射内重力波，这些波向壁面缓慢衰减，或在某些情形下从射流向外辐射；波输送动量并扭曲平均流，使射流中心线速度显著降低。当发生辐射时，不稳定性在高雷诺数下能否持续强烈依赖壁面所施加的边界条件。此外，发现一种不稳定及相应非线性相干结构可在理查森数高于 Miles-Howard 判据时持续存在，该黏性模态的存在不依赖壁面。

Conclusion: 在均匀分层流体中，射流的不稳定性与相干结构可与边界发生显著相互作用：平均流非对称性可长距离存续，内重力波辐射能显著改变平均流与射流强度，且高雷诺数下的稳定性受壁面边界条件支配。同时，存在不依赖壁面并超越 Miles-Howard 判据的黏性不稳定模态，扩展了对分层剪切流稳定性的认识。

Abstract: Jets in stratified flows are ubiquitous in geophysics. A fundamental question is whether the instabilities and coherent structures generated by the flow interact with the physical boundaries and, if so, how. To this end, a simple canonical model flow is employed: a parallel Bickley jet confined between walls in a uniformly stratified fluid. We first show that short wavelength Kelvin-Helmholtz instability can generate asymmetry in the mean flow. Although the fluctuations decay rapidly away from the jet, the asymmetric mean flow does not. We then examine long-wavelength coherent structures that can emit internal gravity waves, which decay slowly towards the walls or, in some cases, radiate away from the jet. These waves transport momentum and distort the mean flow, reducing the jet centreline velocity significantly. When radiation occurs, whether the instability persists at high Reynolds numbers depends strongly on the boundary conditions imposed at the walls. Moreover, we found an instability and associated nonlinear coherent structures that persist at Richardson numbers above the Miles-Howard criterion. The existence of this viscous mode does not rely on the presence of the walls.

</details>


### [215] [Extensible membranes in inviscid flow: aerodynamics and singular limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16484)
*Yu Jun Loo,Silas Alben*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文建立二维无粘（inviscid）可伸长膜模型，膜两侧边缘连续脱落涡，并耦合弱谱Galerkin离散，实现零弯曲刚度（bending rigidity）下的直接计算。研究揭示了柔顺性对升力与涡脱落时序的影响、自由端张力消失导致的欠定问题与涡致拍打机制，以及周期性颤振向混沌转捩的动力学路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建可在零弯曲刚度下直接求解的膜-涡相互作用模型，以阐明膜在集中流体载荷下的响应机制，并处理自由端张力消失所带来的数学与物理退化问题。

Method: 发展二维无粘可伸长膜模型，允许两侧边缘连续涡脱落；将压力跳跃（pressure jump）视为束缚涡量（bound vorticity）的通量；膜方程采用弱谱Galerkin（spectral Galerkin）离散以获得零弯曲刚度的直接计算能力。

Result: 固定-固定膜中，柔顺性使膜体趋近前缘涡、延迟其脱落并在启动阶段增强升力；周期性脱落形成后，柔顺性主要放大力的振荡而非平均升力。涡脱落可使重膜卸载进入压缩，形成弯曲正则化（bending-regularized）的皱褶。当R_2>0时，只要膜保持浸入（immersion），膜方程在张力符号变化下仍适定；释放后缘则引入内在退化，自由端点张力为零使R_2=0问题形式上欠定，而弱形式选取其有界能量分支。消失张力端点俘获压力扰动并驱动快速尖端拍打；主频与有效波数趋于零刚度极限，并带有由奇异Bessel分支产生的反对数修正。降低拉伸模量使周期性颤振失稳，反复的流体驱动离开与弹性回复暗示存在同宿缠绕（homoclinic tangle）对应周期到混沌颤振的转捩。

Conclusion: 柔顺膜在启动阶段通过延迟前缘涡脱落增强升力，但在周期脱落阶段主要放大振荡而非提高平均升力；自由后缘的零张力退化需以弱形式在能量意义下选择解，并主导拍打与频率标度行为；流体驱动的非线性回归结构指示同宿缠绕是周期颤振向混沌转捩的机制。

Abstract: We develop a two-dimensional inviscid model for extensible membranes with continuous vortex shedding from both edges, coupled to a weak spectral Galerkin discretization of the membrane equation that permits direct computation at zero bending rigidity. Viewing the pressure jump as a flux for bound vorticity explains how membranes respond to concentrated fluid loading. For fixed-fixed membranes, compliance draws the body toward the leading-edge vortex, delaying its detachment and enhancing lift primarily during start-up; after periodic shedding develops, compliance mainly amplifies the force oscillations rather than mean lift. Vortex detachment can unload heavy membranes into compression, producing bending-regularized wrinkles. For $R_2>0$, the membrane equation remains well-posed independently of the sign of the tension, provided the membrane remains an immersion. Releasing the trailing edge creates an intrinsic degeneracy: the tension vanishes at the free endpoint, making the $R_2=0$ problem formally underdetermined, while the weak formulation selects its bounded-energy branch. This vanishing-tension endpoint traps pressure disturbances and drives rapid tip-snapping; the dominant frequency and effective wave number approach their zero-rigidity limits with an inverse-logarithmic correction arising from a singular Bessel branch. Finally, decreasing the stretching modulus destabilizes periodic flutter, and the repeated fluid-driven departure and elastic return suggest a homoclinic tangle underlying the transition from periodic to chaotic flutter.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.MN'></div>

# q-bio.MN [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Local energetic coupling enhances the expressivity of chemical computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17339)
*Marco Tuccio,Jason W. Rocks,Joshua E. Goldford*

Main category: q-bio.MN

TL;DR: 该研究通过逆向设计热力学一致的化学反应网络（chemical reaction networks），揭示了控制化学系统计算表达力的物理化学特征。核心发现是：非平衡驱动（nonequilibrium drive）是决定稳态计算表达力最有效的单一资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命系统通过化学计算将环境信号映射为内部化学状态，但尽管分子编程（molecular programming）已有进展，究竟是哪些物理化学特征控制化学系统的计算表达力（computational expressivity）仍不清楚。该研究旨在厘清这一问题。

Method: 采用逆向设计（inverse design）方法构建热力学一致的化学反应网络，使其对环境输入的稳态响应能够计算目标非线性函数。利用隐式微分（implicit differentiation）直接训练自由能景观（free-energy landscape），包括标准化学势、过渡态能量与热力学驱动力（thermodynamic drives）。

Result: 由基本连接与裂解步骤生成的大型网络能够拟合日益复杂的非单调多项式函数，表达力随网络规模呈对数增长，且主要由反应数量预测。对各类能量参数分别训练显示，能够打破细致平衡（detailed balance）的内部热力学驱动力主导可训练性，仅组合参数类别对可达到相当性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果确认非平衡驱动是化学反应网络稳态计算表达力最有效的单一资源，为理解化学系统的计算能力提供了热力学层面的定量依据。

Abstract: Living systems compute with chemistry by mapping environmental signals onto specific internal chemical states. Despite recent advances in molecular programming, it remains unclear which physicochemical features control the computational expressivity of chemical systems. Here we inverse-design thermodynamically consistent chemical reaction networks whose steady-state response to an environmental input computes a target nonlinear function. Using implicit differentiation we train the free-energy landscape directly: standard chemical potentials, transition-state energies and thermodynamic drives. Increasingly large networks generated by elementary ligation and cleavage steps fit increasingly complex nonmonotonic polynomial functions, with expressivity scaling logarithmically with network size, predicted primarily by the number of reactions. Training individual energetic parameter classes reveals that internal thermodynamic drives, capable of breaking detailed balance, dominate trainability, with comparable performances achieved only by pairs of parameter classes. These results identify nonequilibrium drive as the most effective single resource for steady-state computational expressivity in chemical reaction networks.

</details>
